tool_result Read
```
1	# eagle
2	
3	EAGLE-3 draft-model workspace for MiniCPM-SALA. The directory is organized by
4	responsibility, not by accidental historical version names.
5	
6	## Current Layout
7	
8	| Path | Purpose |
9	|---|---|
10	| `bin/` | Human entrypoints for build, collect, train |
11	| `pipelines/target_regen/` | Target-regenerated prompt/data collection |
12	| `pipelines/target_regen/v3mix/` | 300K prompt mix + NVFP4 BOS collection path |
13	| `training/sala_draft/` | AOI + LK + response-mask EAGLE trainer and converter |
14	| `prompts/full_shard/` | Preserved full-shard prompts |
15	| `prompts/target_regen/` | Start prompts for target-regenerated collection |
16	| `data/` | Active generated data and vocab caches |
17	| `weights/` | Active training checkpoints |
18	| `models/det_prefill/` | Preserved det-target prefill baseline draft |
19	| `legacy/v2_v3/` | v2/v3 code, probes, models, weights |
20	| `legacy/v4/` | v4 AOI/LK baseline code, pipeline, model, weights, validation |
21	
22	## Preserved Baselines
23	
24	| Name | Path | Status |
25	|---|---|---|
26	| det-prefill | `models/det_prefill/` | Current preserved production-quality baseline |
27	| v4 AOI/LK | `legacy/v4/model/aoi_lk/` | Historical baseline |
28	| v2 | `legacy/v2_v3/models/v2/` | Historical rollback baseline |
29	| v3 | `legacy/v2_v3/models/v3/` | Rejected long-context baseline |
30	
31	`det_prefill` is kept because it is a validated baseline. The name describes
32	what it is: det-target data collected through the prefill-style pipeline.
33	
34	## Target-Regenerated Pipeline
35	
36	Current large-scale path is v3mix:
37	
38	```bash
39	bash eagle/bin/build_v3mix_300k_prompts.sh
40	
41	bash eagle/bin/start_v3mix_collect_server.sh
42	
43	bash eagle/bin/collect_v3mix_nvfp4_bos.sh
44	```
45	
46	The v3mix server hook writes direct NVFP4 aux-hidden samples; the collector
47	validates those files, uploads sealed segments to BOS asynchronously, and
48	removes uploaded local segment directories.
49	Defaults are set for the current 300K run: 2048 saved rows, 64 running requests
50	with a rolling 512-request window, 64-file segments, `bcecmd` upload, sha256
51	off, progress bars on, and B12X off for collection.
52	Details and the latest 256-sample smoke metrics are in
53	`pipelines/target_regen/v3mix/README.md`.
54	
55	Legacy 10K path:
56	
57	Build start prompts:
58	
59	```bash
60	bash eagle/bin/build_target_regen_prompts.sh
61	```
62	
63	Start the collection server as a long-running task:
64	
65	```bash
66	EAGLE_TARGET_REGEN_HOOK_DIR=/tmp/eagle_target_regen \
67	EAGLE3_TOP_K=128 \
68	MEM_FRACTION_STATIC=0.55 \
69	MAX_RUNNING=24 \
70	PORT=30000 \
71	bash eagle/pipelines/target_regen/start_server.sh
72	```
73	
74	Collect generated training data:
75	
76	```bash
77	bash eagle/bin/collect_target_regen.sh
78	```
79	
80	Default paths:
81	
82	| Artifact | Path |
83	|---|---|
84	| start prompts | `prompts/target_regen/v2mix_10k.jsonl` |
85	| train data | `data/target_regen/v2mix_10k/` |
86	| hook scratch | `/tmp/eagle_target_regen` |
87	
88	The training sequence length is 4096 because the collector stores
89	`prompt_last + generated[:4095]`. The prompt itself is target-model context,
90	not part of the draft training window except for the bridge token.
91	
92	## Training
93	
94	```bash
95	bash eagle/bin/train_target_regen.sh
96	```
97	
98	Direct trainer:
99	
100	```bash
101	python3 eagle/training/sala_draft/train.py \
102	  --steps 3000 \
103	  --eval_every 250 \
104	  --data_dir eagle/data/target_regen/v2mix_10k \
105	  --val_ind_dir eagle/data/target_regen_val/ind_200 \
106	  --val_ood_dir eagle/data/target_regen_val/ood_bench64 \
107	  --vocab_cache eagle/data/vocab_cache_det_prefill.pt \
108	  --output_dir eagle/weights/target_regen
109	```
110	
111	Convert a checkpoint:
112	
113	```bash
114	python3 eagle/training/sala_draft/convert_to_sglang.py \
115	  --ckpt eagle/weights/target_regen/best.pt \
116	  --vocab_cache eagle/data/vocab_cache_det_prefill.pt \
117	  --output_dir eagle/models/target_regen \
118	  --target_model /user_4813494d/models/openbmb/MiniCPM-SALA-gptq46-calib90-90k-NVFP4-det
119	```
120	
121	## Rules
122	
123	- Do not put new active work under `legacy/`.
124	- Do not use full-shard prompts as target-regenerated generation starts.
125	- Do not delete `models/det_prefill/` without an explicit replacement decision.
126	- Stop SGLang only with `bash bench/kill_sglang.sh`.
127
```

> TOOL

tool_result Read
```
1	# EAGLE v3mix Target-Regenerated Data
2	
3	This directory owns the v3mix prompt manifest and NVFP4 collection path.
4	
5	- `build_manifest.py` builds prompt-only JSONL manifests from local/HF/ModelScope sources.
6	- `collect_nvfp4_bos.py` sends prompts to a no-spec target server, waits for the
7	  `EAGLE3_ONESTAGE_DIR` hook output, stores `aux_hidden` as NVFP4, and optionally
8	  streams completed segments to BOS.
9	
10	## Layout
11	
12	```text
13	eagle/pipelines/target_regen/v3mix/
14	├── build_manifest.py       # source mix -> prompt manifest
15	├── collect_nvfp4_bos.py    # collect, NVFP4 compress, segment upload
16	└── README.md
17	```
18	
19	Thin runnable entrypoints live in `eagle/bin/`:
20	
21	- `build_v3mix_300k_prompts.sh`
22	- `collect_v3mix_nvfp4_bos.sh`
23	- `smoke_v3mix_nvfp4.sh`
24	- `start_v3mix_collect_server.sh`
25	
26	## Current 300K Prompt Mix
27	
28	`eagle/prompts/target_regen/v3mix_300k.jsonl` contains 300000 prompts.
29	
30	| Mix group | Count |
31	|---|---:|
32	| `chinese_reasoning_math_stem` | 95000 |
33	| `real_user_multiturn` | 85000 |
34	| `code` | 45000 |
35	| `general_reservoir_edge` | 40000 |
36	| `long_context_writing` | 35000 |
37	
38	Manifest validation from the build run: `bad=0`, `dup_sid=0`, `dup_prompt=0`.
39	
40	## Collection Contract
41	
42	Start a no-spec target server:
43	
44	```bash
45	bash eagle/bin/start_v3mix_collect_server.sh
46	```
47	
48	Collect and stream segments:
49	
50	```bash
51	bash eagle/bin/collect_v3mix_nvfp4_bos.sh
52	```
53	
54	The wrapper uses a stable semantic BOS prefix by default:
55	`bos://anp3-common-model/vista/eagle3_data/v3mix_300k_det_len2048_topk256_aux1-10-22`.
56	Override it only when intentionally starting a separate run.
57	
58	Default collection settings:
59	
60	| Setting | Default |
61	|---|---:|
62	| running requests / request window | 64 / 512 |
63	| segment size | 64 files |
64	| `generate_tokens` | 2047 |
65	| saved rows | 2048 |
66	| `full_ignore_eos_ratio` | 0.01 |
67	| upload method | `bcecmd` |
68	| sha256 manifest | off |
69	| progress bars | on |
70	| aux layers | `[1, 10, 22]` |
71	| top-k logits | 256 when `EAGLE3_TOP_K=256` |
72	| B12X | off (`SGLANG_ENABLE_B12X=0`) |
73	
74	The request uses `max_new_tokens = generate_tokens + 1`. The saved training
75	sequence is `prompt_last + generated[:generate_tokens]`; the extra generated
76	token exists so the last saved generated token has target logits.
77	
78	The collector uses a rolling 512-request client window while the server caps
79	`--max-running-requests` at 64. Each completed request immediately schedules
80	the next prompt, so SGLang's queue stays non-empty until the dataset tail.
81	Segment assignment is deterministic by global sample index (`idx // segment_size`),
82	so retries keep the same local/BOS layout.
83	
84	Hook collection captures FULL hidden-state CUDA graphs only when
85	`EAGLE3_ONESTAGE_DIR` is present. Non-hook production inference does not set
86	that env var and keeps the normal hidden-capture mode.
87	
88	## Upload Model
89	
90	Upload is asynchronous. The collector puts a completed `seg_XXXXXX/` directory
91	into `BosUploader.queue`; background `bos-upload-*` worker threads run the BOS
92	copy. The main collection loop only pauses when:
93	
94	- free space under `--stage-dir` is below `--min-free-gb`
95	- pending upload queue length exceeds `--upload-queue-limit`
96	- an upload worker sets a fatal error
97	
98	Every uploaded segment contains `_SUCCESS.json` with file count, total bytes,
99	and per-file sizes. Per-file sha256 is available with `EAGLE_V3MIX_SHA256=1`,
100	but is off by default. Progress bars are on by default; set
101	`EAGLE_V3MIX_PROGRESS=0` for a quiet run. Successfully uploaded local segment directories are
102	removed. On restart, any sealed segment directory with index lower than
103	`state.next_segment_idx` and missing from `state.uploaded_segments` is requeued.
104	
105	Failure behavior:
106	
107	- request/finalize failures are written to `failures.jsonl`
108	- `state.next_idx` rewinds to the first failed sample in the batch
109	- upload worker failure is fatal to the main loop
110	- partial local segments are left in place for retry
111	
112	## 256-Sample Smoke
113	
114	Run on 2026-05-16, single GPU, no-spec, `dense_as_sparse=True`, 64 concurrency,
115	`full_ignore_eos_ratio=0.01`, `generate_tokens=2047`, `EAGLE3_TOP_K=256`.
116	
117	| Version | Wall s | Train tok/s | Finalize drain s | Uploaded GiB | Upload MiB/s | Failures |
118	|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
119	| pre-overlap baseline | 228.54 | 1533.08 | 64.31 | 3.02 | 95.47 | 0 |
120	| current overlap path | 169.71 | 2069.40 | 11.09 | 3.03 | 87.28 | 0 |
121	
122	Current metrics:
123	
124	- 256 files, 351192 training tokens
125	- average file size 12.11 MiB
126	- average saved rows 1371.84 tokens/file
127	- finalize worker CPU time 87.60 s, of which 76.51 s overlaps request tail
128	- finalize breakdown: load 1.59 s, validate 0.41 s, NVFP4 compress 80.19 s,
129	  save 5.37 s
130	
131	The bottleneck is still target generation. Upload did not backpressure the
132	collector (`pending=0` during the run).
133	
134	Post-fix graph/window check on 2026-05-16 used the rolling collector with
135	`generate_tokens=63`, `batch_size=128`, `limit=128`, and no upload. It completed
136	128 files with 8192 train tokens in 3.65 s (`2244.65 tok/s`), `failures=0`.
137	SGLang decode logs showed `cuda graph: True` and steady refill with
138	`#running-req: 64, #queue-req: 64`; state ended at `next_idx=128`,
139	`next_segment_idx=2`.
140

32	def cjk_ratio(text: str) -> float:
33	    if not text:
34	        return 0.0
35	    cjk = sum(1 for ch in text if "\u4e00" <= ch <= "\u9fff")
36	    letters = sum(1 for ch in text if ch.isalpha() or ("\u4e00" <= ch <= "\u9fff"))
37	    return cjk / max(letters, 1)
38	
39	
40	def norm_text(text: str) -> str:
41	    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text.strip().lower())
42	
43	
44	def iter_jsonl(path: Path) -> Iterable[dict[str, Any]]:
45	    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as f:
46	        for line in f:
47	            if line.strip():
48	                yield json.loads(line)
49

51	def load_json(path: Path) -> Any:

56	def rows_from_sharegpt_messages(
57	    raw_messages: list[dict[str, Any]],
58	) -> tuple[list[dict[str, str]], str]:
59	    messages = []
60	    answer_parts = []
61	    for m in raw_messages or []:
62	        if isinstance(m, dict) and ("human" in m or "assistant" in m) and "role" not in m and "from" not in m:
63	            human = str(m.get("human") or "").strip()
64	            assistant = str(m.get("assistant") or "").strip()
65	            if human:
66	                messages.append({"role": "user", "content": human})
67	            if assistant:
68	                answer_parts.append(assistant)
69	                messages.append({"role": "assistant", "content": assistant})
70	            continue
71	        role = str(m.get("role") or m.get("from") or "user")
72	        if role in {"human", "prompter"}:
73	            role = "user"
74	        elif role in {"gpt", "bot", "assistant"}:
75	            role = "assistant"
76	        elif role not in {"system", "user", "assistant"}:
77	            continue
78	        content = str(m.get("content") or m.get("value") or "").strip()
79	        if not content:
80	            continue
81	        if role == "assistant":
82	            answer_parts.append(content)
83	        messages.append({"role": role, "content": content})
84	    return base.first_user_prefix(messages), "\n".join(answer_parts)
85	
86	
87	def message_seq(value: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
88	    if value is None:
89	        return []
90	    if hasattr(value, "tolist"):
91	        value = value.tolist()
92	    return list(value)
93	
94	
95	def mk_raw(
96	    *,
97	    source: str,
98	    source_group: str,
99	    mix_group: str,
100	    license_bucket: str,
101	    messages: list[dict[str, str]],
102	    answer: str,
103	    id_parts: tuple[Any, ...],
104	) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
105	    if not messages or messages[-1]["role"] != "user":
106	        return None
107	    first_user = messages[-1]["content"]
108	    if len(first_user.strip()) < 2:
109	        return None
110	    return {
111	        "source": source,
112	        "source_id": base.stable_id(source, source_group, *map(str, id_parts)),
113	        "source_group": source_group,
114	        "mix_group": mix_group,
115	        "license_bucket": license_bucket,
116	        "messages": messages,
117	        "answer": answer or "",
118	    }
119	
120	
121	def clamp_messages(messages: list[dict[str, str]], max_user_chars: int) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
122	    out = []
123	    for m in messages:
124	        content = m["content"]
125	        if m["role"] == "user" and len(content) > max_user_chars:
126	            content = content[:max_user_chars].rstrip()
127	        out.append({"role": m["role"], "content": content})
128	    return out
129

131	def append_if(row: dict[str, Any] | None, rows: list[dict[str, Any]], max_docs: int) -> bool:
132	    if row is None:
133	        return False
134	    rows.append(row)
135	    return bool(max_docs and len(rows) >= max_docs)

182	def load_instruction_jsonl(

273	def load_sharegpt_shareai_long(max_docs: int, **_: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
274	    # Pull from all ShareGPT shards, then take the longest prompts.  The main
275	    # ShareGPT slice already consumes many early common_zh rows, so a shallow
276	    # read leaves too little non-overlapping long-context coverage.
277	    rows = load_sharegpt_shareai(max_docs=max_docs * 20)
278	    rows.sort(key=lambda r: len(r["messages"][-1]["content"]), reverse=True)
279	    out = rows[:max_docs]
280	    for r in out:
281	        r["source"] = "sharegpt_shareai_long"
282	        r["source_id"] = base.stable_id(r["source"], r["source_id"])
283	        r["mix_group"] = "long_context_writing"
284	    return out

521	def load_codeexercise(max_docs: int, **_: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
522	    rows = []
523	    path = Path([REDACTED])
524	    for r in iter_jsonl(path):
525	        messages = []
526	        answer_parts = []
527	        for m in r.get("chat_rounds") or []:
528	            role = "user" if m.get("role") == "human" else "assistant"
529	            content = str(m.get("content") or "").strip()
530	            if not content:
531	                continue
532	            if role == "assistant":
533	                answer_parts.append(content)
534	            messages.append({"role": role, "content": content})
535	        prefix = base.first_user_prefix(messages)
536	        row = mk_raw(
537	            source="codeexercise_python",
538	            source_group=str(r.get("data_name") or "python"),
539	            mix_group="code",
540	            license_bucket="modelscope",
541	            messages=prefix,
542	            answer="\n".join(answer_parts),
543	            id_parts=(r.get("id"), prefix[-1]["content"] if prefix else ""),
544	        )
545	        if append_if(row, rows, max_docs):
546	            break
547	    return rows

558	def load_long_messages(
559	    path: Path,

562	    max_docs: int,

564	) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:

575	            answer=ans,
576	            id_parts=(r.get("id", i), messages[-1]["content"] if messages else "", ans[:256]),

603	def load_infinity(
604	    max_docs: int,
605	    *,
606	    subdirs: list[str],
607	    source_group: str,
608	    mix_group: str,
609	    allow_download: bool = False,
610	) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
611	    rows = []
612	    base_dir = Path([REDACTED])
613	    for subdir in subdirs:
614	        for pq in sorted((base_dir / subdir).glob("*.parquet")):
615	            df = pd.read_parquet(pq, columns=["id", "conversations", "langdetect", "source"])
616	            df = df.sample(frac=1.0, random_state=SEED)
617	            for _, r in df.iterrows():
618	                messages, ans = rows_from_sharegpt_messages(message_seq(r.get("conversations")))
619	                if not messages:
620	                    continue
621	                lang = str(r.get("langdetect") or "")
622	                if mix_group != "code" and lang not in {"zh-cn", "zh"} and cjk_ratio(messages[-1]["content"]) < 0.05:
623	                    continue
624	                row = mk_raw(
625	                    source="infinity_instruct",
626	                    source_group=f"{source_group}:{r.get('source') or 'unknown'}",
627	                    mix_group=mix_group,
628	                    license_bucket="cc-by-sa",
629	                    messages=messages,
630	                    answer=ans,
631	                    id_parts=(subdir, r.get("id"), messages[-1]["content"], ans[:256]),
632	                )
633	                if append_if(row, rows, max_docs):
634	                    return rows
635	    return rows

642	        r["license_bucket"] = "modelscope"

646	def source_specs() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
647	    return [
648	        {"name": "sharegpt_shareai", "count": 45_000, "loader": load_sharegpt_shareai},
649	        {"name": "wildchat_zh", "count": 15_000, "loader": load_wildchat_zh},

656	        {"name": "coig_p", "count": 10_000, "loader": load_coig_p, "oversample": 4.0},

659	        {"name": "codefeedback_zh", "count": 12_000, "loader": load_codefeedback_zh},
660	        {"name": "smoltalk_code", "count": 9_000, "loader": load_smoltalk, "kwargs": {"categories": ["coding", "data_analysis"], "mix_group": "code"}},
661	        {"name": "codeexercise", "count": 6_000, "loader": load_codeexercise},
662	        {"name": "codeforces", "count": 4_000, "loader": load_codeforces, "oversample": 4.0},
663	        {"name": "longalign", "count": 8_000, "loader": load_longalign, "oversample": 2.0},
664	        {"name": "longwriter", "count": 5_000, "loader": load_longwriter},
665	        {"name": "smoltalk_long", "count": 10_000, "loader": load_smoltalk, "kwargs": {"categories": ["document_qa", "summary", "editing"], "mix_group": "long_context_writing"}},
666	        {"name": "infinity_long", "count": 8_000, "loader": load_infinity, "kwargs": {"subdirs": ["0625"], "source_group": "0625", "mix_group": "long_context_writing"}},
667	        {"name": "sharegpt_long", "count": 4_000, "loader": load_sharegpt_shareai_long, "oversample": 2.5},
668	        {"name": "infinity_core", "count": 19_000, "loader": load_infinity, "kwargs": {"subdirs": ["7M_core"], "source_group": "core", "mix_group": "general_reservoir_edge"}},
669	        {"name": "smoltalk_general", "count": 11_000, "loader": load_smoltalk, "kwargs": {"categories": ["format", "safe", "planning", "everyday"], "mix_group": "general_reservoir_edge"}},
670	        {"name": "dolphin_r1", "count": 5_400, "loader": load_dolphin},
671	        {"name": "sharegpt_bilingual", "count": 4_000, "loader": load_sharegpt_bilingual},
672	        {"name": "infinity_code_reservoir", "count": 600, "loader": load_infinity, "kwargs": {"subdirs": ["7M_domains/code"], "source_group": "code", "mix_group": "general_reservoir_edge"}},
673	    ]

675	
676	def scaled_counts(total: int) -> list[tuple[dict[str, Any], int]]:
677	    specs = source_specs()
678	    raw = [(spec, total * spec["count"] / TOTAL_BASE) for spec in specs]
679	    counts = [(spec, int(v)) for spec, v in raw]
680	    remain = total - sum(c for _, c in counts)
681	    order = sorted(range(len(raw)), key=lambda i: raw[i][1] - int(raw[i][1]), reverse=True)
682	    out = list(counts)
683	    for i in order[:remain]:
684	        spec, c = out[i]
685	        out[i] = (spec, c + 1)
686	    return [(s, c) for s, c in out if c > 0]
687	
688	
689	def make_prompt_row(tokenizer, raw: dict[str, Any], args) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
690	    row = base.make_row(
691	        tokenizer,
692	        source=raw["source"],
693	        source_id=raw["source_id"],
694	        source_group=raw["source_group"],
695	        messages=raw["messages"],
696	        original_answer=raw.get("answer", ""),
697	        max_prompt_tokens=args.max_prompt_tokens,
698	        min_prompt_tokens=args.min_prompt_tokens,
699	    )
700	    if row is None:
701	        return None
702	    row["mix_group"] = raw.get("mix_group", "")
703	    row["license_bucket"] = raw.get("license_bucket", "")
704	    row["bucket"] = row["prompt_n_tokens"]
705	    return row
706	
707	
708	def tokenize_rows(tokenizer, raws: list[dict[str, Any]], args, label: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
709	    def one(r):
710	        return make_prompt_row(tokenizer, r, args)
711	
712	    out = []
713	    if args.tokenize_workers <= 1:
714	        iterator = map(one, raws)
715	    else:
716	        ex = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.tokenize_workers)
717	        iterator = ex.map(one, raws)
718	    for row in tqdm(iterator, total=len(raws), desc=f"tok {label}"):
719	        if row is not None:
720	            out.append(row)
721	    if args.tokenize_workers > 1:
722	        ex.shutdown(wait=True)
723	    return out
724	
725	
726	def main() -> None:
727	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
728	    ap.add_argument("--out", type=Path, default=Path("eagle/prompts/target_regen/v3mix_300k.jsonl"))
729	    ap.add_argument("--model", default=base.DEFAULT_MODEL)
730	    ap.add_argument("--target_total", type=int, default=300_000)
731	    ap.add_argument("--max_prompt_tokens", type=int, default=4096)
732	    ap.add_argument("--min_prompt_tokens", type=int, default=8)
733	    ap.add_argument("--tokenize_workers", type=int, default=8)
734	    ap.add_argument("--oversample", type=float, default=1.35)
735	    ap.add_argument("--allow_hf_download", action="store_true")
736	    ap.add_argument("--exclude_prompts", type=Path, action="append", default=[])
737	    args = ap.parse_args()
738	
739	    exclude_ids = set()
740	    for path in args.exclude_prompts:
741	        with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as f:
742	            for line in f:
743	                if line.strip():
744	                    sid = json.loads(line).get("source_id")
745	                    if sid:
746	                        exclude_ids.add(str(sid))
747	
748	    print(f"[v3mix] target_total={args.target_total} out={args.out}")
749	    print(f"[v3mix] loading tokenizer: {args.model}")
750	    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model, trust_remote_code=True, use_fast=True)
751	    rng = random.Random(SEED)
752	
753	    selected = []
754	    seen_source_ids = set(exclude_ids)
755	    seen_prompt_hashes = set()
756	    for spec, need in scaled_counts(args.target_total):
757	        max_docs = max(int(need * float(spec.get("oversample", args.oversample))), need + 256)
758	        kwargs = dict(spec.get("kwargs") or {})
759	        kwargs["allow_download"] = args.allow_hf_download
760	        print(f"\n[v3mix] source={spec['name']} need={need} load_max={max_docs}")
761	        raws = spec["loader"](max_docs=max_docs, **kwargs)
762	        rng.shuffle(raws)
763	        print(f"  raw rows: {len(raws)}")
764	        rows = tokenize_rows(tokenizer, raws, args, spec["name"])
765	        print(f"  valid prompt rows: {len(rows)}")
766	
767	        uniq = []
768	        for row in rows:
769	            sid = row["source_id"]
770	            ph = base.stable_id(norm_text(row["prompt_text"]))
771	            if sid in seen_source_ids or ph in seen_prompt_hashes:
772	                continue
773	            seen_source_ids.add(sid)
774	            seen_prompt_hashes.add(ph)
775	            uniq.append(row)
776	            if len(uniq) >= need:
777	                break
778	        if len(uniq) < need:
779	            raise RuntimeError(
780	                f"{spec['name']}: need {need}, only {len(uniq)} usable after filters"
781	            )
782	        selected.extend(uniq)
783	
784	    rng.shuffle(selected)
785	    args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
786	    with args.out.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
787	        for row in selected:
788	            f.write(json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
789	
790	    by_source: dict[str, int] = {}
791	    by_group: dict[str, int] = {}
792	    lengths = []
793	    for row in selected:
794	        by_source[row["source"]] = by_source.get(row["source"], 0) + 1
795	        by_group[row["mix_group"]] = by_group.get(row["mix_group"], 0) + 1
796	        lengths.append(int(row["prompt_n_tokens"]))
797	    lengths.sort()
798	    n = len(lengths)
799	    print(f"\n[v3mix] wrote {args.out}")
800	    print("  rows:", n)
801	    print("  by_group:", by_group)
802	    print("  by_source:", by_source)
803	    print("  prompt_tokens min/p50/p90/p99/max:",
804	          lengths[0], lengths[n // 2], lengths[int(0.90 * (n - 1))],
805	          lengths[int(0.99 * (n - 1))], lengths[-1])
806	    print(f"  size_mb: {args.out.stat().st_size / 1e6:.1f}")
807	
808	
809	if __name__ == "__main__":
810	    main()
811

38	def load_jsonl(path: Path, start: int, limit: int) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
39	    out = []
40	    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as f:
41	        for i, line in enumerate(f):
42	            if i < start or not line.strip():
43	                continue
44	            out.append(json.loads(line))
45	            if limit > 0 and len(out) >= limit:
46	                break
47	    return out

51	    return re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9._-]+", "_", s)[:96] or "unknown"

56	    return seg_dir / f"{idx:07d}_{source}.pt"

59	def segment_dir_for_idx(stage_dir: Path, idx: int, segment_size: int) -> tuple[int, Path]:

63	
64	def disk_free_gb(path: Path) -> float:
65	    target = path
66	    while not target.exists() and target != target.parent:
67	        target = target.parent
68	    st = os.statvfs(target)
69	    return st.f_bavail * st.f_frsize / 1024**3
70	
71	
72	def is_full_ignore_eos(idx: int, ratio: float) -> bool:
73	    if ratio <= 0:
74	        return False
75	    if ratio >= 1:
76	        return True
77	    return ((idx * 2654435761 % 10000) / 10000.0) < ratio
78

80	def parse_bos_uri(uri: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
81	    if not uri.startswith("bos://"):
82	        raise ValueError(f"invalid BOS uri: {uri}")
83	    rest = uri[len("bos://"):]
84	    bucket, _, prefix = rest.partition("/")
85	    return bucket, prefix.strip("/")

88	def parse_segment_idx(path: Path) -> int | None:
89	    m = re.fullmatch(r"seg_(\d+)", path.name)
90	    return int(m.group(1)) if m else None

93	def append_jsonl(path: Path, record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
94	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
95	    with path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
96	        f.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
97	
98	
99	def sha256_file(path: Path, block_size: int = 8 << 20) -> str:
100	    h = hashlib.sha256()
101	    with path.open("rb") as f:
102	        while True:
103	            chunk = f.read(block_size)
104	            if not chunk:
105	                break
106	            h.update(chunk)
107	    return h.hexdigest()
108

111	    idx: int, reason: Any, sample: dict[str, Any] | None = None
112	) -> dict[str, Any]:
113	    if isinstance(reason, (str, int, float, bool)) or reason is None:
114	        value = reason
115	    else:
116	        value = repr(reason)
117	    record = {"idx": int(idx), "reason": value, "time": time.time()}
118	    if sample:

128	class State:
129	    def __init__(self, path: Path):
130	        self.path = path
131	        self.next_idx = 0
132	        self.next_segment_idx = 0
133	        self.uploaded_segments: set[int] = set()
134	        self.n_uploaded_files = 0
135	        self.n_uploaded_bytes = 0
136	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
137	        self.load()
138	
139	    def load(self) -> None:
140	        if not self.path.exists():
141	            return
142	        try:
143	            d = json.loads(self.path.read_text())
144	            self.next_idx = int(d.get("next_idx", 0))
145	            self.next_segment_idx = int(d.get("next_segment_idx", 0))
146	            self.uploaded_segments = set(map(int, d.get("uploaded_segments", [])))
147	            self.n_uploaded_files = int(d.get("n_uploaded_files", 0))
148	            self.n_uploaded_bytes = int(d.get("n_uploaded_bytes", 0))
149	        except Exception as e:
150	            print(f"[state] ignoring unreadable state {self.path}: {e}")
151	
152	    def save(self) -> None:
153	        with self._lock:
154	            self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
155	            tmp = self.path.with_suffix(self.path.suffix + ".tmp")
156	            tmp.write_text(json.dumps({
157	                "next_idx": self.next_idx,
158	                "next_segment_idx": self.next_segment_idx,
159	                "uploaded_segments": sorted(self.uploaded_segments),
160	                "n_uploaded_files": self.n_uploaded_files,
161	                "n_uploaded_bytes": self.n_uploaded_bytes,
162	                "updated_at": time.time(),
163	            }, indent=1))
164	            tmp.replace(self.path)
165	
166	    def mark_uploaded(self, seg_idx: int, n_files: int, n_bytes: int) -> None:
167	        with self._lock:
168	            self.uploaded_segments.add(seg_idx)
169	            self.n_uploaded_files += n_files
170	            self.n_uploaded_bytes += n_bytes
171	        self.save()
172	
173	
174	class BosUploader:
175	    def __init__(self, args, state: State, pbar: tqdm | None):
176	        self.args = args
177	        self.state = state
178	        self.pbar = pbar
179	        self.queue: Queue = Queue()
180	        self.stop = threading.Event()
181	        self.client = None
182	        self.bucket = ""
183	        self.prefix = ""
184	        self.method = args.upload_method
185	        self.uploaded_bytes_this_run = 0
186	        self.uploaded_files_this_run = 0
187	        self.upload_seconds_this_run = 0.0
188	        self._stats_lock = threading.Lock()
189	        self._fatal_error: str | None = None
190	        self._fatal_lock = threading.Lock()
191	        self._queued_segments: set[int] = set()
192	        self._queued_lock = threading.Lock()
193	        if self.method != "none":
194	            self.bucket, self.prefix = parse_bos_uri(args.bos_prefix)
195	            if self.method in {"auto", "sdk"}:
196	                self.client = self._make_sdk_client()
197	                if self.client is not None:
198	                    self.method = "sdk"
199	                elif self.method == "sdk":
200	                    raise RuntimeError("BOS SDK requested but unavailable")
201	                else:
202	                    self.method = "bcecmd"
203	        self.workers = [
204	            threading.Thread(target=self._worker, name=f"bos-upload-{i}", daemon=True)
205	            for i in range(args.upload_workers)
206	        ]
207	        for t in self.workers:
208	            t.start()
209	
210	    def _set_failed(self, message: str) -> None:
211	        with self._fatal_lock:
212	            if self._fatal_error is None:
213	                self._fatal_error = message
214	
215	    def has_failed(self) -> bool:
216	        with self._fatal_lock:
217	            return self._fatal_error is not None
218	
219	    def raise_if_failed(self) -> None:
220	        with self._fatal_lock:
221	            if self._fatal_error is not None:
222	                raise RuntimeError(self._fatal_error)
223	
224	    def _make_sdk_client(self):
225	        try:
226	            from baidubce.auth.bce_credentials import BceCredentials
227	            from baidubce.bce_client_configuration import BceClientConfiguration
228	            from baidubce.services.bos.bos_client import BosClient
229	        except Exception:
230	            return None
231	        cred_path = Path(self.args.bce_conf) / "credentials"
232	        conf_path = Path(self.args.bce_conf) / "config"
233	        if not cred_path.exists():
234	            return None
235	        cp = configparser.ConfigParser()
236	        cp.read(cred_path)
237	        section = "Defaults" if cp.has_section("Defaults") else cp.sections()[0]
238	        ak = cp.get(section, "Ak", fallback=cp.get(section, "ak", fallback=""))
239	        sk = cp.get(section, "Sk", fallback=cp.get(section, "sk", fallback=""))
240	        if not ak or not sk:
241	            return None
242	        endpoint = "http://bj.bcebos.com"
243	        if conf_path.exists():
244	            cp2 = configparser.ConfigParser()
245	            cp2.read(conf_path)
246	            sec2 = "Defaults" if cp2.has_section("Defaults") else (cp2.sections()[0] if cp2.sections() else "")
247	            domain = cp2.get(sec2, "Domain", fallback="").strip()
248	            if domain:
249	                endpoint = "http://" + domain.replace("http://", "").replace("https://", "")
250	        return BosClient(BceClientConfiguration(
251	            credentials=BceCredentials(ak, sk),
252	            endpoint=endpoint,
253	        ))
254	
255	    def pending(self) -> int:
256	        return self.queue.qsize()
257	
258	    def submit(self, seg_dir: Path, seg_idx: int) -> None:
259	        if self.method == "none":
260	            return
261	        self.raise_if_failed()
262	        with self._queued_lock:
263	            if seg_idx in self.state.uploaded_segments or seg_idx in self._queued_segments:
264	                return
265	            self._queued_segments.add(seg_idx)
266	        self.queue.put((seg_dir, seg_idx))
267	
268	    def _worker(self) -> None:
269	        while not self.stop.is_set():
270	            try:
271	                item = self.queue.get(timeout=1.0)
272	            except Empty:
273	                continue
274	            if item is None:
275	                self.queue.task_done()
276	                break
277	            seg_dir, seg_idx = item
278	            try:
279	                self._upload_segment(seg_dir, seg_idx)
280	            except Exception as e:
281	                self._set_failed(
282	                    f"upload failed for seg_{seg_idx:06d}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"
283	                )
284	            finally:
285	                with self._queued_lock:
286	                    self._queued_segments.discard(seg_idx)
287	                self.queue.task_done()
288	
289	    def _upload_segment(self, seg_dir: Path, seg_idx: int) -> None:
290	        if seg_idx in self.state.uploaded_segments:
291	            shutil.rmtree(seg_dir, ignore_errors=True)
292	            return
293	        files = sorted(seg_dir.glob("*.pt"))
294	        if not files:
295	            shutil.rmtree(seg_dir, ignore_errors=True)
296	            return
297	        manifest = self._write_success_manifest(seg_dir, seg_idx, files)
298	        n_bytes = int(manifest["bytes"])
299	        t0 = time.monotonic()
300	        if self.method == "sdk":
301	            self._upload_segment_sdk(seg_dir, seg_idx, files)
302	        else:
303	            self._upload_segment_bcecmd(seg_dir, seg_idx)
304	        elapsed = max(time.monotonic() - t0, 1e-6)
305	        self.state.mark_uploaded(seg_idx, len(files), n_bytes)
306	        with self._stats_lock:
307	            self.uploaded_bytes_this_run += n_bytes
308	            self.uploaded_files_this_run += len(files)
309	            self.upload_seconds_this_run += elapsed
310	        metric = {
311	            "type": "upload_segment",
312	            "segment": seg_idx,
313	            "files": len(files),
314	            "bytes": n_bytes,
315	            "elapsed_s": elapsed,
316	            "mb_s": n_bytes / elapsed / 1024**2,
317	            "method": self.method,
318	            "time": time.time(),
319	        }
320	        append_jsonl(self.args.metrics_file, metric)
321	        shutil.rmtree(seg_dir, ignore_errors=True)
322	        if self.pbar is not None:
323	            self.pbar.update(len(files))
324	
325	    def _write_success_manifest(
326	        self, seg_dir: Path, seg_idx: int, files: list[Path]
327	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
328	        entries = []
329	        total = 0
330	        for f in files:
331	            size = f.stat().st_size
332	            total += size
333	            item = {"name": f.name, "bytes": size}
334	            if self.args.sha256:
335	                item["sha256"] = sha256_file(f)
336	            entries.append(item)
337	        manifest = {
338	            "schema": "eagle_v3mix_segment_v1",
339	            "segment": seg_idx,
340	            "files": len(files),
341	            "bytes": total,
342	            "sha256": bool(self.args.sha256),
343	            "created_at": time.time(),
344	            "entries": entries,
345	        }
346	        (seg_dir / "_SUCCESS.json").write_text(
347	            json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1),
348	            encoding="utf-8",
349	        )
350	        return manifest
351	
352	    def _upload_segment_sdk(self, seg_dir: Path, seg_idx: int, files: list[Path]) -> None:
353	        assert self.client is not None
354	        remote_base = "/".join(x for x in [self.prefix, f"seg_{seg_idx:06d}"] if x)
355	        for attempt in range(1, self.args.upload_retries + 1):
356	            try:
357	                for f in files:
358	                    key = f"{remote_base}/{f.name}"
359	                    self.client.put_object_from_file(self.bucket, key, str(f))
360	                self.client.put_object_from_file(
361	                    self.bucket, f"{remote_base}/_SUCCESS.json", str(seg_dir / "_SUCCESS.json")
362	                )
363	                return
364	            except Exception as e:
365	                if attempt >= self.args.upload_retries:
366	                    raise
367	                print(f"[upload-sdk] seg={seg_idx} attempt={attempt} failed: {e}")
368	                time.sleep(5 * attempt)
369	
370	    def _upload_segment_bcecmd(self, seg_dir: Path, seg_idx: int) -> None:
371	        remote = f"{self.args.bos_prefix.rstrip('/')}/seg_{seg_idx:06d}/"
372	        for attempt in range(1, self.args.upload_retries + 1):
373	            cmd = [
374	                self.args.bcecmd,
375	                "--conf-path", self.args.bce_conf,
376	                "bos", "cp", "-r",
377	                str(seg_dir) + "/",
378	                remote,
379	            ]
380	            try:
381	                subprocess.run(
382	                    cmd,
383	                    check=True,
384	                    stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
385	                    stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
386	                    text=True,
387	                    timeout=self.args.upload_timeout,
388	                )
389	                return
390	            except Exception as e:
391	                if attempt >= self.args.upload_retries:
392	                    raise
393	                print(f"[upload-bcecmd] seg={seg_idx} attempt={attempt} failed: {e}")
394	                time.sleep(5 * attempt)
395	
396	    def wait(self) -> None:
397	        while True:
398	            self.raise_if_failed()
399	            if getattr(self.queue, "unfinished_tasks", 0) == 0:
400	                return
401	            time.sleep(0.5)
402	
403	    def shutdown(self) -> None:
404	        self.stop.set()
405	        for _ in self.workers:
406	            self.queue.put(None)
407	        for t in self.workers:
408	            t.join(timeout=5.0)
409

411	async def send_one(session: aiohttp.ClientSession, sample: dict[str, Any], idx: int, args):
412	    input_ids = sample.get("input_ids")
413	    if not isinstance(input_ids, list) or not input_ids:
414	        return idx, False, "bad_input_ids"
415	    full_ignore_eos = args.ignore_eos or is_full_ignore_eos(idx, args.full_ignore_eos_ratio)
416	    mode = "full" if full_ignore_eos else "norm"
417	    rid = f"{args.rid_prefix}_{mode}_{idx:07d}_{args.generate_tokens}"
418	    hook_path = args.collect_dir / f"{safe_name(rid)}.pt"
419	    hook_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
420	    try:
421	        async with session.post(
422	            f"{args.api_base}/generate",
423	            json={
424	                "rid": rid,
425	                "input_ids": input_ids,
426	                "sampling_params": {
427	                    "temperature": args.temperature,
428	                    "max_new_tokens": args.generate_tokens + 1,
429	                    "ignore_eos": full_ignore_eos,
430	                },
431	            },
432	            timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=args.request_timeout),
433	        ) as resp:
434	            text = await resp.text()
435	            if resp.status != 200:
436	                return idx, False, f"http_{resp.status}:{text[:160]}"
437	            body = json.loads(text)
438	    except Exception as e:
439	        return idx, False, f"request_error:{type(e).__name__}:{e}"
440	    if "error" in body:
441	        return idx, False, f"body_error:{str(body)[:160]}"
442	    return idx, True, {
443	        "idx": idx,
444	        "sample": sample,
445	        "input_ids": input_ids,
446	        "mode": mode,
447	        "ignore_eos": full_ignore_eos,
448	        "hook_path": hook_path,
449	        "body": body,
450	    }
451	
452	
453	def wait_pt(path: Path, timeout_s: float) -> bool:
454	    deadline = time.time() + timeout_s
455	    while time.time() < deadline:
456	        if path.exists() and path.stat().st_size > 0:
457	            return True
458	        time.sleep(0.05)
459	    return False
460	
461	
462	async def preflight_hook_format(
463	    session: aiohttp.ClientSession,
464	    sample: dict[str, Any],
465	    idx: int,
466	    args,
467	) -> None:
468	    input_ids = sample.get("input_ids")
469	    if not isinstance(input_ids, list) or not input_ids:
470	        raise RuntimeError("preflight failed: first resume sample has bad input_ids")
471	    probe_ids = input_ids[-min(len(input_ids), 16):]
472	    rid = f"{args.rid_prefix}_full_preflight_{os.getpid()}_{idx}_1"
473	    hook_path = args.collect_dir / f"{safe_name(rid)}.pt"
474	    hook_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
475	    try:
476	        async with session.post(
477	            f"{args.api_base}/generate",
478	            json={
479	                "rid": rid,
480	                "input_ids": probe_ids,
481	                "sampling_params": {
482	                    "temperature": args.temperature,
483	                    "max_new_tokens": 2,
484	                    "ignore_eos": True,
485	                },
486	            },
487	            timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=min(args.request_timeout, 300.0)),
488	        ) as resp:
489	            text = await resp.text()
490	            if resp.status != 200:
491	                raise RuntimeError(f"preflight generate http_{resp.status}:{text[:200]}")
492	    except Exception as e:
493	        hook_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
494	        raise RuntimeError(f"preflight generate failed: {type(e).__name__}:{e}") from e
495	
496	    if not wait_pt(hook_path, min(args.hook_timeout, 60.0)):
497	        raise RuntimeError(f"preflight failed: no hook output at {hook_path}")
498	    try:
499	        data = torch.load(hook_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True)
500	    finally:
501	        hook_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
502	
503	    direct_nvfp4 = nvfp4_codec.is_nvfp4_sample(data) and "aux_hidden" not in data
504	    if not direct_nvfp4:
505	        metadata = data.get("metadata", {})
506	        keys = sorted(str(k) for k in data.keys())
507	        raise RuntimeError(
508	            "preflight failed: hook is not direct NVFP4; "
509	            f"keys={keys}, format={data.get('format')!r}, metadata={metadata!r}"
510	        )
511	    missing = [k for k in ("aux_packed", "aux_scale", "aux_hidden_shape") if k not in data]
512	    if missing:
513	        raise RuntimeError(f"preflight failed: missing direct NVFP4 keys {missing}")
514	    metadata = data.get("metadata", {})
515	    if metadata.get("aux_storage") not in {None, "nvfp4_direct"}:
516	        raise RuntimeError(f"preflight failed: unexpected aux_storage={metadata.get('aux_storage')!r}")
517	    print(
518	        "[preflight] direct NVFP4 hook ok "
519	        f"format={data.get('format')} shape={tuple(data.get('aux_hidden_shape'))}",
520	        flush=True,
521	    )
522	
523	
524	def finalize_and_compress(ctx: dict[str, Any], dst_path: Path, args) -> tuple[bool, Any]:
525	    hook_path = ctx["hook_path"]
526	    timings: dict[str, float] = {}
527	    total_t0 = time.monotonic()
528	    try:
529	        t0 = time.monotonic()
530	        if not wait_pt(hook_path, args.hook_timeout):
531	            return False, "no_hook_output"
532	        timings["wait_s"] = time.monotonic() - t0
533	
534	        t0 = time.monotonic()
535	        load_kwargs = {
536	            "map_location": "cpu",
537	            "weights_only": True,
538	        }
539	        if args.hook_mmap:
540	            load_kwargs["mmap"] = True
541	        try:
542	            data = torch.load(hook_path, **load_kwargs)
543	        except Exception:
544	            if not args.hook_mmap:
545	                raise
546	            load_kwargs.pop("mmap", None)
547	            data = torch.load(hook_path, **load_kwargs)
548	        timings["load_s"] = time.monotonic() - t0
549	
550	        t0 = time.monotonic()
551	        n = int(data["token_ids"].numel())
552	        expected = args.generate_tokens + 1
553	        if ctx["mode"] == "full" and n != expected:
554	            return False, f"bad_token_len:{n}!={expected}"
555	        if ctx["mode"] == "norm" and not (1 <= n <= expected):
556	            return False, f"bad_token_len_norm:{n}"
557	        if data["assistant_mask"].shape[0] != n:
558	            return False, "bad_mask_len"
559	        if bool(data["assistant_mask"][0].item()):
560	            return False, "prompt_last_masked_trainable"
561	        if int(data["assistant_mask"][1:].sum().item()) != n - 1:
562	            return False, "generated_mask_not_all_trainable"
563	        aux_is_nvfp4 = nvfp4_codec.is_nvfp4_sample(data) and "aux_hidden" not in data
564	        if not aux_is_nvfp4:
565	            return False, "hook_not_direct_nvfp4"
566	        for key in ("aux_packed", "aux_scale", "aux_hidden_shape"):
567	            if key not in data:
568	                return False, f"{key}_missing"
569	        aux_shape = tuple(data["aux_hidden_shape"])
570	        if len(aux_shape) != 2 or aux_shape[0] != n:
571	            return False, f"aux_hidden_shape_bad:{aux_shape}"
572	        if data["aux_packed"].shape[0] != n or data["aux_scale"].shape[0] != n:
573	            return False, "aux_nvfp4_bad_len"
574	        if data["aux_packed"].dtype != torch.uint8:
575	            return False, f"aux_packed_bad_dtype:{data['aux_packed'].dtype}"
576	        if data["aux_scale"].dtype != torch.bfloat16:
577	            return False, f"aux_scale_bad_dtype:{data['aux_scale'].dtype}"
578	        for key in ("top_logit_values", "top_logit_indices", "target_logsumexp"):
579	            if key not in data or data[key].shape[0] != n:
580	                return False, f"{key}_bad_len"
581	        if int(data["token_ids"][0].item()) != int(ctx["input_ids"][-1]):
582	            return False, "prompt_last_token_mismatch"
583	
584	        sample = ctx["sample"]
585	        data["source"] = sample.get("source", "")
586	        data["source_id"] = sample.get("source_id", "")
587	        data["source_group"] = sample.get("source_group", "")
588	        data["mix_group"] = sample.get("mix_group", "")
589	        data["license_bucket"] = sample.get("license_bucket", "")
590	        data["bucket"] = int(sample.get("bucket", len(ctx["input_ids"])))
591	        data["n_prompt_tokens"] = len(ctx["input_ids"])
592	        data["mode"] = ctx["mode"]
593	        data["request_ignore_eos"] = bool(ctx["ignore_eos"])
594	        data["request_max_new_tokens"] = expected
595	        data["n_generated_tokens"] = n - 1
596	        prompt_last_doc = int((sample.get("document_ids") or [0])[-1])
597	        data["document_ids"] = torch.full((n,), prompt_last_doc, dtype=torch.int32)
598	
599	        body = ctx["body"]
600	        output_ids = [int(x) for x in body.get("output_ids") or []]
601	        saved_generated = n - 1
602	        if output_ids and saved_generated > 0:
603	            saved = data["token_ids"][1:].cpu()
604	            prefix = torch.tensor(output_ids[:saved_generated], dtype=torch.long)
605	            if not torch.equal(saved, prefix):
606	                return False, "generated_prefix_mismatch"
607	            data["completion_token_ids"] = torch.tensor(output_ids[:saved_generated], dtype=torch.long)
608	            data["completion_token_ids_with_extra"] = torch.tensor(output_ids, dtype=torch.long)
609	            data["generated_output_ids"] = data["completion_token_ids_with_extra"]
610	            if len(output_ids) > saved_generated:
611	                data["extra_decode_token_id"] = int(output_ids[saved_generated])
612	        data["completion_text"] = body.get("text", "")
613	        data["response"] = data["completion_text"]
614	        timings["validate_s"] = time.monotonic() - t0
615	
616	        t0 = time.monotonic()
617	        compressed = dict(data)
618	        compressed["format"] = nvfp4_codec.FORMAT_TAG
619	        if "token_ids" in compressed and torch.is_tensor(compressed["token_ids"]):
620	            compressed["token_ids"] = compressed["token_ids"].to(torch.int64).contiguous()
621	        if "top_logit_values" in compressed and torch.is_tensor(compressed["top_logit_values"]):
622	            compressed["top_logit_values"] = compressed["top_logit_values"].to(torch.bfloat16).contiguous()
623	        if "top_logit_indices" in compressed and torch.is_tensor(compressed["top_logit_indices"]):
624	            compressed["top_logit_indices"] = compressed["top_logit_indices"].to(torch.int32).contiguous()
625	        timings["compress_s"] = time.monotonic() - t0
626	
627	        t0 = time.monotonic()
628	        dst_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
629	        existed = dst_path.exists()
630	        tmp = dst_path.with_suffix(dst_path.suffix + ".tmp")
631	        torch.save(
632	            compressed,
633	            tmp,
634	            _use_new_zipfile_serialization=(args.save_format == "zip"),
635	        )
636	        tmp.replace(dst_path)
637	        timings["save_s"] = time.monotonic() - t0
638	        timings["total_s"] = time.monotonic() - total_t0
639	        return True, {
640	            "bytes": dst_path.stat().st_size,
641	            "tokens": n,
642	            "existed": existed,
643	            "timings": timings,
644	        }
645	    except Exception as e:
646	        return False, f"finalize_error:{type(e).__name__}:{e}"
647	    finally:
648	        hook_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
649	
650	
651	async def collect_batch(session, batch, start_idx, cur_seg_dir, cur_seg_count, cur_seg_idx,
652	                        args, compress_pool, uploader, state, pbar):
653	    assignments: dict[int, Path] = {}
654	    batch_segments: dict[int, Path] = {}
655	    for i, sample in enumerate(batch):
656	        idx = start_idx + i
657	        seg_idx, seg_dir = segment_dir_for_idx(args.stage_dir, idx, args.segment_size)
658	        seg_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
659	        batch_segments[seg_idx] = seg_dir
660	        dst = sample_output_path(seg_dir, idx, sample)
661	        assignments[idx] = dst
662	
663	    batch_t0 = time.monotonic()
664	    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
665	    tasks = [
666	        asyncio.create_task(send_one(session, sample, start_idx + i, args))
667	        for i, sample in enumerate(batch)
668	    ]
669	    futures: list[tuple[int, asyncio.Future]] = []
670	    failures = []
671	    timing_totals: dict[str, float] = {}
672	    request_done = 0
673	    done_bytes = 0
674	    done_tokens = 0
675	    done_files = 0
676	
677	    def add_timings(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
678	        for key, value in (payload.get("timings") or {}).items():
679	            timing_totals[key] = timing_totals.get(key, 0.0) + float(value)
680	
681	    def handle_finalize_result(idx: int, result: tuple[bool, Any]) -> None:
682	        nonlocal done_bytes, done_tokens, done_files
683	        ok, payload = result
684	        if not ok:
685	            failures.append((idx, payload))
686	            return
687	        if not payload.get("existed", False):
688	            done_files += 1
689	        done_bytes += int(payload["bytes"])
690	        done_tokens += int(payload["tokens"])
691	        add_timings(payload)
692	
693	    def drain_finished_futures() -> list[tuple[int, asyncio.Future]]:
694	        pending = []
695	        for idx, fut in futures:
696	            if fut.done():
697	                handle_finalize_result(idx, fut.result())
698	            else:
699	                pending.append((idx, fut))
700	        return pending
701	
702	    pending_tasks = set(tasks)
703	    while pending_tasks:
704	        done, pending_tasks = await asyncio.wait(
705	            pending_tasks, return_when=asyncio.FIRST_COMPLETED
706	        )
707	        for task in done:
708	            idx, ok, payload = task.result()
709	            request_done += 1
710	            pbar.update(1)
711	            if not ok:
712	                failures.append((idx, payload))
713	                continue
714	            dst = assignments[idx]
715	            fut = loop.run_in_executor(compress_pool, finalize_and_compress, payload, dst, args)
716	            futures.append((idx, fut))
717	        futures = drain_finished_futures()
718	
719	    request_s = time.monotonic() - batch_t0
720	    finalize_drain_t0 = time.monotonic()
721	    for idx, fut in futures:
722	        handle_finalize_result(idx, await fut)
723	    finalize_s = time.monotonic() - finalize_drain_t0
724	    total_s = time.monotonic() - batch_t0
725	    finalize_worker_s = timing_totals.get("total_s", 0.0)
726	    finalize_overlap_s = max(0.0, finalize_worker_s - finalize_s)
727	
728	    if failures:
729	        state.next_idx = min(idx for idx, _ in failures if idx >= 0)
730	    else:
731	        state.next_idx = start_idx + len(batch)
732	        for seg_idx, seg_dir in sorted(batch_segments.items()):
733	            seg_end = (seg_idx + 1) * args.segment_size
734	            if seg_end <= state.next_idx:
735	                n_files = len(list(seg_dir.glob("*.pt")))
736	                if n_files < args.segment_size:
737	                    failures.append((seg_idx * args.segment_size, f"segment_incomplete:{seg_idx}:{n_files}/{args.segment_size}"))
738	                    state.next_idx = min(state.next_idx, seg_idx * args.segment_size)
739	                    break
740	                uploader.submit(seg_dir, seg_idx)
741	    cur_seg_idx, cur_seg_dir = segment_dir_for_idx(args.stage_dir, state.next_idx, args.segment_size)
742	    cur_seg_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
743	    cur_seg_count = len(list(cur_seg_dir.glob("*.pt")))
744	    state.next_segment_idx = cur_seg_idx
745	    state.save()
746	    stats = {
747	        "type": "collect_batch",
748	        "batch_start": start_idx,
749	        "batch_size": len(batch),
750	        "ok": len(batch) - len(failures),
751	        "fail": len(failures),
752	        "new_files": done_files,
753	        "bytes": done_bytes,
754	        "tokens": done_tokens,
755	        "request_s": request_s,
756	        "finalize_s": finalize_s,
757	        "finalize_worker_s": finalize_worker_s,
758	        "finalize_overlap_s": finalize_overlap_s,
759	        "finalize_wait_s": timing_totals.get("wait_s", 0.0),
760	        "finalize_load_s": timing_totals.get("load_s", 0.0),
761	        "finalize_validate_s": timing_totals.get("validate_s", 0.0),
762	        "finalize_compress_s": timing_totals.get("compress_s", 0.0),
763	        "finalize_save_s": timing_totals.get("save_s", 0.0),
764	        "request_done": request_done,
765	        "total_s": total_s,
766	        "time": time.time(),
767	    }
768	    append_jsonl(args.metrics_file, stats)
769	    return cur_seg_dir, cur_seg_count, cur_seg_idx, failures, done_bytes, done_tokens, stats
770	
771	
772	async def collect_stream(session, prompts, start_idx, cur_seg_dir, cur_seg_count, cur_seg_idx,
773	                         args, compress_pool, uploader, state, pbar):
774	    stream_t0 = time.monotonic()
775	    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
776	    next_schedule_offset = 0
777	    next_commit_idx = start_idx
778	    completed: set[int] = set()
779	    request_tasks: dict[asyncio.Task, int] = {}
780	    finalize_futures: dict[asyncio.Future, int] = {}
781	    failures = []
782	    timing_totals: dict[str, float] = {}
783	    request_done = 0
784	    done_bytes = 0
785	    done_tokens = 0
786	    done_files = 0
787	    last_metric_done = 0
788	
789	    def add_timings(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
790	        for key, value in (payload.get("timings") or {}).items():
791	            timing_totals[key] = timing_totals.get(key, 0.0) + float(value)
792	
793	    def current_segment_state() -> tuple[Path, int, int]:
794	        seg_idx, seg_dir = segment_dir_for_idx(args.stage_dir, next_commit_idx, args.segment_size)
795	        seg_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
796	        return seg_dir, len(list(seg_dir.glob("*.pt"))), seg_idx
797	
798	    def commit_completed_prefix() -> None:
799	        nonlocal next_commit_idx, cur_seg_dir, cur_seg_count, cur_seg_idx
800	        advanced = False
801	        while next_commit_idx in completed:
802	            completed.remove(next_commit_idx)
803	            next_commit_idx += 1
804	            advanced = True
805	            if next_commit_idx % args.segment_size == 0:
806	                seg_idx = next_commit_idx // args.segment_size - 1
807	                seg_dir = args.stage_dir / f"seg_{seg_idx:06d}"
808	                n_files = len(list(seg_dir.glob("*.pt")))
809	                if n_files < args.segment_size:
810	                    failures.append((
811	                        seg_idx * args.segment_size,
812	                        f"segment_incomplete:{seg_idx}:{n_files}/{args.segment_size}",
813	                    ))
814	                    next_commit_idx = seg_idx * args.segment_size
815	                    break
816	                uploader.submit(seg_dir, seg_idx)
817	        if advanced:
818	            state.next_idx = next_commit_idx
819	            state.next_segment_idx = next_commit_idx // args.segment_size
820	            state.save()
821	            cur_seg_dir, cur_seg_count, cur_seg_idx = current_segment_state()
822	
823	    def handle_finalize_result(idx: int, result: tuple[bool, Any]) -> None:
824	        nonlocal done_bytes, done_tokens, done_files
825	        ok, payload = result
826	        if not ok:
827	            failures.append((idx, payload))
828	            return
829	        if not payload.get("existed", False):
830	            done_files += 1
831	        done_bytes += int(payload["bytes"])
832	        done_tokens += int(payload["tokens"])
833	        add_timings(payload)
834	        completed.add(idx)
835	        commit_completed_prefix()
836	
837	    def emit_metrics(final: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
838	        elapsed = max(time.monotonic() - stream_t0, 1e-6)
839	        finalize_worker_s = timing_totals.get("total_s", 0.0)
840	        stats = {
841	            "type": "collect_stream",
842	            "start": start_idx,
843	            "scheduled": next_schedule_offset,
844	            "committed_until": next_commit_idx,
845	            "ok": request_done - len(failures),
846	            "fail": len(failures),
847	            "new_files": done_files,
848	            "bytes": done_bytes,
849	            "tokens": done_tokens,
850	            "elapsed_s": elapsed,
851	            "collect_tps": done_tokens / elapsed,
852	            "final": bool(final),
853	            "request_done": request_done,
854	            "request_inflight": len(request_tasks),
855	            "finalize_inflight": len(finalize_futures),
856	            "finalize_worker_s": finalize_worker_s,
857	            "finalize_load_s": timing_totals.get("load_s", 0.0),
858	            "finalize_validate_s": timing_totals.get("validate_s", 0.0),
859	            "finalize_compress_s": timing_totals.get("compress_s", 0.0),
860	            "finalize_save_s": timing_totals.get("save_s", 0.0),
861	            "time": time.time(),
862	        }
863	        append_jsonl(args.metrics_file, stats)
864	        return stats
865	
866	    async def wait_for_capacity() -> None:
867	        while (
868	            disk_free_gb(args.stage_dir) < args.min_free_gb
869	            or uploader.pending() > args.upload_queue_limit
870	        ):
871	            uploader.raise_if_failed()
872	            pbar.set_postfix(
873	                pending=uploader.pending(),
874	                free=f"{disk_free_gb(args.stage_dir):.0f}G",
875	                status="PAUSE",
876	            )
877	            await asyncio.sleep(args.throttle_poll_sec)
878	
879	    async def schedule_more() -> None:
880	        nonlocal next_schedule_offset
881	        while (
882	            not failures
883	            and next_schedule_offset < len(prompts)
884	            and len(request_tasks) < args.batch_size
885	        ):
886	            if (
887	                disk_free_gb(args.stage_dir) < args.min_free_gb
888	                or uploader.pending() > args.upload_queue_limit
889	            ):
890	                if not request_tasks and not finalize_futures:
891	                    await wait_for_capacity()
892	                    continue
893	                break
894	            sample = prompts[next_schedule_offset]
895	            idx = start_idx + next_schedule_offset
896	            task = asyncio.create_task(send_one(session, sample, idx, args))
897	            request_tasks[task] = idx
898	            next_schedule_offset += 1
899	
900	    await schedule_more()
901	    while request_tasks or finalize_futures or (
902	        not failures and next_schedule_offset < len(prompts)
903	    ):
904	        uploader.raise_if_failed()
905	        if not request_tasks and not finalize_futures:
906	            await wait_for_capacity()
907	            await schedule_more()
908	            continue
909	
910	        wait_set = set(request_tasks) | set(finalize_futures)
911	        done, _ = await asyncio.wait(wait_set, return_when=asyncio.FIRST_COMPLETED)
912	        for fut in done:
913	            if fut in request_tasks:
914	                idx = request_tasks.pop(fut)
915	                request_done += 1
916	                pbar.update(1)
917	                ok = False
918	                payload: Any = None
919	                try:
920	                    result_idx, ok, payload = fut.result()
921	                    idx = result_idx
922	                except Exception as e:
923	                    payload = f"request_task_error:{type(e).__name__}:{e}"
924	                if not ok:
925	                    failures.append((idx, payload))
926	                    continue
927	                seg_idx, seg_dir = segment_dir_for_idx(args.stage_dir, idx, args.segment_size)
928	                seg_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
929	                dst = sample_output_path(seg_dir, idx, payload["sample"])
930	                finalize_futures[
931	                    loop.run_in_executor(compress_pool, finalize_and_compress, payload, dst, args)
932	                ] = idx
933	            else:
934	                idx = finalize_futures.pop(fut)
935	                handle_finalize_result(idx, fut.result())
936	
937	        if request_done - last_metric_done >= args.batch_size:
938	            emit_metrics(final=False)
939	            last_metric_done = request_done
940	        await schedule_more()
941	
942	    for fut, idx in list(finalize_futures.items()):
943	        handle_finalize_result(idx, await fut)
944	    commit_completed_prefix()
945	    if failures and state.next_idx > min(idx for idx, _ in failures if idx >= 0):
946	        state.next_idx = min(idx for idx, _ in failures if idx >= 0)
947	        state.next_segment_idx = state.next_idx // args.segment_size
948	        state.save()
949	    cur_seg_dir, cur_seg_count, cur_seg_idx = current_segment_state()
950	    stats = emit_metrics(final=True)
951	    return cur_seg_dir, cur_seg_count, cur_seg_idx, failures, done_bytes, done_tokens, stats
952	
953	
954	async def main_async(args) -> None:
955	    if args.metrics_file is None:
956	        args.metrics_file = args.state_file.parent / "metrics.jsonl"
957	    if args.failure_manifest is None:
958	        args.failure_manifest = args.state_file.parent / "failures.jsonl"
959	    args.collect_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
960	    args.stage_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
961	    args.failure_manifest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
962	    args.failure_manifest.touch(exist_ok=True)
963	    for stale in args.collect_dir.glob("*.pt"):
964	        stale.unlink()
965	
966	    state = State(args.state_file)
967	    start = max(args.start, state.next_idx)
968	    prompts = load_jsonl(args.in_path, start=start, limit=args.limit)
969	    if not prompts:
970	        raise SystemExit(f"no prompts loaded from {args.in_path} at start={start}")
971	
972	    print(json.dumps({
973	        "prompts": len(prompts),
974	        "start": start,
975	        "stage_dir": str(args.stage_dir),
976	        "collect_dir": str(args.collect_dir),
977	        "bos_prefix": args.bos_prefix,
978	        "upload_method": args.upload_method,
979	        "rid_prefix": args.rid_prefix,
980	        "generate_tokens": args.generate_tokens,

1023	    cur_seg_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
1024	    cur_seg_count = len(list(cur_seg_dir.glob("*.pt")))
1025	    done_bytes = 0

1032	    connector = aiohttp.TCPConnector(limit=args.concurrency * 2, force_close=True)
1033	    pbar = tqdm(

1037	        position=0,

1044	                session, prompts, start, cur_seg_dir, cur_seg_count, cur_seg_idx,
1045	                args, compress_pool, uploader, state, pbar,
1046	            )
1047	            failures.extend(stream_failures)
1048	            done_bytes += b_bytes

1050	            pbar.set_postfix(

1052	                pending=uploader.pending(),

1054	                tok=done_tokens,
1055	            )

1059	                    append_jsonl(args.failure_manifest, jsonable_failure(idx, reason, sample))

1068	        raise

1070	        pbar.close()

1072	        wait_error = None

1075	                uploader.submit(cur_seg_dir, cur_seg_idx)

1090	    upload_mb_s = (

1092	        if uploader.uploaded_bytes_this_run else 0.0
1093	    )

1100	        "uploaded_this_run_gb": round(uploader.uploaded_bytes_this_run / 1024**3, 2),
1101	        "upload_mb_s": round(upload_mb_s, 2),

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""NVFP4 aux_hidden storage codec for EAGLE target-regenerated data.
3	
4	The collector receives bf16 ``aux_hidden`` from the target hook.  For large
5	target-regenerated runs this tensor dominates disk usage, so final training
6	files store it as packed FP4 E2M1 values plus bf16 per-16-channel scales.  The
7	trainer decodes it back to bf16 on load and keeps the model forward unchanged.
8	"""
9	from __future__ import annotations
10	
11	from typing import Any
12	
13	import torch
14	
15	
16	FP4_GROUP_SIZE = 16
17	FP4_MAX = 6.0
18	FORMAT_TAG = "nvfp4_aux_v1"
19	LEGACY_FORMAT_TAG = "nvfp4_v1"
20	
21	_FP4_POS = torch.tensor(
22	    [0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0, 6.0], dtype=torch.float32
23	)
24	_FP4_BOUNDS = torch.tensor(
25	    [0.25, 0.75, 1.25, 1.75, 2.5, 3.5, 5.0], dtype=torch.float32
26	)
27	
28	
29	def is_nvfp4_sample(d: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
30	    return (
31	        d.get("format") in {FORMAT_TAG, LEGACY_FORMAT_TAG}
32	        or ("aux_packed" in d and "aux_scale" in d and "aux_hidden" not in d)
33	    )
34	
35	
36	def encode(x: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
37	    """Encode ``(..., D)`` bf16-like tensor into packed NVFP4.
38	
39	    Returns:
40	        packed: ``(..., D // 2)`` uint8, two FP4 codes per byte.
41	        scale: ``(..., D // 16)`` bf16, per-group scale.
42	    """
43	    if x.shape[-1] % FP4_GROUP_SIZE != 0:
44	        raise ValueError(
45	            f"last dim {x.shape[-1]} not divisible by {FP4_GROUP_SIZE}"
46	        )
47	    if x.shape[-1] % 2 != 0:
48	        raise ValueError("last dim must be even for byte packing")
49	    if x.dtype != torch.bfloat16:
50	        x = x.to(torch.bfloat16)
51	
52	    *prefix, dim = x.shape
53	    n_groups = dim // FP4_GROUP_SIZE
54	    grouped = x.contiguous().reshape(*prefix, n_groups, FP4_GROUP_SIZE)
55	    scale = (
56	        grouped.abs().amax(-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-8) / FP4_MAX
57	    ).to(torch.bfloat16)
58	    q = grouped / scale
59	
60	    bounds = _FP4_BOUNDS.to(device=q.device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)
61	    abs_idx = torch.bucketize(
62	        q.abs().clamp(max=FP4_MAX), bounds, out_int32=True
63	    ).to(torch.uint8)
64	    # Canonicalize FP4 zero: code 8 decodes to the same numeric zero as code 0,
65	    # but avoiding negative-zero codes makes packed validation deterministic.
66	    sign_bit = ((q < 0) & (abs_idx != 0)).to(torch.uint8).mul_(8)
67	    codes = (abs_idx | sign_bit).reshape(*prefix, dim)
68	    pair = codes.reshape(*prefix, dim // 2, 2)
69	    packed = (pair[..., 0] | (pair[..., 1] << 4)).to(torch.uint8)
70	    return packed.contiguous(), scale.squeeze(-1).contiguous()
71	
72	
73	def decode(
74	    packed: torch.Tensor,
75	    scale: torch.Tensor,
76	    out_dtype: torch.dtype = torch.bfloat16,
77	) -> torch.Tensor:
78	    """Decode packed NVFP4 tensors back to dense bf16 by default."""
79	    if packed.dtype != torch.uint8:
80	        raise TypeError(f"packed must be uint8, got {packed.dtype}")
81	    dim = packed.shape[-1] * 2
82	    low = (packed & 0x0F).to(torch.long)
83	    high = ((packed >> 4) & 0x0F).to(torch.long)
84	    codes = torch.stack([low, high], dim=-1).reshape(*packed.shape[:-1], dim)
85	
86	    pos = _FP4_POS.to(device=codes.device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)
87	    sign = torch.where(
88	        (codes & 8) != 0,
89	        torch.tensor(-1.0, dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=codes.device),
90	        torch.tensor(1.0, dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=codes.device),
91	    )
92	    fp4_vals = pos[codes & 7] * sign
93	
94	    n_groups = dim // FP4_GROUP_SIZE
95	    fp4_vals = fp4_vals.reshape(*fp4_vals.shape[:-1], n_groups, FP4_GROUP_SIZE)
96	    out = (fp4_vals * scale.unsqueeze(-1)).reshape(*packed.shape[:-1], dim)
97	    return out.to(out_dtype)
98	
99	
100	def compress_sample(d: dict[str, Any], *, keep_aux_hidden: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
101	    """Return a copy of ``d`` with ``aux_hidden`` stored as NVFP4 payload.
102	
103	    Unlike the legacy v2/v3 codec, this preserves all current target_regen
104	    metadata fields such as ``assistant_mask``, ``document_ids`` and
105	    ``target_logsumexp``.
106	    """
107	    if "aux_hidden" not in d:
108	        if is_nvfp4_sample(d):
109	            return d
110	        raise KeyError("sample has neither aux_hidden nor aux_packed/aux_scale")
111	
112	    packed, scale = encode(d["aux_hidden"])
113	    out = dict(d)
114	    if not keep_aux_hidden:
115	        out.pop("aux_hidden", None)
116	    out["aux_packed"] = packed
117	    out["aux_scale"] = scale
118	    out["aux_hidden_shape"] = tuple(d["aux_hidden"].shape)
119	    out["format"] = FORMAT_TAG
120	
121	    if "token_ids" in out and torch.is_tensor(out["token_ids"]):
122	        out["token_ids"] = out["token_ids"].to(torch.int64).contiguous()
123	    if "top_logit_values" in out and torch.is_tensor(out["top_logit_values"]):
124	        out["top_logit_values"] = out["top_logit_values"].to(torch.bfloat16).contiguous()
125	    if "top_logit_indices" in out and torch.is_tensor(out["top_logit_indices"]):
126	        out["top_logit_indices"] = out["top_logit_indices"].to(torch.int32).contiguous()
127	    return out
128	
129	
130	def decompress_sample(
131	    d: dict[str, Any],
132	    *,
133	    keep_packed: bool = False,
134	    out_dtype: torch.dtype = torch.bfloat16,
135	) -> dict[str, Any]:
136	    """Return a copy of an NVFP4 sample with dense ``aux_hidden`` restored."""
137	    if not is_nvfp4_sample(d):
138	        return d
139	    out = dict(d)
140	    out["aux_hidden"] = decode(out["aux_packed"], out["aux_scale"], out_dtype=out_dtype)
141	    if not keep_packed:
142	        out.pop("aux_packed", None)
143	        out.pop("aux_scale", None)
144	    return out
145	
146	
147	def _in_memory_roundtrip(x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
148	    *prefix, dim = x.shape
149	    grouped = x.reshape(*prefix, dim // FP4_GROUP_SIZE, FP4_GROUP_SIZE)
150	    scale = grouped.abs().amax(-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-8) / FP4_MAX
151	    scale_bf16 = scale.to(torch.bfloat16).to(scale.dtype)
152	    bounds = _FP4_BOUNDS.to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)
153	    pos = _FP4_POS.to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)
154	    q = grouped / scale_bf16
155	    sign = q.sign()
156	    idx = torch.bucketize(q.abs().clamp(max=FP4_MAX), bounds)
157	    return (sign * pos[idx] * scale_bf16).to(x.dtype).reshape(*prefix, dim)
158	
159	
160	def _test_roundtrip() -> None:
161	    torch.manual_seed(0)
162	    shapes = [(2048, 12288), (128, 4096), (7, 3, 64), (32, 16)]
163	    for shape in shapes:
164	        x = torch.randn(*shape, dtype=torch.bfloat16) * 3.0
165	        packed, scale = encode(x)
166	        decoded = decode(packed, scale)
167	        ref = _in_memory_roundtrip(x)
168	        equal = torch.equal(decoded, ref)
169	        print(
170	            f"shape={shape} packed={tuple(packed.shape)} scale={tuple(scale.shape)} "
171	            f"bit_exact={equal}"
172	        )
173	        if not equal:
174	            raise AssertionError(f"roundtrip mismatch for {shape}")
175	
176	
177	def _test_disk(tmp_path: str = "/tmp/eagle_nvfp4_codec_test.pt") -> None:
178	    import os
179	
180	    raw = {
181	        "token_ids": torch.randint(0, 73000, (128,), dtype=torch.int64),
182	        "aux_hidden": torch.randn(128, 12288, dtype=torch.bfloat16),
183	        "top_logit_values": torch.randn(128, 128, dtype=torch.bfloat16),
184	        "top_logit_indices": torch.randint(0, 73000, (128, 128), dtype=torch.int32),
185	        "target_logsumexp": torch.randn(128, dtype=torch.float32),
186	        "assistant_mask": torch.ones(128, dtype=torch.bool),
187	        "document_ids": torch.zeros(128, dtype=torch.int32),
188	    }
189	    compressed = compress_sample(raw)
190	    torch.save(compressed, tmp_path)
191	    loaded = torch.load(tmp_path, weights_only=True, map_location="cpu")
192	    decoded = decompress_sample(loaded)
193	    ref = _in_memory_roundtrip(raw["aux_hidden"])
194	    if not torch.equal(decoded["aux_hidden"], ref):
195	        raise AssertionError("disk roundtrip mismatch")
196	    print(f"disk_roundtrip_ok size_mb={os.path.getsize(tmp_path) / 1024 / 1024:.2f}")
197	    os.unlink(tmp_path)
198	
199	
200	if __name__ == "__main__":
201	    _test_roundtrip()
202	    _test_disk()
203	    print("All NVFP4 codec tests passed")
204

1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	set -euo pipefail
3	
4	cd /user_4813494d/openbmb
5	
6	# EAGLE target-regenerated collection server.
7	# The model hook captures:
8	#   prompt-last row from EXTEND, then generated-token rows from DECODE.
9	# CUDA graph stays enabled so collection follows the production decode path.
10	# Defaults below are the formal collection settings.
11	
12	TARGET_MODEL="${EAGLE_TARGET_MODEL:-/user_4813494d/models/openbmb/MiniCPM-SALA-gptq46-calib90-90k-NVFP4-det}"

15	mkdir -p "${HOOK_DIR}"
16	
17	EAGLE3_ONESTAGE_DIR="${HOOK_DIR}" \
18	EAGLE3_ONESTAGE_RID_PREFIX="${EAGLE3_ONESTAGE_RID_PREFIX:-e3os}" \
19	EAGLE3_AUX_LAYERS="${EAGLE3_AUX_LAYERS:-1,10,22}" \
20	EAGLE3_MAX_TOKENS="${EAGLE3_MAX_TOKENS:-4096}" \
21	EAGLE3_TOP_K="${EAGLE3_TOP_K:-128}" \
22	EAGLE3_ONESTAGE_NVFP4="${EAGLE3_ONESTAGE_NVFP4:-1}" \
23	EAGLE3_ONESTAGE_SAVE_WORKERS="${EAGLE3_ONESTAGE_SAVE_WORKERS:-4}" \
24	SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD="${SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD:-48}" \
25	SGLANG_MINICPM_PLAN_CACHE="${SGLANG_MINICPM_PLAN_CACHE:-1}" \
26	SGLANG_FP4_TUNE_CACHE="${SGLANG_FP4_TUNE_CACHE:-/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/assets/mm_fp4_tune_sm120.json}" \
27	CUTE_DSL_ARCH="${CUTE_DSL_ARCH:-sm_120a}" \
28	CUTE_DSL_CACHE_DIR="${CUTE_DSL_CACHE_DIR:-/tmp/cute_dsl_cache}" \
29	SGLANG_ENABLE_B12X="${SGLANG_ENABLE_B12X:-0}" \

32	python3 -m sglang.launch_server \
