95	# ── STE round (legacy fake-quant path) ────────────────────────────────────
96	
97	def _fp4_round(x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
98	    """Round each element to the nearest FP4 E2M1 representable value.
99	
100	    Input: any float tensor (preferably already in [-6, 6]).
101	    Output: same dtype, values in {0, ±0.5, ±1, ±1.5, ±2, ±3, ±4, ±6}.
102	    """
103	    pos = _FP4_POS.to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)
104	    bounds = _FP4_BOUNDS.to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)
105	    sign = x.sign()
106	    idx = torch.bucketize(x.abs().clamp(max=6.0), bounds)
107	    return sign * pos[idx]
108	
109	
110	class _FP4QuantSTE(torch.autograd.Function):
111	    """Fake-quantize weight to FP4 E2M1 in forward; straight-through in backward.
112	
113	    Per-group-of-16 scaling: ``scale = max(|w_group|) / 6.0``. Stays in the
114	    weight's native dtype (bf16); only the per-group scale (1 value per 16
115	    weights) goes through fp32 for clamp stability.
116	    """
117	    @staticmethod
118	    def forward(ctx, weight: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
119	        N, K = weight.shape
120	        w_g = weight.reshape(N, K // FP4_GROUP_SIZE, FP4_GROUP_SIZE)
121	        abs_max = w_g.abs().amax(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
122	        scale = (abs_max.float().clamp(min=1e-8) / 6.0).to(weight.dtype)
123	        w_q = _fp4_round(w_g / scale) * scale
124	        return w_q.reshape(N, K)
125	
126	    @staticmethod
127	    def backward(ctx, grad: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
128	        return grad  # STE
129	
130	
131	# ── True 4-bit forward path (NVFP4 cutlass) ───────────────────────────────
132	
133	def _compute_global_scale(t: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
134	    """Per-tensor input_global_scale matching the NVFP4 reference."""
135	    amax = t.detach().abs().amax().to(torch.float32).clamp(min=1e-6)
136	    return (_FP4_E4M3_E2M1_PRODUCT / amax).view(1)
137	
138	
139	class _NVFP4LinearFn(torch.autograd.Function):
140	    """Forward: NVFP4 GEMM (true 4-bit). Backward: bf16 STE.
141	
142	    NVFP4 official scale convention applied so input_global_scale * input fits
143	    the joint FP4*E4M3 dynamic range. ``alpha = 1 / (gs_x * gs_w)`` descales
144	    the output back to the original magnitude. Without this, weights with
145	    small amax (e.g. ~0.01 after init_mlp_from_target) trigger e4m3 underflow
146	    and the GEMM produces NaN on entire blocks.
147	
148	    STE backward: forward uses the FP4-quantized weight, but the gradient
149	    w.r.t. the master bf16 weight is the standard linear gradient
150	    (grad_w = grad_out.T @ x), identical to the legacy fake-quant STE.
151	    """
152	    @staticmethod
153	    def forward(ctx, x, weight_bf16, wq, wsf, w_gs):
154	        _maybe_load_nvfp4_autotune()
155	        x2d = x.reshape(-1, x.shape[-1]).contiguous()
156	        x_gs = _compute_global_scale(x2d)
157	        xq, xsf = _sgl_scaled_fp4_quant(x2d, x_gs)
158	        alpha = (1.0 / (x_gs * w_gs)).contiguous()
159	        out2d = _fi_mm_fp4(
160	            xq, wq.T, xsf, wsf.T, alpha,
161	            out_dtype=torch.bfloat16,
162	            block_size=16, use_nvfp4=True, backend="cutlass",
163	        )
164	        out_shape = list(x.shape[:-1]) + [out2d.shape[-1]]
165	        ctx.save_for_backward(x, weight_bf16)
166	        return out2d.view(out_shape)
167	
168	    @staticmethod
169	    def backward(ctx, grad_out):
170	        x, w = ctx.saved_tensors
171	        grad_out_2d = grad_out.reshape(-1, grad_out.shape[-1])
172	        x_2d = x.reshape(-1, x.shape[-1])
173	        grad_x = (grad_out_2d @ w).view_as(x)
174	        grad_w = grad_out_2d.t() @ x_2d
175	        return grad_x, grad_w, None, None, None
176	
177	
178	# ── Linear wrapper ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
179	
180	class FP4QATLinear(nn.Linear):
181	    """nn.Linear with FP4 fake-quantization on weights during training.
182	
183	    Eval / FP4_QAT=False / ``_fp4_skip=True``: plain bf16 ``F.linear`` so the
184	    final RTN export matches inference exactly.
185	    """
186	    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
187	        if not (self.training and FP4_QAT):
188	            return F.linear(x, self.weight, self.bias)
189	        if getattr(self, "_fp4_skip", False):
190	            return F.linear(x, self.weight, self.bias)
191	
192	        if NVFP4_FORWARD:
193	            wq = getattr(self, "_fp4_qw_packed", None)
194	            wsf = getattr(self, "_fp4_qw_sf", None)
195	            w_gs = getattr(self, "_fp4_w_gs", None)
196	            if wq is None:
197	                w_gs = _compute_global_scale(self.weight)
198	                wq, wsf = _sgl_scaled_fp4_quant(self.weight, w_gs)
199	            out = _NVFP4LinearFn.apply(x, self.weight, wq, wsf, w_gs)
200	            return out if self.bias is None else (out + self.bias)
201	
202	        # Legacy fake-STE (bf16 GEMM, FP4-rounded weight).
203	        qw = getattr(self, "_fp4_qw", None)
204	        if qw is None:
205	            qw = _FP4QuantSTE.apply(self.weight)
206	        return F.linear(x, qw, self.bias)
207	
208	
209	def fp4_quant_freeze(model: nn.Module) -> None:
210	    """Pre-quantize every FP4QATLinear weight so multi-step forwards reuse it.
211	
212	    NVFP4_FORWARD=1: caches packed (wq, wsf, w_gs) — weight quantize cost is
213	    paid once per optimizer step instead of TTT_STEPS times.
214	    NVFP4_FORWARD=0: caches fake-STE quantized weight (bf16 tensor).
215	    """
216	    if not FP4_QAT:
217	        return
218	    for m in model.modules():
219	        if isinstance(m, FP4QATLinear) and m.training:
220	            if getattr(m, "_fp4_skip", False):
221	                continue
222	            if NVFP4_FORWARD:
223	                with torch.no_grad():
224	                    w_gs = _compute_global_scale(m.weight)
225	                    wq, wsf = _sgl_scaled_fp4_quant(m.weight, w_gs)
226	                m._fp4_qw_packed = wq
227	                m._fp4_qw_sf = wsf
228	                m._fp4_w_gs = w_gs
229	            else:
230	                m._fp4_qw = _FP4QuantSTE.apply(m.weight)
231	
232	
233	def fp4_quant_unfreeze(model: nn.Module) -> None:
234	    """Clear the per-step quantized-weight cache after backward."""
235	    for m in model.modules():
236	        if isinstance(m, FP4QATLinear):
237	            m._fp4_qw = None
238	            m._fp4_qw_packed = None
239	            m._fp4_qw_sf = None

  "content": "\"\"\"Config schema: dataclass + yaml load + validation + dump.\n\nA config is the single source of truth for a run. All cli entries take exactly\none flag (--config PATH); everything else flows through the yaml.\n\nOn run start the effective config is dumped to ``<output_dir>/effective.yaml``\ntogether with the resolved git sha so a saved run is fully reproducible.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport dataclasses\nimport json\nimport os\nimport subprocess\nimport time\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport yaml\n\n\n# ── Sub-configs ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass DataCfg:\n    \"\"\"Where training / eval data lives on the node-local FS.\n\n    All three dirs MUST exist before training starts. ``train_dir`` may be a\n    BOS-synced user_4813494d (pre_sync_bos.py) holding seg_*/*.pt; the loader globs\n    recursively. ``val_ind_dir`` and ``val_ood_dir`` may be empty (set to \"\"\n    to skip the corresponding eval pass).\n    \"\"\"\n    train_dir: str\n    val_ind_dir: str = \"\"\n    val_ood_dir: str = \"\"\n    vocab_cache: str = \"\"\n    # When >0, sample at most this many files from train_dir (after shuffle).\n    # overfit uses this to fix the support to N samples.\n    train_max_files: int = 0\n    # Per-pass eval caps (number of files).\n    eval_max_ind: int = 200\n    eval_max_ood: int = 64\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass TargetCfg:\n    \"\"\"Where the target embed/lm_head live.\n\n    Prefer the artifacts file extracted by tools/extract_target_artifacts.py\n    (small, 1.2 GB safetensors with two tensors). Fall back to the full target\n    model dir for local dev convenience.\n    \"\"\"\n    # safetensors with keys {model.embed_tokens.weight, lm_head.weight}\n    artifacts_file: str = \"\"\n    # Or full target model dir; uses safetensors.index.json layout.\n    model_dir: str = \"\"\n    # MLP/o_proj warm-start source (BF16 target). Empty -> skip warm-start.\n    bf16_model_dir: str = \"\"\n    init_layer: int = 16\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ModelCfg:\n    \"\"\"Architecture / training knobs that map onto eagle env vars.\n\n    These must be injected into os.environ BEFORE any ``eagle.*`` module is\n    imported because the trainer reads them at module top level.\n    \"\"\"\n    ttt_steps: int = 3\n    seq_len_max: int = 4096\n    pack: bool = False\n    pack_min_seg: int = 64\n    # Force bf16 fake-STE path: required on H100 (no sm_120 NVFP4 tensor cores).\n    # Set False locally only to sanity-check the sm_120 NVFP4 path.\n    nvfp4_forward: bool = False\n    exclude_lm_head_from_fp4: bool = True\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass OptimCfg:\n    \"\"\"Per-rank batch + optimizer + LR schedule.\n\n    effective_batch = micro_batch * grad_accum * world_size.\n    \"\"\"\n    micro_batch: int = 4\n    grad_accum: int = 4\n    lr: float = 5e-4\n    warmup_ratio: float = 0.05\n    warmup_min_steps: int = 50\n    lr_min_factor: float = 0.05\n    weight_decay: float = 0.01\n    beta1: float = 0.9\n    beta2: float = 0.95\n    max_grad_norm: float = 1.0\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass TrainCfg:\n    \"\"\"Loop control. ``steps`` is optimizer steps, not microbatches.\"\"\"\n    steps: int = 5000\n    eval_every: int = 250\n    log_every: int = 1\n    ckpt_every: int = 0  # 0 = ckpt only on eval-improvement and final\n    seed: int = 42\n    # Free CUDA cache every N steps; 0 disables.\n    empty_cache_every: int = 25\n    # Resume from this dir if non-empty.\n    resume_from: str = \"\"\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass RuntimeCfg:\n    \"\"\"Output, logging, prefetch tuning.\"\"\"\n    output_user_4813494d: str = \"runs\"\n    run_name: str = \"\"  # empty -> auto from config name + timestamp\n    prefetch_queue: int = 2\n    dataloader_workers: int = 4\n    # When True, evaluator is allowed to skip ind/ood that are missing on disk.\n    eval_ok_if_missing: bool = True\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ProbeCfg:\n    \"\"\"Knobs only consumed by batch_probe / profile entries.\"\"\"\n    # batch_probe: sweep micro_batch in this list (or auto-binary-search if []).\n    sweep_micro_batches: list[int] = field(default_factory=list)\n    # profile: number of warmup + active steps.\n    profile_warmup: int = 3\n    profile_active: int = 10\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass Config:\n    data: DataCfg\n    target: TargetCfg\n    model: ModelCfg = field(default_factory=ModelCfg)\n    optim: OptimCfg = field(default_factory=OptimCfg)\n    train: TrainCfg = field(default_factory=TrainCfg)\n    runtime: RuntimeCfg = field(default_factory=RuntimeCfg)\n    probe: ProbeCfg = field(default_factory=ProbeCfg)\n\n\n# ── Load / dump ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef _instantiate(cls, data: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:\n    \"\"\"Recursively build a dataclass tree from a dict, rejecting unknown keys.\n\n    Defensive against typos: a misspelled field in yaml fails loudly instead\n    of being silently ignored.\n    \"\"\"\n    field_types = {f.name: f.type for f in dataclasses.fields(cls)}\n    extra = set(data) - set(field_types)\n    if extra:\n        raise ValueError(f\"{cls.__name__}: unknown config keys: {sorted(extra)}\")\n    kwargs = {}\n    for name, ftype in field_types.items():\n        if name not in data:\n            continue\n        value = data[name]\n        if dataclasses.is_dataclass(ftype) and isinstance(value, dict):\n            kwargs[name] = _instantiate(ftype, value)\n        else:\n            kwargs[name] = value\n    return cls(**kwargs)\n\n\ndef load_config(path: str | Path) -> Config:\n    \"\"\"Load and validate a yaml config.\"\"\"\n    path = Path(path)\n    if not path.exists():\n        raise FileNotFoundError(f\"config not found: {path}\")\n    raw = yaml.safe_load(path.read_text())\n    if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n        raise ValueError(f\"{path}: top-level yaml must be a mapping\")\n    cfg = _instantiate(Config, raw)\n    _validate(cfg)\n    return cfg\n\n\ndef _validate(cfg: Config) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Reject ill-formed configs early — easier to debug than a 30-min crash.\"\"\"\n    if cfg.optim.micro_batch <= 0 or cfg.optim.grad_accum <= 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"optim.micro_batch and optim.grad_accum must be > 0\")\n    if cfg.train.steps <= 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"train.steps must be > 0\")\n    if cfg.model.ttt_steps <= 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"model.ttt_steps must be > 0\")\n    if cfg.model.seq_len_max <= 1:\n        raise ValueError(\"model.seq_len_max must be > 1\")\n    if not (0.0 <= cfg.optim.warmup_ratio <= 1.0):\n        raise ValueError(\"optim.warmup_ratio must be in [0, 1]\")\n    if not (0.0 <= cfg.optim.lr_min_factor <= 1.0):\n        raise ValueError(\"optim.lr_min_factor must be in [0, 1]\")\n    if not cfg.target.artifacts_file and not cfg.target.model_dir:\n        raise ValueError(\"target.artifacts_file or target.model_dir must be set\")\n\n\ndef to_dict(cfg: Config) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    return dataclasses.asdict(cfg)\n\n\ndef dump_effective(cfg: Config, output_dir: Path) -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Write the resolved config + git sha + timestamp to <output_dir>.\n\n    Goes through json then yaml so the order is deterministic and diffable.\n    \"\"\"\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    payload = {\n        \"config\": to_dict(cfg),\n        \"git_sha\": _git_sha(),\n        \"git_dirty\": _git_dirty(),\n        \"timestamp\": time.time(),\n        \"host\": os.uname().nodename,\n    }\n    path = output_dir / \"effective.yaml\"\n    path.write_text(yaml.safe_dump(json.loads(json.dumps(payload)), sort_keys=True, allow_unicode=True))\n    return path\n\n\ndef _git_sha() -> str:\n    try:\n        return subprocess.check_output(\n            [\"git\", \"rev-parse\", \"HEAD\"], stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,\n        ).decode().strip()\n    except Exception:\n        return \"unknown\"\n\n\ndef _git_dirty() -> bool:\n    try:\n        out = subprocess.check_output(\n            [\"git\", \"status\", \"--porcelain\"], stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,\n        ).decode().strip()\n        return bool(out)\n    except Exception:\n        return False\n\n\ndef inject_model_env(cfg: Config) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Set eagle env vars so trainer module-level constants pick them up.\n\n    MUST be called before any ``eagle.*`` import. Idempotent.\n    \"\"\"\n    os.environ[\"EAGLE_DRAFT_TTT_STEPS\"] = str(cfg.model.ttt_steps)\n    os.environ[\"EAGLE_DRAFT_SEQ_LEN_MAX\"] = str(cfg.model.seq_len_max)\n    os.environ[\"EAGLE_DRAFT_PACK\"] = \"1\" if cfg.model.pack else \"0\"\n    os.environ[\"EAGLE_DRAFT_PACK_MIN_SEG\"] = str(cfg.model.pack_min_seg)\n    # Keep batch / grad_accum out of the env — scale owns those, not the trainer.\n    os.environ[\"EAGLE_NVFP4_FORWARD\"] = \"1\" if cfg.model.nvfp4_forward else \"0\"\n    os.environ[\"EAGLE_NVFP4_EXCLUDE_LM_HEAD\"] = (\n        \"1\" if cfg.model.exclude_lm_head_from_fp4 else \"0\"\n    )\n\n\ndef resolve_run_dir(cfg: Config, config_path: Path) -> Path:\n    \"\"\"``<output_user_4813494d>/<run_name>__<git-sha-short>__<timestamp>``.\"\"\"\n    if cfg.runtime.run_name:\n        name = cfg.runtime.run_name\n    else:\n        name = config_path.stem\n    sha = _git_sha()[:8]\n    ts = time.strftime(\"%Y%m%d-%H%M%S\")\n    return Path(cfg.runtime.output_user_4813494d) / f\"{name}__{sha}__{ts}\"\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/scale/core/dist.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"DDP init + small ergonomic helpers.\n\ntorchrun is the only supported launcher. Single-GPU runs use\n``torchrun --nproc_per_node=1`` so the DDP code path is exercised even in\nlocal dev — there is no separate non-DDP branch to maintain.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport contextlib\nimport datetime\nimport os\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport torch\nimport torch.distributed as dist\n\n\n# ── Init / teardown ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef init() -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"Initialize the process group from torchrun-set env vars.\n\n    Returns a small dict so the caller doesn't have to plumb world_size\n    everywhere. Re-entry is safe (returns the current state).\n    \"\"\"\n    if dist.is_available() and dist.is_initialized():\n        return state()\n\n    world_size = int(os.environ.get(\"WORLD_SIZE\", \"1\"))\n    rank = int(os.environ.get(\"RANK\", \"0\"))\n    local_rank = int(os.environ.get(\"LOCAL_RANK\", \"0\"))\n\n    if not torch.cuda.is_available():\n        raise RuntimeError(\"scale requires CUDA; no GPU detected\")\n    torch.cuda.set_device(local_rank)\n\n    if world_size > 1 or \"MASTER_ADDR\" in os.environ:\n        # Long timeout: H100 first-launch can stall on driver init.\n        dist.init_process_group(\n            backend=\"nccl\",\n            timeout=datetime.timedelta(minutes=30),\n            world_size=world_size,\n            rank=rank,\n        )\n    return {\"rank\": rank, \"local_rank\": local_rank, \"world_size\": world_size}\n\n\ndef shutdown() -> None:\n    if dist.is_available() and dist.is_initialized():\n        dist.destroy_process_group()\n\n\ndef state() -> dict[str, int]:\n    if dist.is_available() and dist.is_initialized():\n        return {\n            \"rank\": dist.get_rank(),\n            \"world_size\": dist.get_world_size(),\n            \"local_rank\": int(os.environ.get(\"LOCAL_RANK\", \"0\")),\n        }\n    return {\"rank\": 0, \"world_size\": 1, \"local_rank\": 0}\n\n\ndef is_rank0() -> bool:\n    return state()[\"rank\"] == 0\n\n\ndef barrier() -> None:\n    if dist.is_available() and dist.is_initialized():\n        dist.barrier()\n\n\n# ── Reductions ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef reduce_mean(value: float | torch.Tensor, device: torch.device) -> float:\n    \"\"\"All-reduce a scalar across ranks and return the mean as float.\n\n    Safe under world_size=1 (returns the value as-is). Used for global loss\n    + acc logging so jsonl numbers reflect the true cross-rank average rather\n    than rank0's local slice.\n    \"\"\"\n    s = state()\n    if s[\"world_size\"] == 1:\n        if torch.is_tensor(value):\n            return float(value.detach().cpu().item())\n        return float(value)\n    t = value.detach().to(device) if torch.is_tensor(value) else torch.tensor(value, device=device)\n    t = t.float()\n    dist.all_reduce(t, op=dist.ReduceOp.AVG)\n    return float(t.item())\n\n\ndef reduce_sum(value: float | torch.Tensor, device: torch.device) -> float:\n    s = state()\n    if s[\"world_size\"] == 1:\n        if torch.is_tensor(value):\n            return float(value.detach().cpu().item())\n        return float(value)\n    t = value.detach().to(device) if torch.is_tensor(value) else torch.tensor(value, device=device)\n    t = t.float()\n    dist.all_reduce(t, op=dist.ReduceOp.SUM)\n    return float(t.item())\n\n\n# ── Gradient accumulation ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\n@contextlib.contextmanager\ndef no_sync(model: Any):\n    \"\"\"``model.no_sync()`` if available, else a no-op.\n\n    Used inside the GA inner loop to skip allreduce on every microbatch except\n    the last. Saves ~(GA-1)/GA of the per-step DDP communication cost.\n\n    Plain DistributedDataParallel exposes no_sync; nn.Module does not. The\n    fallback makes single-GPU runs (where model isn't DDP-wrapped) work too.\n    \"\"\"\n    fn = getattr(model, \"no_sync\", None)\n    if fn is None:\n        yield\n        return\n    with fn():\n        yield\n\n\n# ── rank0-only helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\n@contextlib.contextmanager\ndef rank0_first():\n    \"\"\"Block all non-zero ranks until rank0 exits the context.\n\n    Useful for one-time setup (mkdir, atomic file create) that you want rank0\n    to do alone — others wait at the entry barrier, then proceed once rank0\n    has finished.\n    \"\"\"\n    s = state()\n    if s[\"world_size\"] == 1 or s[\"rank\"] != 0:\n        # Non-zero ranks wait here for rank0's exit barrier.\n        if s[\"world_size\"] > 1:\n            dist.barrier()\n        try:\n            yield\n        finally:\n            if s[\"world_size\"] > 1:\n                dist.barrier()\n        return\n    try:\n        yield\n    finally:\n        if s[\"world_size\"] > 1:\n            dist.barrier()\n            dist.barrier()  # paired with non-zero rank's two barriers\n\n\ndef broadcast_object(obj: Any, src: int = 0) -> Any:\n    \"\"\"Broadcast a small picklable object from src to all ranks.\n\n    Used to share random seeds, dataset file lists, and resume metadata.\n    Returns obj unchanged on world_size=1.\n    \"\"\"\n    s = state()\n    if s[\"world_size\"] == 1:\n        return obj\n    holder = [obj] if s[\"rank\"] == src else [None]\n    dist.broadcast_object_list(holder, src=src)\n    return holder[0]\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/scale/core/dataset.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"NVFP4 dataset + DDP-aware sampler + collate.\n\nEach .pt under the train dir is one independent sample (the v3mix collector\nwrites one file per prompt; segment dirs only matter for upload). We glob the\ntree once, sort for determinism, then let a DistributedSampler shard the\nfilename list across ranks.\n\nNVFP4 decode happens inside ``__getitem__`` on the worker process, so the\nCPU-heavy ``nvfp4_codec.decompress_sample`` overlaps with GPU compute on the\nmain process.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport random\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Iterator\n\nimport torch\nfrom torch.utils.data import Dataset, DistributedSampler, Sampler\n\n# These imports trigger eagle.* module top-level code that reads env vars\n# (TTT_STEPS, SEQ_LEN_MAX, NVFP4_FORWARD, ...). The caller MUST call\n# scale.core.config.inject_model_env(cfg) before importing this module.\nfrom eagle import nvfp4_codec  # noqa: E402\n\n\n# Keys we slice / pad / stack. Everything else is metadata and dropped at\n# batch time.\n_SAMPLE_KEYS = (\n    \"token_ids\",\n    \"aux_hidden\",\n    \"top_logit_values\",\n    \"top_logit_indices\",\n    \"target_logsumexp\",\n    \"assistant_mask\",\n    \"document_ids\",\n)\n\n\n# ── Dataset ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef enumerate_files(train_dir: str | Path, max_files: int = 0) -> list[Path]:\n    \"\"\"Glob ``train_dir`` recursively for .pt files and return a sorted list.\n\n    Sorting gives every rank the same file order independent of FS readdir\n    timing; DistributedSampler then shards deterministically.\n    \"\"\"\n    user_4813494d = Path(train_dir)\n    if not user_4813494d.exists():\n        raise FileNotFoundError(f\"train_dir does not exist: {user_4813494d}\")\n    files = sorted(user_4813494d.rglob(\"*.pt\"))\n    if not files:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"no .pt files under {user_4813494d}\")\n    if max_files > 0:\n        files = files[:max_files]\n    return files\n\n\nclass NVFP4Dataset(Dataset):\n    \"\"\"Iterates .pt files, decodes NVFP4 aux_hidden on read.\n\n    Held tensors are CPU bf16 / int (per nvfp4_codec output). Move to GPU\n    happens in the collate function via ``pin_memory + non_blocking``.\n\n    Files smaller than ``min_tokens`` are dropped at __init__ (rare; usually\n    means truncated download). We skip rather than fail so a stragglers batch\n    doesn't abort the run.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, files: list[Path], min_tokens: int = 8):\n        self.files = list(files)\n        self.min_tokens = min_tokens\n\n    def __len__(self) -> int:\n        return len(self.files)\n\n    def __getitem__(self, idx: int) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        path = self.files[idx]\n        sample = torch.load(path, weights_only=True, map_location=\"cpu\")\n        if nvfp4_codec.is_nvfp4_sample(sample):\n            sample = nvfp4_codec.decompress_sample(sample)\n        # Defensive: if the file is too short to be useful, return a sentinel\n        # the collate fn can detect. We can't raise here because workers swallow\n        # exceptions ungracefully.\n        if int(sample[\"token_ids\"].shape[0]) < self.min_tokens:\n            return {\"_skip\": True, \"_path\": str(path)}\n        # Keep only the keys we care about (drops metadata strings that don't\n        # pickle as tensors and bloat IPC).\n        out = {k: sample[k] for k in _SAMPLE_KEYS if k in sample}\n        # document_ids may be missing on some old v2/v3 files; default to a\n        # single-doc tensor so collate path is uniform.\n        if \"document_ids\" not in out:\n            n = out[\"token_ids\"].shape[0]\n            out[\"document_ids\"] = torch.zeros(n, dtype=torch.int32)\n        return out\n\n\n# ── Collate (single-doc, padded) ───────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass CollateCfg:\n    \"\"\"Knobs surfaced to the collate fn (avoid global state).\"\"\"\n    seq_len_max: int\n    dtype: torch.dtype = torch.bfloat16\n    tail: bool = False  # eval OOD wants the response tail, not the prompt head\n    pin_memory: bool = True\n\n\ndef _slice(sample: dict, max_len: int, tail: bool) -> dict:\n    n = int(sample[\"token_ids\"].shape[0])\n    if n <= max_len:\n        return sample\n    start = n - max_len if tail else 0\n    out = dict(sample)\n    for k in _SAMPLE_KEYS:\n        if k in sample and torch.is_tensor(sample[k]) and sample[k].shape[0] == n:\n            out[k] = sample[k][start : start + max_len]\n    return out\n\n\ndef make_collate(cfg: CollateCfg):\n    \"\"\"Return a collate function with a captured cfg.\n\n    The returned closure is process-local (workers don't need to pickle it\n    across the IPC boundary in the multiprocessing fork model).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def collate(samples: list[dict]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n        # Drop sentinels emitted by NVFP4Dataset for short files.\n        samples = [s for s in samples if not s.get(\"_skip\")]\n        if not samples:\n            return None\n        samples = [_slice(s, cfg.seq_len_max, cfg.tail) for s in samples]\n        seq_len = min(max(s[\"token_ids\"].shape[0] for s in samples), cfg.seq_len_max)\n\n        def stack(key: str, pad_value):\n            tensors = []\n            for s in samples:\n                t = s[key]\n                if t.shape[0] > seq_len:\n                    t = t[-seq_len:] if cfg.tail else t[:seq_len]\n                elif t.shape[0] < seq_len:\n                    pad_shape = list(t.shape)\n                    pad_shape[0] = seq_len - t.shape[0]\n                    pad = torch.full(pad_shape, pad_value, dtype=t.dtype)\n                    t = torch.cat([t, pad], dim=0)\n                tensors.append(t)\n            out = torch.stack(tensors)\n            if cfg.pin_memory and torch.cuda.is_available():\n                out = out.pin_memory()\n            return out\n\n        batch = {\n            \"token_ids\": stack(\"token_ids\", 0).long(),\n            \"aux_hidden\": stack(\"aux_hidden\", 0.0).to(cfg.dtype),\n            \"target_logits_values\": stack(\"top_logit_values\", 0.0).to(torch.float32),\n            \"target_logits_indices\": stack(\"top_logit_indices\", 0).long(),\n            \"target_logsumexp\": stack(\"target_logsumexp\", 0.0).to(torch.float32),\n            \"assistant_mask\": stack(\"assistant_mask\", False).bool(),\n            \"document_ids\": stack(\"document_ids\", 0).long(),\n            \"is_packed\": False,\n        }\n        return batch\n\n    return collate\n\n\n# ── Sampler ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\nclass ResumableDistributedSampler(DistributedSampler):\n    \"\"\"DistributedSampler that can resume from a given (epoch, batch_offset).\n\n    Default DistributedSampler exposes ``set_epoch`` but not an offset, so a\n    resume restarts the epoch from index 0. We add a per-rank ``start_index``\n    that skips the first N samples on the next iteration only — subsequent\n    epochs reset to 0 as normal.\n\n    State is the tuple (epoch, sample_index_within_epoch). The trainer is\n    expected to track both and call ``set_state`` after loading a ckpt.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):\n        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)\n        self._resume_start = 0\n\n    def set_state(self, epoch: int, start_index: int) -> None:\n        self.set_epoch(epoch)\n        self._resume_start = max(0, int(start_index))\n\n    def __iter__(self) -> Iterator[int]:\n        indices = list(super().__iter__())\n        if self._resume_start > 0:\n            indices = indices[self._resume_start :]\n            # One-shot: subsequent epochs start from 0.\n            self._resume_start = 0\n        return iter(indices)\n\n\nclass FixedRepeatSampler(Sampler[int]):\n    \"\"\"Cycle indefinitely through ``indices``, shuffled each pass.\n\n    Used by ``overfit`` to fix the support set to N samples while still\n    shuffling within the support so the loss curve isn't a periodic pattern.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, indices: list[int], seed: int = 0):\n        self.indices = list(indices)\n        self.seed = seed\n        self._epoch = 0\n\n    def __iter__(self) -> Iterator[int]:\n        rng = random.Random(self.seed + self._epoch)\n        order = list(self.indices)\n        rng.shuffle(order)\n        self._epoch += 1\n        return iter(order)\n\n    def __len__(self) -> int:\n        return len(self.indices)\n\n\n# ── DataLoader builder ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef build_train_loader(\n    dataset: NVFP4Dataset,\n    micro_batch: int,\n    seq_len_max: int,\n    world_size: int,\n    rank: int,\n    num_workers: int,\n    seed: int,\n    fixed_support: bool = False,\n    pin_memory: bool = True,\n):\n    \"\"\"Construct the dataset/sampler/dataloader triplet for training.\n\n    ``fixed_support=True`` swaps in the FixedRepeatSampler (overfit mode).\n    Otherwise the standard ResumableDistributedSampler is used.\n\n    The returned sampler is also returned so the trainer can call set_state\n    on it from the resume path.\n    \"\"\"\n    if fixed_support:\n        # Overfit on rank0's slice only — multi-rank overfit makes no sense\n        # because every rank should see every sample of the (small) support.\n        indices = list(range(len(dataset)))\n        sampler: Sampler[int] = FixedRepeatSampler(indices, seed=seed)\n    else:\n        sampler = ResumableDistributedSampler(\n            dataset,\n            num_replicas=world_size,\n            rank=rank,\n            shuffle=True,\n            seed=seed,\n            drop_last=True,\n        )\n\n    collate = make_collate(CollateCfg(\n        seq_len_max=seq_len_max,\n        dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n        pin_memory=pin_memory,\n    ))\n\n    loader = torch.utils.data.DataLoader(\n        dataset,\n        batch_size=micro_batch,\n        sampler=sampler,\n        num_workers=num_workers,\n        collate_fn=collate,\n        pin_memory=False,  # we pin per-tensor inside collate so .pin_memory() works on dict values\n        persistent_workers=(num_workers > 0),\n        prefetch_factor=2 if num_workers > 0 else None,\n        drop_last=True,\n    )\n    return loader, sampler\n\n\ndef build_eval_loader(\n    files: list[Path],\n    seq_len_max: int,\n    num_workers: int,\n    tail: bool,\n):\n    \"\"\"One-pass eval loader. Single-rank only; eval is rank0-only by convention.\"\"\"\n    dataset = NVFP4Dataset(files)\n    collate = make_collate(CollateCfg(\n        seq_len_max=seq_len_max,\n        dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n        tail=tail,\n        pin_memory=True,\n    ))\n    return torch.utils.data.DataLoader(\n        dataset,\n        batch_size=1,\n        shuffle=False,\n        num_workers=num_workers,\n        collate_fn=collate,\n        pin_memory=False,\n        persistent_workers=(num_workers > 0),\n        prefetch_factor=2 if num_workers > 0 else None,\n    )\n\n\ndef batch_to_cuda(batch: dict, device: torch.device) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Async H2D for the dict tensors. Pinned src auto-routes to copy stream.\"\"\"\n    return {\n        k: (v.to(device, non_blocking=True) if torch.is_tensor(v) else v)\n        for k, v in batch.items()\n    }\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/scale/core/logging.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Logger: jsonl to file + stdout, rank0-only by default.\n\nNo external deps (no wandb / tensorboard) — H100 submission environment has\nno external network. Downstream tooling can grep / jq / pandas the jsonl\nafter the run finishes.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom scale.core import dist\n\n\nclass JsonlLogger:\n    \"\"\"Append-only jsonl writer.\n\n    All records get an automatic ``time`` field so retrospective analysis can\n    join with system metrics by timestamp. Non-rank0 ranks silently no-op so\n    a log file is never touched twice.\n\n    The writer keeps the file handle open and flushes after every record so a\n    job killed mid-step still leaves a complete file up to the last write.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, path: Path, also_stdout: bool = True):\n        self.path = Path(path)\n        self.also_stdout = also_stdout\n        self._fh = None\n        if dist.is_rank0():\n            self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n            self._fh = self.path.open(\"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n    def log(self, kind: str, **fields: Any) -> None:\n        if not dist.is_rank0():\n            return\n        record = {\"kind\": kind, \"time\": time.time(), **fields}\n        line = json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False, default=_jsonable)\n        self._fh.write(line + \"\\n\")\n        self._fh.flush()\n        if self.also_stdout:\n            print(line, flush=True)\n\n    def info(self, msg: str, **fields: Any) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Console-only human-readable line (also still written to jsonl).\"\"\"\n        self.log(\"info\", message=msg, **fields)\n\n    def close(self) -> None:\n        if self._fh is not None:\n            self._fh.close()\n            self._fh = None\n\n\ndef _jsonable(value):\n    \"\"\"Fall back so torch tensors / numpy scalars / Path values serialize.\n\n    We try the cheapest conversions first; anything truly opaque becomes a\n    repr() to keep the writer from dying mid-step.\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        import torch\n        if torch.is_tensor(value):\n            if value.numel() == 1:\n                return value.item()\n            return value.detach().cpu().tolist()\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    if hasattr(value, \"item\"):\n        try:\n            return value.item()\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n    if isinstance(value, Path):\n        return str(value)\n    return repr(value)\n\n\ndef banner(msg: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Big visible header on rank0 stdout. No jsonl entry.\"\"\"\n    if not dist.is_rank0():\n        return\n    bar = \"=\" * 70\n    print(f\"\\n{bar}\\n  {msg}\\n{bar}\", flush=True)\n    sys.stdout.flush()\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/scale/core/ckpt.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Checkpoint save (rank0) + load with broadcast to all ranks.\n\nThe on-disk format mirrors the legacy ``best.pt`` so the converter in\n``eagle/training/sala_draft/convert_to_sglang.py`` keeps working.\n\nResume contract:\n    * model weights (excluding the frozen embed_tokens — saves ~600 MB)\n    * optimizer state (m/v for true AdamW resume)\n    * sampler state (epoch + sample index, so we don't re-train recent data)\n    * RNG state (python + torch + cuda)\n    * global_step + best_metric\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport dataclasses\nimport random\nimport time\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\n\nfrom scale.core import dist\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ResumeState:\n    \"\"\"Everything needed to pick up training mid-stream.\"\"\"\n    global_step: int = 0\n    epoch: int = 0\n    sample_index: int = 0  # next sample index within current epoch (per-rank)\n    best_metric: float = -1.0\n    best_step: int = -1\n\n\ndef _unwrap(model: nn.Module) -> nn.Module:\n    return model.module if hasattr(model, \"module\") else model\n\n\ndef save(\n    dest_dir: Path,\n    name: str,\n    model: nn.Module,\n    optimizer: torch.optim.Optimizer,\n    state: ResumeState,\n    extra: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n) -> Path | None:\n    \"\"\"Atomic rank0 save. Returns the written path (None on non-rank0).\"\"\"\n    if not dist.is_rank0():\n        return None\n\n    dest_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    out_path = dest_dir / name\n    tmp_path = dest_dir / (name + \".tmp\")\n\n    raw = _unwrap(model)\n    # Drop frozen embed_tokens like the legacy trainer.\n    model_state = {\n        k: v.detach().cpu()\n        for k, v in raw.named_parameters()\n        if not k.startswith(\"embed_tokens\")\n    }\n    # Buffers (t2d / d2t / draft_idx_map / rope_cos / rope_sin) need to be\n    # saved too so reload picks up the vocab cache without re-running it.\n    for k, v in raw.named_buffers():\n        if k.startswith(\"embed_tokens\"):\n            continue\n        model_state[k] = v.detach().cpu()\n\n    ckpt = {\n        \"model_state_dict\": model_state,\n        \"optimizer_state_dict\": optimizer.state_dict(),\n        \"resume_state\": dataclasses.asdict(state),\n        \"rng\": _capture_rng(),\n        \"timestamp\": time.time(),\n    }\n    if extra:\n        ckpt[\"extra\"] = extra\n\n    torch.save(ckpt, tmp_path)\n    tmp_path.replace(out_path)\n    return out_path\n\n\ndef load(\n    src_path: Path,\n    model: nn.Module,\n    optimizer: torch.optim.Optimizer | None,\n) -> ResumeState:\n    \"\"\"Load on rank0, broadcast model/optim/state to other ranks.\n\n    Strategy: rank0 reads from disk, then DDP's standard parameter broadcast\n    (triggered by the next forward) handles weight sync. Optimizer state and\n    resume_state are broadcast explicitly via broadcast_object_list because\n    they are not DDP-managed.\n    \"\"\"\n    src_path = Path(src_path)\n    if dist.is_rank0():\n        if not src_path.exists():\n            raise FileNotFoundError(f\"resume ckpt not found: {src_path}\")\n        ckpt = torch.load(src_path, weights_only=False, map_location=\"cpu\")\n    else:\n        ckpt = None\n\n    raw = _unwrap(model)\n    # Model weights: rank0 loads, others zero-init then re-broadcast on first\n    # forward (DDP does this automatically on construct). We still ensure\n    # rank0 loaded successfully before all ranks proceed.\n    if dist.is_rank0():\n        missing, unexpected = raw.load_state_dict(ckpt[\"model_state_dict\"], strict=False)\n        if missing:\n            print(f\"[ckpt] missing keys (will use init): {missing[:3]}{'...' if len(missing) > 3 else ''}\")\n        if unexpected:\n            print(f\"[ckpt] unexpected keys: {unexpected[:3]}{'...' if len(unexpected) > 3 else ''}\")\n\n    # Broadcast model parameters from rank0 to all ranks (avoids needing\n    # every rank to read the same file).\n    state_info = dist.state()\n    if state_info[\"world_size\"] > 1:\n        import torch.distributed as tdist\n        for p in raw.parameters():\n            tdist.broadcast(p.data, src=0)\n        for b in raw.buffers():\n            if b.dtype.is_floating_point or b.dtype == torch.bool or b.dtype in (\n                torch.int8, torch.int16, torch.int32, torch.int64,\n            ):\n                tdist.broadcast(b.data, src=0)\n\n    # Optimizer state: broadcast as a python object.\n    if optimizer is not None:\n        opt_state = ckpt[\"optimizer_state_dict\"] if dist.is_rank0() else None\n        opt_state = dist.broadcast_object(opt_state, src=0)\n        if opt_state is not None:\n            try:\n                optimizer.load_state_dict(opt_state)\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"[ckpt] optimizer state incompatible, fresh: {e}\")\n\n    resume_dict = ckpt[\"resume_state\"] if dist.is_rank0() else None\n    resume_dict = dist.broadcast_object(resume_dict, src=0)\n    state = ResumeState(**resume_dict) if resume_dict else ResumeState()\n\n    rng_state = ckpt[\"rng\"] if dist.is_rank0() else None\n    rng_state = dist.broadcast_object(rng_state, src=0)\n    if rng_state:\n        _restore_rng(rng_state)\n\n    dist.barrier()\n    return state\n\n\ndef _capture_rng() -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Snapshot python/numpy/torch/cuda RNG state for exact resume.\"\"\"\n    state = {\n        \"python\": random.getstate(),\n        \"numpy\": np.random.get_state(),\n        \"torch\": torch.get_rng_state().tolist(),\n    }\n    if torch.cuda.is_available():\n        state[\"cuda\"] = [t.tolist() for t in torch.cuda.get_rng_state_all()]\n    return state\n\n\ndef _restore_rng(state: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    random.setstate(state[\"python\"])\n    np.random.set_state(state[\"numpy\"])\n    torch.set_rng_state(torch.tensor(state[\"torch\"], dtype=torch.uint8))\n    if torch.cuda.is_available() and \"cuda\" in state:\n        cuda_states = [torch.tensor(t, dtype=torch.uint8) for t in state[\"cuda\"]]\n        torch.cuda.set_rng_state_all(cuda_states)\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/scale/core/trainer.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Unified train loop shared by every cli entry.\n\nDifferences from the legacy single-GPU trainer:\n  * DDP-aware (NCCL allreduce, no_sync in GA inner loop, rank0-only eval/ckpt)\n  * Resume covers (model, optim, sampler, rng, step) — no more 'resume from\n    step 0 with stale data position' silent failure mode\n  * No interactive flags; all config lives in yaml\n  * jsonl logging, no rich/tqdm dependency in the hot path\n  * bf16 fake-STE forward by default (H100 has no NVFP4 tensor cores); the\n    sm_120 NVFP4 forward path is still reachable via model.nvfp4_forward=true\n\nPublic surface:\n    build_runtime(cfg, run_dir) -> Runtime\n    train_loop(runtime, mode='train'|'smoke'|'overfit', max_steps=None)\n    evaluate(runtime, split='ind'|'ood', max_files=...) -> list[float] | None\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport math\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom contextlib import nullcontext\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\nfrom safetensors import safe_open\nfrom torch.nn.parallel import DistributedDataParallel as DDP\n\nfrom scale.core import ckpt as ckpt_mod\nfrom scale.core import dist as dist_mod\nfrom scale.core.config import Config, inject_model_env\nfrom scale.core.dataset import (\n    NVFP4Dataset,\n    batch_to_cuda,\n    build_eval_loader,\n    build_train_loader,\n    enumerate_files,\n)\nfrom scale.core.logging import JsonlLogger, banner\n\n# Eagle imports happen lazily inside _build_model so inject_model_env runs first.\n\n\n# ── Runtime container ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass Runtime:\n    cfg: Config\n    run_dir: Path\n    device: torch.device\n    model: nn.Module  # wrapped (DDP or raw)\n    raw_model: nn.Module  # unwrapped for buffer access\n    optimizer: torch.optim.Optimizer\n    train_loader: Any\n    train_sampler: Any\n    val_ind_files: list[Path]\n    val_ood_files: list[Path]\n    state: ckpt_mod.ResumeState\n    logger: JsonlLogger\n\n\n# ── Build ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef _load_target_artifacts(cfg: Config) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\n    \"\"\"Load embed + lm_head weights from the configured target source.\n\n    Prefers the small artifacts_file extracted by tools/extract_target_artifacts.py\n    (two tensors, ~1.2 GB) over the full target model dir (8+ GB, NVFP4-packed).\n    \"\"\"\n    af = cfg.target.artifacts_file\n    if af and Path(af).exists():\n        with safe_open(af, framework=\"pt\", device=\"cpu\") as f:\n            embed = f.get_tensor(\"model.embed_tokens.weight\").float()\n            lm = f.get_tensor(\"lm_head.weight\").float()\n        return embed, lm\n\n    if not cfg.target.model_dir:\n        raise FileNotFoundError(\n            f\"target.artifacts_file does not exist ({af}) and target.model_dir is empty\"\n        )\n    # Fallback: walk the full safetensors index (mirrors v3train.load_embed_and_lm_head).\n    import json\n    md = Path(cfg.target.model_dir)\n    index_path = md / \"model.safetensors.index.json\"\n    if index_path.exists():\n        index = json.loads(index_path.read_text())\n        wm = index[\"weight_map\"]\n        with safe_open(md / wm[\"model.embed_tokens.weight\"], framework=\"pt\", device=\"cpu\") as f:\n            embed = f.get_tensor(\"model.embed_tokens.weight\").float()\n        lm_file = wm.get(\"lm_head.weight\")\n        if lm_file:\n            with safe_open(md / lm_file, framework=\"pt\", device=\"cpu\") as f:\n                lm = f.get_tensor(\"lm_head.weight\").float()\n        else:\n            lm = embed.clone()\n    else:\n        with safe_open(md / \"model.safetensors\", framework=\"pt\", device=\"cpu\") as f:\n            embed = f.get_tensor(\"model.embed_tokens.weight\").float()\n            try:\n                lm = f.get_tensor(\"lm_head.weight\").float()\n            except Exception:\n                lm = embed.clone()\n    return embed, lm\n\n\ndef _init_mlp_warm_start(model: nn.Module, cfg: Config) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Optionally warm-start MLP/o_proj from a BF16 target layer.\n\n    Returns the number of tensors successfully copied. Skips silently if the\n    BF16 target dir is missing or empty.\n    \"\"\"\n    src = cfg.target.bf16_model_dir\n    if not src or not Path(src).is_dir():\n        return 0\n    import json\n    index_path = Path(src) / \"model.safetensors.index.json\"\n    if not index_path.exists():\n        return 0\n    index = json.loads(index_path.read_text())\n    wm = index[\"weight_map\"]\n    pfx = f\"model.layers.{cfg.target.init_layer}\"\n    raw = model.module if hasattr(model, \"module\") else model\n    layer_map = {\n        f\"{pfx}.mlp.gate_proj.weight\": raw.midlayer.mlp.gate_proj.weight,\n        f\"{pfx}.mlp.up_proj.weight\": raw.midlayer.mlp.up_proj.weight,\n        f\"{pfx}.mlp.down_proj.weight\": raw.midlayer.mlp.down_proj.weight,\n        f\"{pfx}.self_attn.o_proj.weight\": raw.midlayer.self_attn.o_proj.weight,\n    }\n    loaded = 0\n    for key, param in layer_map.items():\n        if key not in wm:\n            continue\n        with safe_open(Path(src) / wm[key], framework=\"pt\", device=\"cpu\") as f:\n            w = f.get_tensor(key).to(torch.bfloat16)\n        if w.shape == param.shape:\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                param.copy_(w.to(param.device))\n            loaded += 1\n    return loaded\n\n\ndef _build_model(cfg: Config, device: torch.device) -> tuple[nn.Module, nn.Module]:\n    \"\"\"Build the wrapped (DDP) and unwrapped model.\n\n    Imports eagle.* lazily, after inject_model_env has run, so the trainer's\n    module-level constants reflect the config.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Imports here, not at file top, so config-driven env vars are honored.\n    from eagle.training.sala_draft.train import Eagle3ModelV4  # noqa: E402\n\n    embed_w, lm_head_w = _load_target_artifacts(cfg)\n\n    raw = Eagle3ModelV4(embed_w, lm_head_w).to(device).to(torch.bfloat16)\n    if cfg.data.vocab_cache:\n        raw.load_v4_vocab_cache(Path(cfg.data.vocab_cache))\n    n_warm = _init_mlp_warm_start(raw, cfg)\n    if dist_mod.is_rank0():\n        print(f\"[scale] MLP warm-start tensors loaded: {n_warm}\")\n\n    state = dist_mod.state()\n    if state[\"world_size\"] > 1:\n        # static_graph=True requires the autograd graph to be identical across\n        # iterations — which our TTT-fixed loop satisfies. gradient_as_bucket_view\n        # cuts a copy on each allreduce. find_unused_parameters=False is the\n        # default but we make it explicit for clarity.\n        model = DDP(\n            raw,\n            device_ids=[state[\"local_rank\"]],\n            output_device=state[\"local_rank\"],\n            gradient_as_bucket_view=True,\n            static_graph=True,\n            find_unused_parameters=False,\n        )\n    else:\n        model = raw\n\n    return model, raw\n\n\ndef _build_optimizer(model: nn.Module, cfg: Config) -> torch.optim.Optimizer:\n    \"\"\"AdamW with weight-decay split. Same convention as legacy trainer.\"\"\"\n    decay, no_decay = [], []\n    for name, p in model.named_parameters():\n        if not p.requires_grad:\n            continue\n        if name.endswith(\".bias\") or name.endswith(\"norm.weight\"):\n            no_decay.append(p)\n        else:\n            decay.append(p)\n    return torch.optim.AdamW(\n        [\n            {\"params\": decay, \"weight_decay\": cfg.optim.weight_decay},\n            {\"params\": no_decay, \"weight_decay\": 0.0},\n        ],\n        lr=cfg.optim.lr,\n        betas=(cfg.optim.beta1, cfg.optim.beta2),\n        fused=True,\n    )\n\n\ndef _resolve_files(cfg: Config) -> tuple[list[Path], list[Path], list[Path]]:\n    train_files = enumerate_files(cfg.data.train_dir, max_files=cfg.data.train_max_files)\n    val_ind = []\n    val_ood = []\n    if cfg.data.val_ind_dir and Path(cfg.data.val_ind_dir).exists():\n        val_ind = sorted(Path(cfg.data.val_ind_dir).glob(\"*.pt\"))[: cfg.data.eval_max_ind]\n    if cfg.data.val_ood_dir and Path(cfg.data.val_ood_dir).exists():\n        val_ood = sorted(Path(cfg.data.val_ood_dir).glob(\"*.pt\"))[: cfg.data.eval_max_ood]\n    return train_files, val_ind, val_ood\n\n\ndef build_runtime(cfg: Config, run_dir: Path, fixed_support: bool = False) -> Runtime:\n    \"\"\"Wire everything together. Must be called after dist_mod.init().\"\"\"\n    # Inject eagle env vars BEFORE any eagle.* import in dataset/trainer.\n    inject_model_env(cfg)\n\n    state_info = dist_mod.state()\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{state_info['local_rank']}\")\n    torch.manual_seed(cfg.train.seed + state_info[\"rank\"])\n\n    train_files, val_ind, val_ood = _resolve_files(cfg)\n    if dist_mod.is_rank0():\n        banner(\n            f\"scale {cfg.runtime.run_name or 'run'} \"\n            f\"world={state_info['world_size']} rank={state_info['rank']} \"\n            f\"train={len(train_files)} val_ind={len(val_ind)} val_ood={len(val_ood)}\"\n        )\n\n    model, raw_model = _build_model(cfg, device)\n    optimizer = _build_optimizer(model, cfg)\n\n    train_loader, train_sampler = build_train_loader(\n        NVFP4Dataset(train_files),\n        micro_batch=cfg.optim.micro_batch,\n        seq_len_max=cfg.model.seq_len_max,\n        world_size=state_info[\"world_size\"],\n        rank=state_info[\"rank\"],\n        num_workers=cfg.runtime.dataloader_workers,\n        seed=cfg.train.seed,\n        fixed_support=fixed_support,\n        pin_memory=True,\n    )\n\n    logger = JsonlLogger(run_dir / \"train.jsonl\", also_stdout=True)\n\n    state = ckpt_mod.ResumeState()\n    if cfg.train.resume_from:\n        resume_path = Path(cfg.train.resume_from)\n        if resume_path.is_dir():\n            resume_path = resume_path / \"best.pt\"\n        state = ckpt_mod.load(resume_path, model, optimizer)\n        if dist_mod.is_rank0():\n            logger.log(\"resume\", path=str(resume_path), **state.__dict__)\n\n    return Runtime(\n        cfg=cfg,\n        run_dir=run_dir,\n        device=device,\n        model=model,\n        raw_model=raw_model,\n        optimizer=optimizer,\n        train_loader=train_loader,\n        train_sampler=train_sampler,\n        val_ind_files=val_ind,\n        val_ood_files=val_ood,\n        state=state,\n        logger=logger,\n    )\n\n\n# ── LR schedule ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef make_lr_fn(cfg: Config):\n    \"\"\"Linear warmup → cosine decay, matches the legacy trainer.\"\"\"\n    total = cfg.train.steps\n    warmup = max(cfg.optim.warmup_min_steps, int(round(total * cfg.optim.warmup_ratio)))\n    floor_factor = cfg.optim.lr_min_factor\n    lr = cfg.optim.lr\n\n    def fn(step: int) -> float:\n        if step < warmup:\n            return lr * step / max(1, warmup)\n        progress = (step - warmup) / max(1, total - warmup)\n        progress = min(1.0, max(0.0, progress))\n        cos = 0.5 * (1.0 + math.cos(math.pi * progress))\n        return lr * (floor_factor + (1.0 - floor_factor) * cos)\n\n    return fn\n\n\ndef _set_lr(opt: torch.optim.Optimizer, lr: float) -> None:\n    for g in opt.param_groups:\n        g[\"lr\"] = lr\n\n\n# ── Evaluation ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef evaluate(\n    runtime: Runtime,\n    files: list[Path],\n    label: str,\n    tail: bool,\n) -> list[float] | None:\n    \"\"\"Rank0-only forward over the eval files. Returns per-TTT-step acc.\"\"\"\n    if not files:\n        return None\n    if not dist_mod.is_rank0():\n        return None\n\n    cfg = runtime.cfg\n    raw = runtime.raw_model\n    raw.eval()\n    ttt = cfg.model.ttt_steps\n\n    loader = build_eval_loader(\n        files,\n        seq_len_max=cfg.model.seq_len_max,\n        num_workers=cfg.runtime.dataloader_workers,\n        tail=tail,\n    )\n\n    correct = [0.0] * ttt\n    valid = [0.0] * ttt\n    n_batches = 0\n    with torch.no_grad():\n        for batch in loader:\n            if batch is None:\n                continue\n            batch = batch_to_cuda(batch, runtime.device)\n            _, _, _, stats = raw(**batch, return_stats=True)\n            for i in range(ttt):\n                correct[i] += stats[\"correct\"][i]\n                valid[i] += stats[\"valid\"][i]\n            n_batches += 1\n    raw.train()\n    if n_batches == 0:\n        return None\n    return [correct[i] / max(valid[i], 1.0) for i in range(ttt)]\n\n\n# ── Main loop ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef train_loop(\n    runtime: Runtime,\n    *,\n    max_steps: int | None = None,\n    save_best: bool = True,\n    profile_hook=None,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Run the optimizer loop and return a summary dict.\n\n    ``max_steps`` overrides cfg.train.steps when set (used by smoke / probe).\n    ``profile_hook(step)`` runs after each optimizer step and can call out to\n    a torch.profiler.profile() context the caller manages.\n    \"\"\"\n    cfg = runtime.cfg\n    model = runtime.model\n    opt = runtime.optimizer\n    device = runtime.device\n    logger = runtime.logger\n    state = runtime.state\n\n    target_steps = max_steps if max_steps is not None else cfg.train.steps\n    lr_fn = make_lr_fn(cfg)\n\n    # Step-0 eval (skip on resume — already evaluated before the prior crash).\n    if state.global_step == 0 and save_best:\n        best = _eval_and_maybe_save(runtime, state, save_best=save_best)\n        state = best\n\n    model.train()\n    data_iter = _epoch_iterator(runtime)\n    step_t0 = time.monotonic()\n    tokens_seen = 0\n\n    while state.global_step < target_steps:\n        state.global_step += 1\n        opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)\n        lr_now = lr_fn(state.global_step)\n        _set_lr(opt, lr_now)\n\n        accum_loss = 0.0\n        accum_acc = [0.0] * cfg.model.ttt_steps\n        accum_n = 0\n\n        for micro_step in range(cfg.optim.grad_accum):\n            batch = next(data_iter, None)\n            if batch is None:\n                # Epoch boundary; re-seed sampler and continue.\n                state.epoch += 1\n                state.sample_index = 0\n                data_iter = _epoch_iterator(runtime)\n                batch = next(data_iter)\n            if batch is None:\n                raise RuntimeError(\"data loader produced no usable batches\")\n            tokens_seen += int(batch[\"token_ids\"].numel())\n            batch = batch_to_cuda(batch, device)\n\n            is_last_ga = micro_step == cfg.optim.grad_accum - 1\n            sync_ctx = nullcontext() if is_last_ga else dist_mod.no_sync(model)\n            with sync_ctx:\n                total_loss, _step_losses, step_accs = model(**batch)\n                (total_loss / cfg.optim.grad_accum).backward()\n\n            accum_loss += float(total_loss.detach().item())\n            for i, a in enumerate(step_accs):\n                accum_acc[i] += float(a)\n            accum_n += 1\n            state.sample_index += cfg.optim.micro_batch\n\n        grad_norm = torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), cfg.optim.max_grad_norm)\n        opt.step()\n\n        avg_loss = accum_loss / max(accum_n, 1)\n        avg_acc = [a / max(accum_n, 1) for a in accum_acc]\n        # Reduce across ranks for honest aggregated logging.\n        avg_loss = dist_mod.reduce_mean(avg_loss, device)\n        avg_acc = [dist_mod.reduce_mean(a, device) for a in avg_acc]\n\n        if math.isnan(avg_loss) or math.isinf(avg_loss):\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"loss NaN/Inf at step {state.global_step}\")\n\n        if state.global_step % cfg.train.log_every == 0:\n            elapsed = time.monotonic() - step_t0\n            step_t0 = time.monotonic()\n            logger.log(\n                \"train\",\n                step=state.global_step,\n                epoch=state.epoch,\n                loss=avg_loss,\n                acc=avg_acc,\n                grad_norm=float(grad_norm.detach().item()),\n                lr=lr_now,\n                step_s=elapsed,\n                tok_per_s=tokens_seen / max(elapsed, 1e-6),\n            )\n            tokens_seen = 0\n\n        if profile_hook is not None:\n            profile_hook(state.global_step)\n\n        if cfg.train.eval_every > 0 and state.global_step % cfg.train.eval_every == 0:\n            state = _eval_and_maybe_save(runtime, state, save_best=save_best)\n            model.train()\n\n        if cfg.train.ckpt_every > 0 and state.global_step % cfg.train.ckpt_every == 0:\n            ckpt_mod.save(runtime.run_dir, \"latest.pt\", model, opt, state)\n\n        if cfg.train.empty_cache_every > 0 and state.global_step % cfg.train.empty_cache_every == 0:\n            torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n    # Final eval + save\n    if save_best:\n        state = _eval_and_maybe_save(runtime, state, save_best=save_best, final=True)\n    ckpt_mod.save(runtime.run_dir, \"final.pt\", model, opt, state)\n    runtime.state = state\n    return {\"state\": state, \"run_dir\": str(runtime.run_dir)}\n\n\ndef _epoch_iterator(runtime: Runtime):\n    \"\"\"Wrap the dataloader so the sampler's set_state is honored on resume.\"\"\"\n    sampler = runtime.train_sampler\n    state = runtime.state\n    if hasattr(sampler, \"set_state\"):\n        sampler.set_state(state.epoch, state.sample_index)\n    elif hasattr(sampler, \"set_epoch\"):\n        sampler.set_epoch(state.epoch)\n    for batch in runtime.train_loader:\n        if batch is None:\n            continue\n        yield batch\n\n\ndef _eval_and_maybe_save(\n    runtime: Runtime,\n    state: ckpt_mod.ResumeState,\n    save_best: bool,\n    final: bool = False,\n) -> ckpt_mod.ResumeState:\n    cfg = runtime.cfg\n    ind = evaluate(runtime, runtime.val_ind_files, \"ind\", tail=False)\n    ood = evaluate(runtime, runtime.val_ood_files, \"ood\", tail=True)\n    select = (ood[0] if ood else (ind[0] if ind else -1.0))\n    improved = select > state.best_metric\n    if dist_mod.is_rank0():\n        runtime.logger.log(\n            \"eval\",\n            step=state.global_step,\n            ind=ind,\n            ood=ood,\n            best_metric=state.best_metric,\n            select_metric=select,\n            improved=improved,\n            final=final,\n        )\n    if save_best and improved:\n        state.best_metric = select\n        state.best_step = state.global_step\n        ckpt_mod.save(\n            runtime.run_dir,\n            \"best.pt\",\n            runtime.model,\n            runtime.optimizer,\n            state,\n            extra={\"ind\": ind, \"ood\": ood},\n        )\n    dist_mod.barrier()\n    return state\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/scale/cli/_common.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Shared cli plumbing — argparse, dist init, run dir, teardown.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nfrom contextlib import contextmanager\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom scale.core import dist\nfrom scale.core.config import Config, dump_effective, load_config, resolve_run_dir\nfrom scale.core.logging import banner\n\n\ndef parse_args(prog: str) -> argparse.Namespace:\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=prog)\n    ap.add_argument(\"--config\", required=True, type=Path)\n    return ap.parse_args()\n\n\ndef setup(prog: str) -> tuple[Config, Path]:\n    args = parse_args(prog)\n    cfg = load_config(args.config)\n    dist.init()\n    run_dir = resolve_run_dir(cfg, args.config)\n    run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    if dist.is_rank0():\n        dump_effective(cfg, run_dir)\n        banner(f\"{prog} → {run_dir}\")\n    dist.barrier()\n    return cfg, run_dir\n\n\n@contextmanager\ndef lifecycle(prog: str):\n    \"\"\"Setup, yield (cfg, run_dir), and tear down.\"\"\"\n    cfg, run_dir = setup(prog)\n    try:\n        yield cfg, run_dir\n    finally:\n        dist.shutdown()\n"
}

  "content": "\"\"\"Full training entry. Reads cfg.train.steps from the yaml.\n\nRun:\n    torchrun --nproc_per_node=8 -m scale.cli.train --config configs/h100/train.yaml\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom scale.cli._common import lifecycle\nfrom scale.core.trainer import build_runtime, train_loop\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    with lifecycle(\"scale.cli.train\") as (cfg, run_dir):\n        runtime = build_runtime(cfg, run_dir, fixed_support=False)\n        train_loop(runtime, save_best=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/scale/cli/smoke.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Smoke entry: short e2e run that exercises every path.\n\nVerifies in ~50 step that dist init / dataloader / forward / backward /\nallreduce / eval / ckpt save+load / jsonl logging all work end to end. Loss\nis not expected to converge — the only fail mode is a crash or NaN.\n\nRun:\n    torchrun --nproc_per_node=1 -m scale.cli.smoke --config configs/local/smoke.yaml\n    torchrun --nproc_per_node=8 -m scale.cli.smoke --config configs/h100/smoke.yaml\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom scale.cli._common import lifecycle\nfrom scale.core.trainer import build_runtime, train_loop\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    with lifecycle(\"scale.cli.smoke\") as (cfg, run_dir):\n        runtime = build_runtime(cfg, run_dir, fixed_support=False)\n        # smoke always honors cfg.train.steps (yaml sets it small, e.g. 50).\n        train_loop(runtime, save_best=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/scale/cli/overfit.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Overfit entry: train on N=cfg.data.train_max_files samples until acc0 → ~1.\n\nMath-correctness sanity. If the loop, loss, masking and decode pipeline are\nright, a tiny support set should drive draft step-0 acc on those samples\ntoward 1.0 within a few hundred steps. If it doesn't, something is wrong —\nNEVER promote to a real run before this gate is green.\n\nRun:\n    torchrun --nproc_per_node=1 -m scale.cli.overfit --config configs/local/overfit.yaml\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom scale.cli._common import lifecycle\nfrom scale.core.trainer import build_runtime, train_loop\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    with lifecycle(\"scale.cli.overfit\") as (cfg, run_dir):\n        # train_max_files MUST be small. Validate so a misconfig doesn't waste\n        # an H100 slot training on the full set under fixed_support.\n        if cfg.data.train_max_files == 0 or cfg.data.train_max_files > 64:\n            raise ValueError(\n                \"overfit requires data.train_max_files > 0 and <= 64; \"\n                f\"got {cfg.data.train_max_files}\"\n            )\n        runtime = build_runtime(cfg, run_dir, fixed_support=True)\n        # No best-tracking; overfit acc is measured from the train loss curve.\n        train_loop(runtime, save_best=False)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/scale/cli/batch_probe.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Batch-size probe: find per-rank micro_batch ceiling for the configured shape.\n\nTwo modes:\n  * explicit sweep — cfg.probe.sweep_micro_batches is non-empty; try each in\n    order, record (micro_batch, peak_mem_gb, step_s, ok) to probe.jsonl\n  * auto binary search — sweep_micro_batches=[]; binary-search [1, max_try]\n    for the largest micro_batch that survives 10 optimizer steps without OOM\n\nAlways logs to ``<run_dir>/probe.jsonl``. Never writes a best.pt or final.pt.\n\nRun:\n    torchrun --nproc_per_node=8 -m scale.cli.batch_probe --config configs/h100/batch_probe.yaml\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport dataclasses\nimport gc\nimport json\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport torch\n\nfrom scale.cli._common import lifecycle\nfrom scale.core import dist\nfrom scale.core.config import Config\nfrom scale.core.trainer import build_runtime, train_loop\n\n\n_MAX_AUTO = 32  # auto search upper bound\n\n\ndef _try_one(cfg: Config, run_dir: Path, micro_batch: int) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Attempt 10 steps at the given micro_batch. Return result dict.\"\"\"\n    # Patch the dataclass field for this attempt.\n    patched = dataclasses.replace(\n        cfg,\n        optim=dataclasses.replace(cfg.optim, micro_batch=micro_batch),\n        train=dataclasses.replace(cfg.train, eval_every=0, ckpt_every=0, log_every=1),\n    )\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n    torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats()\n    t0 = time.monotonic()\n    try:\n        runtime = build_runtime(patched, run_dir, fixed_support=False)\n        train_loop(runtime, max_steps=10, save_best=False)\n        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0\n        peak = torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() / 1024**3\n        return {\n            \"micro_batch\": micro_batch,\n            \"ok\": True,\n            \"peak_mem_gb\": peak,\n            \"wall_s\": elapsed,\n            \"step_s_est\": elapsed / 10,\n        }\n    except torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError as e:\n        return {\"micro_batch\": micro_batch, \"ok\": False, \"reason\": \"oom\", \"error\": str(e)[:200]}\n    except Exception as e:\n        return {\"micro_batch\": micro_batch, \"ok\": False, \"reason\": \"error\", \"error\": str(e)[:200]}\n    finally:\n        gc.collect()\n        torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n\ndef _save(run_dir: Path, results: list[dict]) -> None:\n    if not dist.is_rank0():\n        return\n    out = run_dir / \"probe.jsonl\"\n    with out.open(\"w\") as f:\n        for r in results:\n            f.write(json.dumps(r) + \"\\n\")\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    with lifecycle(\"scale.cli.batch_probe\") as (cfg, run_dir):\n        sweep = list(cfg.probe.sweep_micro_batches)\n        results: list[dict] = []\n        if sweep:\n            for mb in sweep:\n                r = _try_one(cfg, run_dir, mb)\n                if dist.is_rank0():\n                    print(f\"[probe] mb={mb}: {r}\")\n                results.append(r)\n        else:\n            # Binary search: keep doubling until OOM, then binary refine.\n            lo, hi = 1, 1\n            while hi <= _MAX_AUTO:\n                r = _try_one(cfg, run_dir, hi)\n                results.append(r)\n                if dist.is_rank0():\n                    print(f\"[probe] auto mb={hi}: {r}\")\n                if not r[\"ok\"]:\n                    break\n                lo = hi\n                hi *= 2\n            # Binary search between last OK (lo) and first OOM (hi).\n            while hi - lo > 1:\n                mid = (lo + hi) // 2\n                r = _try_one(cfg, run_dir, mid)\n                results.append(r)\n                if dist.is_rank0():\n                    print(f\"[probe] auto mb={mid}: {r}\")\n                if r[\"ok\"]:\n                    lo = mid\n                else:\n                    hi = mid\n            if dist.is_rank0():\n                print(f\"[probe] auto search done; max safe micro_batch = {lo}\")\n\n        _save(run_dir, results)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/scale/cli/profile.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Profile entry: torch.profiler over N warm + M active steps, dump chrome trace.\n\nOutput:\n  * ``<run_dir>/profile/trace.json`` — Chrome trace, open in chrome://tracing\n    or perfetto.dev for kernel-level analysis\n  * ``<run_dir>/profile/summary.txt`` — top-20 ops by self CUDA time\n\nBoth small enough to download from a submission environment by hand.\n\nRun:\n    torchrun --nproc_per_node=8 -m scale.cli.profile --config configs/h100/profile.yaml\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport torch\n\nfrom scale.cli._common import lifecycle\nfrom scale.core import dist\nfrom scale.core.trainer import build_runtime, train_loop\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    with lifecycle(\"scale.cli.profile\") as (cfg, run_dir):\n        runtime = build_runtime(cfg, run_dir, fixed_support=False)\n\n        warmup = cfg.probe.profile_warmup\n        active = cfg.probe.profile_active\n        total_steps = warmup + active\n\n        prof_dir = run_dir / \"profile\"\n        prof_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n        activities = [torch.profiler.ProfilerActivity.CPU, torch.profiler.ProfilerActivity.CUDA]\n        # Only rank0 writes the chrome trace; other ranks profile but discard.\n        on_trace_ready = (\n            torch.profiler.tensorboard_trace_handler(str(prof_dir))\n            if dist.is_rank0()\n            else (lambda p: None)\n        )\n\n        with torch.profiler.profile(\n            activities=activities,\n            schedule=torch.profiler.schedule(\n                wait=0, warmup=warmup, active=active, repeat=1,\n            ),\n            on_trace_ready=on_trace_ready,\n            record_shapes=False,\n            profile_memory=False,\n            with_stack=False,\n        ) as prof:\n            def hook(step: int) -> None:\n                prof.step()\n\n            train_loop(runtime, max_steps=total_steps, save_best=False, profile_hook=hook)\n\n        if dist.is_rank0():\n            summary = prof.key_averages().table(\n                sort_by=\"self_cuda_time_total\", row_limit=20,\n            )\n            (prof_dir / \"summary.txt\").write_text(summary)\n            print(summary)\n            print(f\"[profile] chrome trace written under {prof_dir}/\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

  "content": "# Local overfit sanity. Verifies the math end-to-end on 4 samples in ~5 min.\n# Expected: train acc[0] reaches >0.95 by step 200; loss steadily decreases.\n# If acc[0] doesn't climb, the loss / mask / decode path is broken.\n\ndata:\n  train_dir: /user_4813494d/data/eagle_stage/overfit_train4_nvfp4\n  train_max_files: 4\n  vocab_cache: /user_4813494d/data/eagle_stage/vocab_cache_v3mix.pt\n  # No val: overfit signal is the training loss curve itself.\n  val_ind_dir: \"\"\n  val_ood_dir: \"\"\n\ntarget:\n  # No extract step locally yet — read directly from the NVFP4 target model dir\n  # (it still holds the bf16 embed + lm_head tensors).\n  model_dir: /user_4813494d/models/openbmb/MiniCPM-SALA-gptq46-calib90-90k-NVFP4-det\n  bf16_model_dir: /user_4813494d/models/openbmb/MiniCPM-SALA\n  init_layer: 16\n\nmodel:\n  ttt_steps: 3\n  seq_len_max: 1024\n  pack: false\n  nvfp4_forward: false   # bf16 fake-STE; same code path as H100\n  exclude_lm_head_from_fp4: true\n\noptim:\n  micro_batch: 1\n  grad_accum: 1\n  lr: 5.0e-4\n  warmup_ratio: 0.0\n  warmup_min_steps: 0\n  lr_min_factor: 1.0   # constant LR for overfit — no schedule confusion\n  weight_decay: 0.0    # off for overfit; we want pure memorization\n  max_grad_norm: 1.0\n\ntrain:\n  steps: 200\n  eval_every: 0\n  log_every: 10\n  ckpt_every: 0\n  seed: 42\n  empty_cache_every: 0\n\nruntime:\n  output_user_4813494d: scale/runs\n  run_name: local_overfit\n  dataloader_workers: 2\n  prefetch_queue: 2\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/configs/h100/train.yaml",
  "content": "# H100 8-GPU full v3mix 300K training.\n#\n# Path conventions (entrypoint.sh exports SCALE_DATA_user_4813494d / SCALE_OUTPUT_user_4813494d):\n#   /data/v3mix_300k/                  pre_sync_bos output (300K .pt files)\n#   /data/target_artifacts.safetensors extracted embed + lm_head (~1.2 GB)\n#   /data/vocab_cache_v3mix.pt         pre-built vocab cache\n#   /data/eval/ind/                    held-out IND samples\n#   /data/eval/ood/                    held-out OOD samples\n#   /output/runs/                      per-run subdirs land here\n#\n# Tune optim.micro_batch from batch_probe output before pushing.\n\ndata:\n  train_dir: /data/v3mix_300k\n  vocab_cache: /data/vocab_cache_v3mix.pt\n  val_ind_dir: /data/eval/ind\n  val_ood_dir: /data/eval/ood\n  eval_max_ind: 200\n  eval_max_ood: 64\n\ntarget:\n  artifacts_file: /data/target_artifacts.safetensors\n  # bf16 source for MLP warm-start — optional, skip if not synced.\n  bf16_model_dir: \"\"\n\nmodel:\n  ttt_steps: 3\n  seq_len_max: 2048   # v3mix saved length is 2048\n  pack: false         # enable after batch_probe shows headroom\n  nvfp4_forward: false   # H100 sm_90: no native NVFP4 tensor cores\n  exclude_lm_head_from_fp4: true\n\noptim:\n  # Per-rank batch. 8 ranks * micro_batch * grad_accum = effective batch.\n  # Set from H100 batch_probe; safe starting point.\n  micro_batch: 4\n  grad_accum: 1\n  lr: 5.0e-4\n  warmup_ratio: 0.05\n  warmup_min_steps: 50\n  lr_min_factor: 0.05\n  weight_decay: 0.01\n  max_grad_norm: 1.0\n\ntrain:\n  # 300K samples / (8 * 4 * 1) = ~9400 step/epoch; 1.6 epoch ≈ 15000 step.\n  steps: 15000\n  eval_every: 500\n  log_every: 10\n  ckpt_every: 2000   # full ckpt (resume-able) regardless of eval improvement\n  seed: 42\n  empty_cache_every: 100\n\nruntime:\n  output_user_4813494d: /output/runs\n  run_name: v3mix_300k_h100\n  dataloader_workers: 4\n  prefetch_queue: 2\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/docker/Dockerfile",
  "content": "# Multi-stage build for the scale training image.\n#\n# Build context MUST be the repo user_4813494d (/user_4813494d/openbmb), not scale/, so we can\n# COPY eagle/ alongside scale/. See docker/build_and_push.sh.\n#\n# Base image expectations:\n#   - PyTorch >= 2.3 with CUDA 12 (H100 sm_90 friendly)\n#   - safetensors, numpy, pyyaml, tqdm CAN be missing — we install them below\n#\n# Override BASE_IMAGE at build time if your platform mandates a different one,\n# e.g. --build-arg BASE_IMAGE=nvcr.io/nvidia/pytorch:24.10-py3\n\nARG BASE_IMAGE=pytorch/pytorch:2.4.0-cuda12.4-cudnn9-runtime\n\n# ── Stage 1: install Python deps from offline wheels ─────────────────────\nFROM ${BASE_IMAGE} AS deps\n\nWORKDIR /tmp/install\n\n# Copy wheels first so this layer cache survives code edits.\nCOPY scale/docker/wheels/ /tmp/install/wheels/\nCOPY scale/requirements.txt /tmp/install/requirements.txt\n\n# Offline install: --no-index + --find-links guarantees zero external network.\n# If wheels/ is empty (dev convenience) fall through to a normal install so\n# the build still works on the dev box; production builds populate wheels/ via\n# build_and_push.sh.\nRUN if [ \"$(ls -A /tmp/install/wheels 2>/dev/null)\" ]; then \\\n        pip install --no-cache-dir --no-index --find-links /tmp/install/wheels \\\n            -r /tmp/install/requirements.txt ; \\\n    else \\\n        echo \"WARN: wheels/ is empty, falling back to online install\" >&2 && \\\n        pip install --no-cache-dir -r /tmp/install/requirements.txt ; \\\n    fi\n\n\n# ── Stage 2: runtime image with code ─────────────────────────────────────\nFROM deps AS runtime\n\nWORKDIR /workspace\n\n# eagle/ provides Eagle3ModelV4 + nvfp4_codec + arch + fp4_quant — required at\n# import time by scale.core.trainer. Treated as read-only inside the container.\nCOPY eagle/ /workspace/eagle/\n\n# scale package + configs.\nCOPY scale/scale/ /workspace/scale/\nCOPY scale/configs/ /workspace/configs/\nCOPY scale/docker/entrypoint.sh /workspace/entrypoint.sh\nRUN chmod +x /workspace/entrypoint.sh\n\n# Both packages on path; importable as `import eagle` and `import scale`.\nENV PYTHONPATH=/workspace\nENV PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1\n# Use expandable segments to avoid fragmentation on long training runs.\nENV PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\n\n# Honor SCALE_ENTRY / SCALE_CONFIG / SCALE_NPROC at start time; see entrypoint.sh.\nENTRYPOINT [\"/workspace/entrypoint.sh\"]\n"
}

  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/docker/entrypoint.sh",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Single image, many entries. Platform sets env vars; we dispatch torchrun.\n#\n# Required env:\n#   SCALE_ENTRY    one of: train | smoke | overfit | batch_probe | profile\n#   SCALE_CONFIG   path under /workspace/configs/ (e.g. h100/train.yaml) OR\n#                  an absolute path inside the container\n#   SCALE_NPROC    GPUs per node, typically 8 on H100\n#\n# Optional env (defaults shown):\n#   SCALE_OUTPUT_user_4813494d=/output   (overrides cfg.runtime.output_user_4813494d only if a\n#                                separate launch wraps SCALE_CFG_OVERRIDES, which\n#                                this entrypoint does not — set output_user_4813494d in\n#                                the yaml instead)\n#   SCALE_EXTRA_ARGS=\"\"         extra torchrun flags\n#\n# torchrun init is rendezvous-less (--standalone) since we run single-node 8-GPU.\n# Multi-node would need RDZV_BACKEND / RDZV_ENDPOINT — out of scope here.\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nENTRY=\"${SCALE_ENTRY:-train}\"\nCONFIG=\"${SCALE_CONFIG:-h100/train.yaml}\"\nNPROC=\"${SCALE_NPROC:-8}\"\nEXTRA=\"${SCALE_EXTRA_ARGS:-}\"\n\n# Resolve CONFIG: if it isn't absolute, look under /workspace/configs/.\ncase \"${CONFIG}\" in\n    /*) CFG_PATH=\"${CONFIG}\" ;;\n    *)  CFG_PATH=\"/workspace/configs/${CONFIG}\" ;;\nesac\n\nif [[ ! -f \"${CFG_PATH}\" ]]; then\n    echo \"entrypoint: config not found: ${CFG_PATH}\" >&2\n    exit 2\nfi\n\n# Validate ENTRY is a real module before launching (cheap sanity vs torchrun's\n# late failure).\ncase \"${ENTRY}\" in\n    train|smoke|overfit|batch_probe|profile) ;;\n    *) echo \"entrypoint: unknown SCALE_ENTRY=${ENTRY}\" >&2; exit 2 ;;\nesac\n\ncd /workspace\n\necho \"============================================================\"\necho \"  scale entrypoint\"\necho \"  entry  : ${ENTRY}\"\necho \"  config : ${CFG_PATH}\"\necho \"  nproc  : ${NPROC}\"\necho \"  extra  : ${EXTRA}\"\necho \"============================================================\"\n\n# shellcheck disable=SC2086\nexec torchrun \\\n    --nproc_per_node=\"${NPROC}\" \\\n    --standalone \\\n    -m \"scale.cli.${ENTRY}\" \\\n    --config \"${CFG_PATH}\" \\\n    ${EXTRA}\n"
}
