{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/docker/build_and_push.sh",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# One-button build + tag (git sha) + push.\n#\n# Usage:\n#   bash scale/docker/build_and_push.sh [registry/image] [base_image_override]\n#\n# Examples:\n#   bash scale/docker/build_and_push.sh registry.internal/team/scale\n#   bash scale/docker/build_and_push.sh registry.internal/team/scale nvcr.io/nvidia/pytorch:24.10-py3\n#\n# Run from repo user_4813494d. The build context is the repo user_4813494d because Dockerfile\n# COPYs both eagle/ and scale/.\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nREPO_user_4813494d=\"$(git -C \"$(dirname \"$0\")\" rev-parse --show-toplevel)\"\ncd \"${REPO_user_4813494d}\"\n\nIMAGE=\"${1:-scale}\"\nBASE_IMAGE=\"${2:-pytorch/pytorch:2.4.0-cuda12.4-cudnn9-runtime}\"\n\nSHA=\"$(git rev-parse --short=12 HEAD)\"\nDIRTY=\"\"\nif [[ -n \"$(git status --porcelain)\" ]]; then\n    DIRTY=\"-dirty\"\nfi\nTAG=\"${IMAGE}:${SHA}${DIRTY}\"\n\nWHEELS_DIR=\"scale/docker/wheels\"\nmkdir -p \"${WHEELS_DIR}\"\n\n# Refresh offline wheels if we have external network. This is a one-shot\n# operation on the dev box; image builds inside the submission environment\n# read whatever wheels/ contains.\nif [[ \"${SCALE_SKIP_WHEEL_DOWNLOAD:-0}\" != \"1\" ]]; then\n    echo \"[build_and_push] downloading wheels to ${WHEELS_DIR}/ (set SCALE_SKIP_WHEEL_DOWNLOAD=1 to skip)...\"\n    pip download -r scale/requirements.txt -d \"${WHEELS_DIR}\" \\\n        --only-binary=:all: \\\n        --platform manylinux2014_x86_64 \\\n        --python-version 3.10 \\\n        --implementation cp \\\n        --abi cp310 \\\n        || echo \"[build_and_push] warn: wheel download failed; image build will fall back to online install\"\nfi\n\necho \"============================================================\"\necho \"  build:  ${TAG}\"\necho \"  base :  ${BASE_IMAGE}\"\necho \"============================================================\"\n\ndocker build \\\n    --file scale/docker/Dockerfile \\\n    --build-arg BASE_IMAGE=\"${BASE_IMAGE}\" \\\n    --tag \"${TAG}\" \\\n    .\n\nif [[ \"${SCALE_NO_PUSH:-0}\" == \"1\" ]]; then\n    echo \"[build_and_push] SCALE_NO_PUSH=1 set; skipping push\"\n    echo \"[build_and_push] image ready: ${TAG}\"\n    exit 0\nfi\n\nif [[ \"${IMAGE}\" == \"scale\" ]]; then\n    echo \"[build_and_push] image is local (no registry prefix); skipping push\"\n    echo \"[build_and_push] to push, pass registry/image as first arg\"\n    exit 0\nfi\n\necho \"[build_and_push] pushing ${TAG}...\"\ndocker push \"${TAG}\"\necho \"[build_and_push] done: ${TAG}\"\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/tools/extract_target_artifacts.py",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Extract embed_tokens + lm_head weights from the target model.\n\nThe full MiniCPM-SALA-NVFP4-det target is 8 GB+; the only tensors training\nneeds are the two embedding matrices (~1.2 GB combined). Extracting them once\nlocally and shipping a single safetensors file shaves >85% off the H100\nsync footprint.\n\nUsage:\n    python3 scale/tools/extract_target_artifacts.py \\\\\n        --src /user_4813494d/models/openbmb/MiniCPM-SALA-gptq46-calib90-90k-NVFP4-det \\\\\n        --dst /user_4813494d/data/eagle_stage/target_artifacts.safetensors\n\nThe dst file is what scale config target.artifacts_file should point at.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom safetensors import safe_open\nfrom safetensors.torch import save_file\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    ap.add_argument(\"--src\", required=True, type=Path,\n                    help=\"target model directory containing safetensors\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--dst\", required=True, type=Path,\n                    help=\"output safetensors path\")\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    if not args.src.is_dir():\n        raise FileNotFoundError(f\"src not a directory: {args.src}\")\n\n    index_path = args.src / \"model.safetensors.index.json\"\n    if index_path.exists():\n        index = json.loads(index_path.read_text())\n        wm = index[\"weight_map\"]\n        emb_file = args.src / wm[\"model.embed_tokens.weight\"]\n        lm_file = args.src / wm.get(\"lm_head.weight\", wm[\"model.embed_tokens.weight\"])\n    else:\n        emb_file = args.src / \"model.safetensors\"\n        lm_file = emb_file\n\n    out: dict = {}\n    with safe_open(emb_file, framework=\"pt\", device=\"cpu\") as f:\n        out[\"model.embed_tokens.weight\"] = f.get_tensor(\"model.embed_tokens.weight\").contiguous()\n    with safe_open(lm_file, framework=\"pt\", device=\"cpu\") as f:\n        keys = list(f.keys())\n        if \"lm_head.weight\" in keys:\n            out[\"lm_head.weight\"] = f.get_tensor(\"lm_head.weight\").contiguous()\n        else:\n            print(\"[extract] lm_head.weight not found, tying to embed_tokens\")\n            out[\"lm_head.weight\"] = out[\"model.embed_tokens.weight\"].clone()\n\n    args.dst.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    save_file(out, str(args.dst))\n\n    size_gb = args.dst.stat().st_size / 1024**3\n    print(f\"[extract] wrote {args.dst} ({size_gb:.2f} GiB)\")\n    for k, v in out.items():\n        print(f\"  {k}: {tuple(v.shape)} {v.dtype}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/tools/pre_sync_bos.py",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Sync the v3mix 300K segments from BOS to a node-local SSD.\n\nThe H100 platform syncs data once before training starts. This tool runs on\nthe H100 host (or by an init container) and pulls the BOS prefix down so the\ntraining image just sees local files.\n\nUsage on H100 host:\n    python3 tools/pre_sync_bos.py \\\\\n        --bos bos://anp3-common-model/vista/eagle3_data/v3mix_300k_det_len2048_topk256_aux1-10-22 \\\\\n        --dst /data/v3mix_300k \\\\\n        --bcecmd /usr/local/bin/bcecmd --bce-conf /etc/bce_conf \\\\\n        --workers 8\n\nThe sync is incremental: existing seg_NNNNNN directories with a valid\n_SUCCESS.json file are skipped. A missing seg_NNNNNN/_SUCCESS.json is\nre-downloaded.\n\nThis tool intentionally has zero Python deps beyond the standard library so\nit can run inside a minimum-base init container.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport concurrent.futures\nimport json\nimport os\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n\ndef run(cmd: list[str], timeout: float = 600.0) -> tuple[int, str]:\n    proc = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout)\n    return proc.returncode, (proc.stdout + proc.stderr)[:4000]\n\n\ndef list_remote_segments(bcecmd: str, bce_conf: str, prefix: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"List seg_NNNNNN/ entries under the BOS prefix.\"\"\"\n    rc, out = run([bcecmd, \"--conf-path\", bce_conf, \"bos\", \"ls\", prefix + \"/\"])\n    if rc != 0:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"bos ls failed: {out[:300]}\")\n    segments = []\n    for line in out.splitlines():\n        line = line.strip().rstrip(\"/\")\n        name = line.rsplit(\"/\", 1)[-1]\n        if name.startswith(\"seg_\") and len(name) == 10:\n            segments.append(name)\n    return sorted(set(segments))\n\n\ndef is_synced(local_seg_dir: Path) -> bool:\n    success = local_seg_dir / \"_SUCCESS.json\"\n    if not success.exists():\n        return False\n    try:\n        manifest = json.loads(success.read_text())\n        n_files = int(manifest[\"files\"])\n    except Exception:\n        return False\n    on_disk = len(list(local_seg_dir.glob(\"*.pt\")))\n    return on_disk == n_files\n\n\ndef sync_one(args: argparse.Namespace, seg: str) -> tuple[str, bool, str]:\n    dst = Path(args.dst) / seg\n    if is_synced(dst):\n        return seg, True, \"cached\"\n    dst.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    remote = f\"{args.bos.rstrip('/')}/{seg}/\"\n    cmd = [\n        args.bcecmd, \"--conf-path\", args.bce_conf,\n        \"bos\", \"cp\", \"-r\", remote, str(dst) + \"/\",\n    ]\n    rc, out = run(cmd, timeout=args.timeout)\n    if rc != 0:\n        return seg, False, out[:300]\n    if not is_synced(dst):\n        return seg, False, \"post-sync verify failed\"\n    return seg, True, \"ok\"\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    ap.add_argument(\"--bos\", required=True, help=\"BOS prefix, e.g. bos://bucket/path\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--dst\", required=True, help=\"local destination user_4813494d\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--bcecmd\", default=\"/usr/local/bin/bcecmd\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--bce-conf\", default=\"/etc/bce_conf\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--workers\", type=int, default=4)\n    ap.add_argument(\"--timeout\", type=float, default=3600)\n    ap.add_argument(\"--limit\", type=int, default=0, help=\"cap segments synced (0 = all)\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--start\", type=int, default=0, help=\"seg index to start at\")\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    Path(args.dst).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    print(f\"[pre_sync] listing remote segments under {args.bos}...\")\n    segments = list_remote_segments(args.bcecmd, args.bce_conf, args.bos)\n    if args.start > 0:\n        segments = [s for s in segments if int(s.split(\"_\")[1]) >= args.start]\n    if args.limit > 0:\n        segments = segments[: args.limit]\n    print(f\"[pre_sync] {len(segments)} segments to consider\")\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    ok = 0\n    fail = 0\n    cached = 0\n    with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.workers) as pool:\n        futures = {pool.submit(sync_one, args, s): s for s in segments}\n        for i, fut in enumerate(concurrent.futures.as_completed(futures), 1):\n            seg, success, msg = fut.result()\n            if success:\n                ok += 1\n                if msg == \"cached\":\n                    cached += 1\n            else:\n                fail += 1\n                print(f\"[pre_sync] FAIL {seg}: {msg}\", file=sys.stderr)\n            if i % 50 == 0 or i == len(segments):\n                rate = i / max(time.time() - t0, 1e-6)\n                print(f\"[pre_sync] {i}/{len(segments)} ok={ok} cached={cached} fail={fail} \"\n                      f\"({rate:.1f} seg/s, {(time.time()-t0)/60:.1f} min)\")\n\n    if fail:\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/tools/local_docker_dryrun.sh",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Local docker dry-run: build the scale image, then run overfit + smoke\n# entries inside it against locally-mounted data.\n#\n# Mirrors the H100 submission flow so a bug in Dockerfile / entrypoint / env\n# protocol blows up here, not after a 10-minute platform launch.\n#\n# Pre-reqs:\n#   * docker installed on this host\n#   * /user_4813494d/data and /user_4813494d/models accessible (default mount source)\n#   * GPU passthrough working (--gpus all)\n#\n# Usage:\n#   bash scale/tools/local_docker_dryrun.sh\n#\n# Skip rebuild:\n#   SCALE_DRY_SKIP_BUILD=1 bash scale/tools/local_docker_dryrun.sh\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nREPO_user_4813494d=\"$(git -C \"$(dirname \"$0\")\" rev-parse --show-toplevel)\"\ncd \"${REPO_user_4813494d}\"\n\nIMAGE=\"${SCALE_DRY_IMAGE:-scale:dryrun}\"\n\nif [[ \"${SCALE_DRY_SKIP_BUILD:-0}\" != \"1\" ]]; then\n    echo \"[dryrun] building ${IMAGE}...\"\n    SCALE_NO_PUSH=1 SCALE_SKIP_WHEEL_DOWNLOAD=1 \\\n        bash scale/docker/build_and_push.sh \"${IMAGE}\" \\\n        || { echo \"[dryrun] build failed\"; exit 1; }\nfi\n\nmkdir -p scale/runs\n\n# Path map inside the container:\n#   /data       <- /user_4813494d/data\n#   /models     <- /user_4813494d/models  (rare; configs typically only need /data)\n#   /workspace/runs <- /user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/runs  (run outputs)\n# Configs already in image at /workspace/configs/.\n\nrun_entry () {\n    local entry=\"$1\"\n    local config=\"$2\"\n    echo \"============================================================\"\n    echo \"  [dryrun] entry=${entry} config=${config}\"\n    echo \"============================================================\"\n    docker run --rm --gpus all \\\n        -v /user_4813494d/data:/data \\\n        -v /user_4813494d/models:/models \\\n        -v \"${REPO_user_4813494d}/scale/runs:/workspace/runs\" \\\n        -e SCALE_ENTRY=\"${entry}\" \\\n        -e SCALE_CONFIG=\"${config}\" \\\n        -e SCALE_NPROC=1 \\\n        \"${IMAGE}\"\n}\n\n# Note: configs/local/*.yaml are pre-built for /user_4813494d/data paths. Inside the\n# container we mount /user_4813494d/data at /data, so the data paths under cfg.data.*\n# need to swap /user_4813494d/data -> /data. The easy way is to use container-friendly\n# configs; for now we override by mounting the entire host data tree at the\n# same path:\ndocker run --rm --gpus all \\\n    -v /user_4813494d/data:/user_4813494d/data \\\n    -v /user_4813494d/models:/user_4813494d/models \\\n    -v \"${REPO_user_4813494d}/scale/runs:/workspace/runs\" \\\n    -e SCALE_ENTRY=overfit \\\n    -e SCALE_CONFIG=local/overfit.yaml \\\n    -e SCALE_NPROC=1 \\\n    \"${IMAGE}\"\n\ndocker run --rm --gpus all \\\n    -v /user_4813494d/data:/user_4813494d/data \\\n    -v /user_4813494d/models:/user_4813494d/models \\\n    -v \"${REPO_user_4813494d}/scale/runs:/workspace/runs\" \\\n    -e SCALE_ENTRY=smoke \\\n    -e SCALE_CONFIG=local/smoke.yaml \\\n    -e SCALE_NPROC=1 \\\n    \"${IMAGE}\"\n\necho \"[dryrun] all entries passed under image ${IMAGE}\"\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/scale/tests/test_config.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Config schema unit tests. CPU only.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport textwrap\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom scale.core.config import (\n    Config,\n    inject_model_env,\n    load_config,\n    resolve_run_dir,\n)\n\n\ndef _write(tmp: Path, text: str) -> Path:\n    p = tmp / \"cfg.yaml\"\n    p.write_text(textwrap.dedent(text))\n    return p\n\n\ndef test_minimal_loads(tmp_path):\n    cfg = load_config(_write(tmp_path, \"\"\"\n        data:\n          train_dir: /tmp/x\n        target:\n          model_dir: /tmp/y\n    \"\"\"))\n    assert isinstance(cfg, Config)\n    assert cfg.data.train_dir == \"/tmp/x\"\n    assert cfg.optim.micro_batch == 4  # default\n    assert cfg.model.ttt_steps == 3  # default\n\n\ndef test_unknown_top_level_key_rejected(tmp_path):\n    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=\"unknown config keys\"):\n        load_config(_write(tmp_path, \"\"\"\n            data:\n              train_dir: /tmp/x\n            target:\n              model_dir: /tmp/y\n            unicorn: 42\n        \"\"\"))\n\n\ndef test_unknown_nested_key_rejected(tmp_path):\n    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=\"unknown config keys\"):\n        load_config(_write(tmp_path, \"\"\"\n            data:\n              train_dir: /tmp/x\n              wrong_key: 1\n            target:\n              model_dir: /tmp/y\n        \"\"\"))\n\n\ndef test_missing_target_rejected(tmp_path):\n    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=\"target\"):\n        load_config(_write(tmp_path, \"\"\"\n            data:\n              train_dir: /tmp/x\n            target: {}\n        \"\"\"))\n\n\ndef test_bad_batch_rejected(tmp_path):\n    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=\"micro_batch\"):\n        load_config(_write(tmp_path, \"\"\"\n            data:\n              train_dir: /tmp/x\n            target:\n              model_dir: /tmp/y\n            optim:\n              micro_batch: 0\n        \"\"\"))\n\n\ndef test_inject_env_sets_eagle_vars(tmp_path, monkeypatch):\n    # Ensure clean slate.\n    monkeypatch.delenv(\"EAGLE_DRAFT_TTT_STEPS\", raising=False)\n    monkeypatch.delenv(\"EAGLE_NVFP4_FORWARD\", raising=False)\n    cfg = load_config(_write(tmp_path, \"\"\"\n        data:\n          train_dir: /tmp/x\n        target:\n          model_dir: /tmp/y\n        model:\n          ttt_steps: 5\n          nvfp4_forward: false\n    \"\"\"))\n    inject_model_env(cfg)\n    import os\n    assert os.environ[\"EAGLE_DRAFT_TTT_STEPS\"] == \"5\"\n    assert os.environ[\"EAGLE_NVFP4_FORWARD\"] == \"0\"\n\n\ndef test_run_dir_includes_name_and_sha(tmp_path):\n    cfg_path = _write(tmp_path, \"\"\"\n        data:\n          train_dir: /tmp/x\n        target:\n          model_dir: /tmp/y\n        runtime:\n          output_user_4813494d: /tmp/out\n          run_name: hello\n    \"\"\")\n    cfg = load_config(cfg_path)\n    rd = resolve_run_dir(cfg, cfg_path)\n    assert str(rd).startswith(\"/tmp/out/hello__\")\n    # has sha + timestamp segments\n    parts = rd.name.split(\"__\")\n    assert len(parts) == 3\n"
}

user_4813494d via\n# conftest fallback below.\n_user_4813494d = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]\nif str(_user_4813494d) not in sys.path:\n    sys.path.insert(0, str(_user_4813494d))\n\nfrom scale.core.dataset import (  # noqa: E402\n    CollateCfg,\n    FixedRepeatSampler,\n    NVFP4Dataset,\n    ResumableDistributedSampler,\n    enumerate_files,\n    make_collate,\n)\n\n\n# ── Fixtures ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef _make_fake_sample(n_tokens: int = 32, aux_dim: int = 12288) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Build a synthetic sample shaped like a real v3mix .pt file.\"\"\"\n    return {\n        \"token_ids\": torch.randint(0, 73000, (n_tokens,), dtype=torch.int64),\n        \"aux_hidden\": torch.randn(n_tokens, aux_dim, dtype=torch.bfloat16),\n        \"top_logit_values\": torch.randn(n_tokens, 128, dtype=torch.bfloat16),\n        \"top_logit_indices\": torch.randint(0, 73000, (n_tokens, 128), dtype=torch.int32),\n        \"target_logsumexp\": torch.randn(n_tokens, dtype=torch.float32),\n        \"assistant_mask\": torch.ones(n_tokens, dtype=torch.bool),\n        \"document_ids\": torch.zeros(n_tokens, dtype=torch.int32),\n    }\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef fake_data_dir(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Two synthetic .pt files small enough for fast unit tests.\"\"\"\n    for i in range(2):\n        torch.save(_make_fake_sample(n_tokens=64), tmp_path / f\"sample_{i:04d}.pt\")\n    return tmp_path\n\n\n# ── Tests ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef test_enumerate_files(fake_data_dir):\n    files = enumerate_files(fake_data_dir)\n    assert len(files) == 2\n    assert all(f.suffix == \".pt\" for f in files)\n\n\ndef test_enumerate_files_missing_dir(tmp_path):\n    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError):\n        enumerate_files(tmp_path / \"does-not-exist\")\n\n\ndef test_dataset_loads_and_decodes(fake_data_dir):\n    ds = NVFP4Dataset(enumerate_files(fake_data_dir))\n    s = ds[0]\n    assert s[\"token_ids\"].dtype == torch.int64\n    assert s[\"aux_hidden\"].dtype == torch.bfloat16\n    assert s[\"aux_hidden\"].shape == (64, 12288)\n    assert \"document_ids\" in s\n\n\ndef test_dataset_skips_short_file(tmp_path):\n    torch.save(_make_fake_sample(n_tokens=2), tmp_path / \"tiny.pt\")\n    ds = NVFP4Dataset([tmp_path / \"tiny.pt\"], min_tokens=16)\n    s = ds[0]\n    assert s.get(\"_skip\") is True\n\n\ndef test_collate_pads_shorter_to_max(fake_data_dir):\n    ds = NVFP4Dataset(enumerate_files(fake_data_dir))\n    cfg = CollateCfg(seq_len_max=64, pin_memory=False)\n    collate = make_collate(cfg)\n    batch = collate([ds[0], ds[1]])\n    assert batch[\"token_ids\"].shape == (2, 64)\n    assert batch[\"aux_hidden\"].shape == (2, 64, 12288)\n    assert batch[\"aux_hidden\"].dtype == torch.bfloat16\n    assert batch[\"is_packed\"] is False\n\n\ndef test_collate_truncates_longer_to_max(tmp_path):\n    torch.save(_make_fake_sample(n_tokens=128), tmp_path / \"long.pt\")\n    ds = NVFP4Dataset([tmp_path / \"long.pt\"])\n    cfg = CollateCfg(seq_len_max=64, pin_memory=False)\n    batch = make_collate(cfg)([ds[0]])\n    assert batch[\"token_ids\"].shape == (1, 64)\n\n\ndef test_collate_drops_skip_sentinels(tmp_path):\n    torch.save(_make_fake_sample(n_tokens=2), tmp_path / \"tiny.pt\")\n    torch.save(_make_fake_sample(n_tokens=32), tmp_path / \"ok.pt\")\n    ds = NVFP4Dataset([tmp_path / \"tiny.pt\", tmp_path / \"ok.pt\"], min_tokens=8)\n    cfg = CollateCfg(seq_len_max=32, pin_memory=False)\n    batch = make_collate(cfg)([ds[0], ds[1]])\n    assert batch is not None\n    assert batch[\"token_ids\"].shape == (1, 32)\n\n\ndef test_collate_returns_none_on_all_skip(tmp_path):\n    torch.save(_make_fake_sample(n_tokens=2), tmp_path / \"tiny.pt\")\n    ds = NVFP4Dataset([tmp_path / \"tiny.pt\"], min_tokens=8)\n    cfg = CollateCfg(seq_len_max=32, pin_memory=False)\n    batch = make_collate(cfg)([ds[0]])\n    assert batch is None\n\n\ndef test_resumable_sampler_skips_on_set_state(fake_data_dir):\n    # Build a dataset of 8 fake files.\n    for i in range(8):\n        torch.save(_make_fake_sample(n_tokens=16), fake_data_dir / f\"r{i:04d}.pt\")\n    ds = NVFP4Dataset(enumerate_files(fake_data_dir))\n    sampler = ResumableDistributedSampler(ds, num_replicas=1, rank=0, shuffle=False, drop_last=False)\n    sampler.set_state(epoch=0, start_index=3)\n    seen = list(sampler)\n    assert len(seen) == len(ds) - 3\n    # One epoch later, start fresh.\n    sampler.set_state(epoch=1, start_index=0)\n    seen2 = list(sampler)\n    assert len(seen2) == len(ds)\n\n\ndef test_fixed_repeat_sampler_cycles_with_shuffle():\n    s = FixedRepeatSampler([0, 1, 2, 3], seed=0)\n    a = list(s)\n    b = list(s)\n    # Both are permutations of the full support.\n    assert sorted(a) == [0, 1, 2, 3]\n    assert sorted(b) == [0, 1, 2, 3]\n    # Different epochs probabilistically shuffle differently (4! = 24 perms,\n    # 1/24 chance of accidental equality; flake-resistant by checking >=1 of 3).\n    c = list(s)\n    assert not (a == b == c)\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/scale/tests/test_ckpt.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Checkpoint roundtrip tests. CPU only — no DDP.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\n\nfrom scale.core import ckpt as ckpt_mod\n\n\nclass _TinyModel(nn.Module):\n    \"\"\"Mimics the wrapping shape: a few params + one buffer.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.linear = nn.Linear(8, 4)\n        self.register_buffer(\"t2d\", torch.zeros(16, dtype=torch.bool))\n        # Mock the frozen embed_tokens that the save path filters out.\n        self.embed_tokens = nn.Embedding(32, 4)\n        self.embed_tokens.requires_grad_(False)\n\n\ndef test_save_and_load_roundtrip(tmp_path: Path):\n    m = _TinyModel()\n    opt = torch.optim.AdamW(m.parameters(), lr=1e-3)\n    # Take one step so optim state is non-empty.\n    loss = m.linear(torch.randn(2, 8)).sum()\n    loss.backward()\n    opt.step()\n\n    state = ckpt_mod.ResumeState(\n        global_step=42, epoch=2, sample_index=99,\n        best_metric=0.75, best_step=40,\n    )\n    m.t2d[5] = True\n\n    path = ckpt_mod.save(tmp_path, \"best.pt\", m, opt, state, extra={\"foo\": \"bar\"})\n    assert path is not None and path.exists()\n\n    # Re-instantiate.\n    m2 = _TinyModel()\n    opt2 = torch.optim.AdamW(m2.parameters(), lr=1e-3)\n    loaded = ckpt_mod.load(path, m2, opt2)\n\n    # State recovered.\n    assert loaded.global_step == 42\n    assert loaded.epoch == 2\n    assert loaded.sample_index == 99\n    assert loaded.best_metric == 0.75\n    assert loaded.best_step == 40\n\n    # Linear weights match.\n    assert torch.equal(m.linear.weight, m2.linear.weight)\n    assert torch.equal(m.linear.bias, m2.linear.bias)\n    # Buffers preserved.\n    assert m2.t2d[5].item() is True\n\n\ndef test_save_excludes_embed_tokens(tmp_path: Path):\n    m = _TinyModel()\n    opt = torch.optim.AdamW(m.parameters(), lr=1e-3)\n    state = ckpt_mod.ResumeState()\n    path = ckpt_mod.save(tmp_path, \"best.pt\", m, opt, state)\n    ck = torch.load(path, weights_only=False)\n    keys = list(ck[\"model_state_dict\"].keys())\n    assert not any(k.startswith(\"embed_tokens\") for k in keys)\n    assert \"linear.weight\" in keys\n"
}

  "content": "\"\"\"Distributed helpers — covers the world_size=1 fallback paths.\n\nTrue multi-rank behavior requires torchrun + NCCL and is exercised via the\nsmoke entry, not pytest. CPU-only tests here focus on the no-op branches.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport torch\n\nfrom scale.core import dist as dist_mod\n\n\ndef test_state_defaults_to_rank0():\n    s = dist_mod.state()\n    assert s[\"world_size\"] == 1\n    assert s[\"rank\"] == 0\n    assert s[\"local_rank\"] == 0\n\n\ndef test_is_rank0_true_in_single_process():\n    assert dist_mod.is_rank0() is True\n\n\ndef test_reduce_mean_passthrough_world1():\n    # No CUDA needed because the world_size=1 branch never touches the device.\n    val = dist_mod.reduce_mean(0.42, torch.device(\"cpu\"))\n    assert abs(val - 0.42) < 1e-6\n\n\ndef test_reduce_sum_passthrough_world1():\n    val = dist_mod.reduce_sum(7.0, torch.device(\"cpu\"))\n    assert val == 7.0\n\n\ndef test_no_sync_falls_through_on_plain_module():\n    \"\"\"no_sync on a plain nn.Module shouldn't blow up — it just no-ops.\"\"\"\n    import torch.nn as nn\n    m = nn.Linear(4, 4)\n    with dist_mod.no_sync(m):\n        pass\n\n\ndef test_broadcast_object_passthrough_world1():\n    assert dist_mod.broadcast_object({\"a\": 1}) == {\"a\": 1}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/scale/tests/test_nvfp4_codec_roundtrip.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Sanity that the NVFP4 codec we depend on still round-trips bit-exact.\n\nThis is a guard test: if eagle.nvfp4_codec ever drifts (e.g. someone changes\nthe FP4 bucketize boundaries), our dataset.decompress_sample silently changes\nits decoded output and training accuracy moves without an obvious cause.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport sys\n\n_user_4813494d = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]\nif str(_user_4813494d) not in sys.path:\n    sys.path.insert(0, str(_user_4813494d))\n\nimport torch  # noqa: E402\n\nfrom eagle import nvfp4_codec  # noqa: E402\n\n\ndef test_encode_decode_bit_exact_small():\n    torch.manual_seed(0)\n    x = torch.randn(64, 4096, dtype=torch.bfloat16) * 2.0\n    packed, scale = nvfp4_codec.encode(x)\n    decoded = nvfp4_codec.decode(packed, scale)\n    # Reference: same algorithm done end-to-end in bf16.\n    *_, dim = x.shape\n    grouped = x.reshape(*x.shape[:-1], dim // nvfp4_codec.FP4_GROUP_SIZE, nvfp4_codec.FP4_GROUP_SIZE)\n    s_ref = (grouped.abs().amax(-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-8) / nvfp4_codec.FP4_MAX).to(torch.bfloat16)\n    bounds = nvfp4_codec._FP4_BOUNDS.to(dtype=x.dtype)\n    pos = nvfp4_codec._FP4_POS.to(dtype=x.dtype)\n    q = grouped / s_ref\n    sign = q.sign()\n    idx = torch.bucketize(q.abs().clamp(max=nvfp4_codec.FP4_MAX), bounds)\n    ref = (sign * pos[idx] * s_ref).reshape(*x.shape)\n    assert torch.equal(decoded, ref), \"NVFP4 codec drift detected\"\n\n\ndef test_compress_decompress_dict_preserves_metadata():\n    raw = {\n        \"token_ids\": torch.randint(0, 73000, (32,), dtype=torch.int64),\n        \"aux_hidden\": torch.randn(32, 12288, dtype=torch.bfloat16),\n        \"assistant_mask\": torch.ones(32, dtype=torch.bool),\n        \"document_ids\": torch.zeros(32, dtype=torch.int32),\n        \"top_logit_values\": torch.randn(32, 128, dtype=torch.bfloat16),\n        \"top_logit_indices\": torch.randint(0, 73000, (32, 128), dtype=torch.int32),\n        \"target_logsumexp\": torch.randn(32, dtype=torch.float32),\n    }\n    compressed = nvfp4_codec.compress_sample(raw)\n    assert \"aux_packed\" in compressed and \"aux_scale\" in compressed\n    assert \"aux_hidden\" not in compressed\n    decompressed = nvfp4_codec.decompress_sample(compressed)\n    assert \"aux_hidden\" in decompressed\n    assert decompressed[\"aux_hidden\"].dtype == torch.bfloat16\n    assert decompressed[\"aux_hidden\"].shape == (32, 12288)\n    # Metadata round-trips\n    assert torch.equal(decompressed[\"token_ids\"], raw[\"token_ids\"])\n    assert torch.equal(decompressed[\"assistant_mask\"], raw[\"assistant_mask\"])\n"
}

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - Initial: Deep-read EAGLE work, with note that v3mix 300K dataset has been collected
   - Migration goal: Move SALA EAGLE-3 draft training from local single-GPU (RTX 6000D sm_120) to H100×8 production servers via task submission (no interactive debugging)
   - Critical constraint: All scripts MUST be developed and tested locally before submission, since H100 platform is submission-only with no debug access
   - Must implement: multi-GPU parallel framework, batch_probe, profile, smoke_train, overfit, train scripts
   - Each must run BOTH locally (validate) AND on H100 (production)
   - Avoid jumping directly to `train` - validation gates: overfit → smoke → batch_probe → profile → only then train
   - Quality bar: "正确实现 → 高效实现 → 优雅实现 → 最优实现"
   - User explicitly stated: "你自己来操作 仔细思考 设计 在本地完成实现 ... 本地测试 无需征求我的意见 请完成所有本地工作"
   - Naming/structure feedback: User rejected `h100_train` as "ugly" (couples hardware vendor); rejected too-flat structure (wanted multi-layer)
   - Environment constraints (user feedback): training does NOT need full sgl/cuDNN/cu13 inference stack; H100 platform is "Docker image push, platform pulls"; data syncs to local SSD before training; H100 force bf16 fake STE (no NVFP4 tensor cores on sm_90); submission environment has NO external network (only internal BOS/registry)

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - SALA EAGLE-3 draft model with LK^λ loss, AOI position IDs, response-only mask, cross-doc attention
   - NVFP4 storage codec (eagle.nvfp4_codec) for aux_hidden compression (3.2× ratio)
   - PyTorch DistributedDataParallel (DDP) with gradient_as_bucket_view + static_graph
   - torchrun launcher with --standalone for single-node
   - no_sync context manager for gradient accumulation efficiency
   - ResumableDistributedSampler with set_state(epoch, sample_index)
   - bf16 fake-STE forward (_FP4QuantSTE) vs NVFP4 native forward (_NVFP4LinearFn)
   - fp4_quant_freeze/unfreeze for TTT-step weight cache
   - Cosine LR with warmup
   - jsonl-only logging (no wandb due to no external network)
   - Multi-stage Docker build with offline wheels (--no-index --find-links)
   - Env-only entrypoint protocol (SCALE_ENTRY/SCALE_CONFIG/SCALE_NPROC)

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - `eagle/training/sala_draft/train.py` (read for understanding)
     - Defines Eagle3ModelV4 which extends v3train.Eagle3Model

   - `eagle/legacy/v2_v3/train.py` (read)

   - `scale/pyproject.toml` (created)

196	# ── LK^λ loss ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
197	
198	def lk_lambda_loss_topk(target_logits_values: torch.Tensor,
199	                        target_logits_indices: torch.Tensor,
200	                        target_logsumexp: torch.Tensor | None,
201	                        draft_logits: torch.Tensor,
202	                        t2d: torch.Tensor,
203	                        draft_idx_map: torch.Tensor,
204	                        kl_scale: float = LK_KL_SCALE,
205	                        kl_decay: float = LK_KL_DECAY,
206	                        mask: torch.Tensor | None = None) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
207	    """LK^lambda loss on the collected target top-K support.
208	
209	    Equivalent to dense-scatter over topK∩draft-vocab but never materializes
210	    a (B, S, full_vocab) tensor. ``draft_logits`` may be bf16 or fp32; reduce-
211	    style ops upcast internally and we only realize fp32 on K=128 slices.
212	    """
213	    indices = target_logits_indices.long().clamp(0, VOCAB_SIZE - 1)
214	    in_draft = t2d[indices]
215	    mapped_idx = draft_idx_map[indices]
216	    target_values = target_logits_values.float()
217	
218	    # Fuse 5 where(...)s + logsumexp into one masked log_softmax + boolean
219	    # multiplies. F.log_softmax computes (logits - logsumexp(logits)) in one
220	    # fused kernel; the masked-out positions sit at -inf in support_logits
221	    # so they end up at -inf in log_p_support, and we zero-mask before any
222	    # arithmetic that would propagate -inf into NaN.
223	    neg_inf = torch.finfo(target_values.dtype).min
224	    in_draft_f = in_draft.to(target_values.dtype)   # (B, S, K) float mask
225	    support_logits = torch.where(in_draft, target_values, neg_inf)
226	    log_p_support = F.log_softmax(support_logits, dim=-1)
227	    # Zero-mask before downstream products so 0 * -inf doesn't appear.
228	    log_p_support = log_p_support * in_draft_f
229	    p_support = log_p_support.exp() * in_draft_f
230	
231	    # Draft normalizer: logsumexp upcasts to fp32 internally even for bf16
232	    # input; we then keep it in fp32 for KL math. The full (B,S,V) tensor is
233	    # never realized as fp32.
234	    draft_log_z = torch.logsumexp(draft_logits, dim=-1, keepdim=True).float()
235	    support_log_q = draft_logits.gather(2, mapped_idx).float() - draft_log_z
236	    kl = (p_support * (log_p_support - support_log_q) * in_draft_f).sum(dim=-1)
237	
238	    if target_logsumexp is None:
239	        full_log_z = torch.logsumexp(target_values, dim=-1)
240	    else:
241	        full_log_z = target_logsumexp.float()
242	    p_on_draft_support = torch.exp(target_values - full_log_z.unsqueeze(-1)) * in_draft_f
243	    q_support = support_log_q.exp() * in_draft_f
244	    alpha = torch.minimum(p_on_draft_support, q_support).sum(dim=-1)
245	
246	    if mask is not None:
247	        m = mask.float()
248	        n_valid = m.sum().clamp(min=1.0)
249	        kl_loss = (kl * m).sum() / n_valid
250	        acceptance_rate = (alpha * m).sum() / n_valid
251	    else:
252	        kl_loss = kl.mean()
253	        acceptance_rate = alpha.mean()
254	    kl_weight = kl_scale * torch.exp(-kl_decay * acceptance_rate.detach())
255	    loss = kl_weight * kl_loss + (1.0 - kl_weight) * (1.0 - acceptance_rate)
256	    return loss, acceptance_rate.detach()
257	
258	
259	# ── V4 Model: extends v3 model with cross-doc + assistant mask aware forward ──
260	
261	class Eagle3ModelV4(v3train.Eagle3Model):
262	    """Extends v3 Eagle3Model: AOI + LK^λ + response-only mask + cross-doc attn.
263	
264	    forward() accepts additional inputs: assistant_mask, document_ids.
265	    """
266	
267	    def __init__(self, embed_weight, lm_head_weight):
268	        super().__init__(embed_weight, lm_head_weight)
269	        # Override RoPE cache to support large positions (226K). Cast to the
270	        # training dtype so RoPE multiplies stay in bf16 (avoids implicit
271	        # fp32 promotion against bf16 q/k).
272	        cos, sin = v3train._build_rope_cache(HEAD_DIM, max_seq=ROPE_MAX_SEQ, theta=ROPE_THETA)
273	        self.midlayer.self_attn.rope_cos = cos.to(DEVICE).to(DTYPE)
274	        self.midlayer.self_attn.rope_sin = sin.to(DEVICE).to(DTYPE)
275	        self.ttt_steps = TTT_STEPS
276	        self.loss_decay = LOSS_DECAY
277	
278	    def load_v4_vocab_cache(self, cache_path: Path):
279	        """Override v3's build_vocab_mapping with force-included d2t."""
280	        d = torch.load(cache_path, weights_only=True)
281	        d2t = d["d2t"]
282	        selected_ids = d["selected_ids"]
283	        # t2d: target_id → True if in draft subset
284	        t2d = torch.zeros(VOCAB_SIZE, dtype=torch.bool)
285	        t2d[selected_ids] = True

408	                    )
409	            else:
410	                hidden_out, cache_k_list, cache_v_list = self.midlayer(
411	                    input_emb, hidden, cache_k_list, cache_v_list,
412	                    causal_mask=None, position_ids=position_ids,
413	                    packed_attn_mask=per_step_masks[step],
414	                )
415	            hidden = hidden_out
416	
417	            with torch.no_grad():
418	                target_argmax_full = target_indices[:, :, 0].long().clamp(0, VOCAB_SIZE - 1)
419	                target_in_draft = self.t2d[target_argmax_full]
420	                if assistant_mask is not None:
421	                    mask = target_in_draft & assistant_mask.bool()
422	                else:
423	                    mask = target_in_draft
424	
425	            normed = self.norm(hidden_out)
426	            # Keep logits in bf16 — reductions inside the loss upcast as needed.
427	            logits = self.lm_head(normed)
428	
429	            loss, _ = lk_lambda_loss_topk(
430	                target_values, target_indices, target_logsumexp,
431	                logits, self.t2d, self.draft_idx_map, mask=mask,
432	            )
433	            step_losses.append(loss)
434	
435	            with torch.no_grad():
436	                pred_idx = logits.argmax(-1)
437	                target_draft_idx = self.draft_idx_map[target_argmax_full]
438	                correct = (pred_idx == target_draft_idx).float() * mask.float()
439	                n_correct = correct.sum().item()
440	                n_valid = mask.float().sum().item()
441	                step_corrects.append(n_correct)
442	                step_valids.append(n_valid)
443	                step_accs.append(n_correct / (n_valid + 1e-6))
444	
445	        total_loss = sum(self.loss_decay ** i * l for i, l in enumerate(step_losses))
446	        # Release the FP4 cache; weights will be re-quantized on next forward.
447	        v3train.fp4_quant_unfreeze(self)
448	        if return_stats:
449	            return total_loss, step_losses, step_accs, {
450	                "correct": step_corrects, "valid": step_valids,
451	            }
452	        return total_loss, step_losses, step_accs
453	
454	
455	# ── V4 dataloader (bf16 or NVFP4-aux .pt) ──────────────────────────────
456	
457	def load_sample_v4(pt_path: Path) -> dict:
458	    """Load one v4 .pt on CPU and decode NVFP4 aux storage if present."""
459	    sample = torch.load(pt_path, weights_only=True, map_location="cpu")
460	    if nvfp4_codec.is_nvfp4_sample(sample):
461	        sample = nvfp4_codec.decompress_sample(sample)
462	    return sample
463	
464

1	"""Unified train loop shared by every cli entry.
2	
3	Differences from the legacy single-GPU trainer:
4	  * DDP-aware (NCCL allreduce, no_sync in GA inner loop, rank0-only eval/ckpt)
5	  * Resume covers (model, optim, sampler, rng, step) — no more 'resume from
6	    step 0 with stale data position' silent failure mode
7	  * No interactive flags; all config lives in yaml
8	  * jsonl logging, no rich/tqdm dependency in the hot path
9	  * bf16 fake-STE forward by default (H100 has no NVFP4 tensor cores); the
10	    sm_120 NVFP4 forward path is still reachable via model.nvfp4_forward=true
11	
12	Public surface:
13	    build_runtime(cfg, run_dir) -> Runtime
14	    train_loop(runtime, mode='train'|'smoke'|'overfit', max_steps=None)
15	    evaluate(runtime, split='ind'|'ood', max_files=...) -> list[float] | None
16	"""
17	from __future__ import annotations
18	
19	import math
20	import os
21	import time
22	from contextlib import nullcontext
23	from dataclasses import dataclass
24	from pathlib import Path
25	from typing import Any
26	
27	import torch
28	import torch.nn as nn
29	from safetensors import safe_open
30	from torch.nn.parallel import DistributedDataParallel as DDP
31	
32	from scale.core import ckpt as ckpt_mod
33	from scale.core import dist as dist_mod
34	from scale.core.config import Config, inject_model_env
35	from scale.core.dataset import (
36	    NVFP4Dataset,
37	    batch_to_cuda,
38	    build_eval_loader,
39	    build_train_loader,
40	    enumerate_files,
41	)
42	from scale.core.logging import JsonlLogger, banner
43	
44	# Eagle imports happen lazily inside _build_model so inject_model_env runs first.
45	
46	
47	# ── Runtime container ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
48	
49	
50	@dataclass
51	class Runtime:
52	    cfg: Config
53	    run_dir: Path
54	    device: torch.device
55	    model: nn.Module  # wrapped (DDP or raw)
56	    raw_model: nn.Module  # unwrapped for buffer access
57	    optimizer: torch.optim.Optimizer
58	    train_loader: Any
59	    train_sampler: Any
60	    val_ind_files: list[Path]
61	    val_ood_files: list[Path]
62	    state: ckpt_mod.ResumeState
63	    logger: JsonlLogger
64	
65	
66	# ── Build ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
67	
68	
69	def _load_target_artifacts(cfg: Config) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
70	    """Load embed + lm_head weights from the configured target source.
71	
72	    Prefers the small artifacts_file extracted by tools/extract_target_artifacts.py
73	    (two tensors, ~1.2 GB) over the full target model dir (8+ GB, NVFP4-packed).
74	    """
75	    af = cfg.target.artifacts_file
76	    if af and Path(af).exists():
77	        with safe_open(af, framework="pt", device="cpu") as f:
78	            embed = f.get_tensor("model.embed_tokens.weight").float()
79	            lm = f.get_tensor("lm_head.weight").float()
80	        return embed, lm
81	
82	    if not cfg.target.model_dir:
83	        raise FileNotFoundError(
84	            f"target.artifacts_file does not exist ({af}) and target.model_dir is empty"
85	        )
86	    # Fallback: walk the full safetensors index (mirrors v3train.load_embed_and_lm_head).
87	    import json
88	    md = Path(cfg.target.model_dir)
89	    index_path = md / "model.safetensors.index.json"
90	    if index_path.exists():
91	        index = json.loads(index_path.read_text())
92	        wm = index["weight_map"]
93	        with safe_open(md / wm["model.embed_tokens.weight"], framework="pt", device="cpu") as f:
94	            embed = f.get_tensor("model.embed_tokens.weight").float()
95	        lm_file = wm.get("lm_head.weight")
96	        if lm_file:
97	            with safe_open(md / lm_file, framework="pt", device="cpu") as f:
98	                lm = f.get_tensor("lm_head.weight").float()
99	        else:
100	            lm = embed.clone()
101	    else:
102	        with safe_open(md / "model.safetensors", framework="pt", device="cpu") as f:
103	            embed = f.get_tensor("model.embed_tokens.weight").float()
104	            try:
105	                lm = f.get_tensor("lm_head.weight").float()
106	            except Exception:
107	                lm = embed.clone()
108	    return embed, lm
109	
110	
111	def _init_mlp_warm_start(model: nn.Module, cfg: Config) -> int:
112	    """Optionally warm-start MLP/o_proj from a BF16 target layer.
113	
114	    Returns the number of tensors successfully copied. Skips silently if the
115	    BF16 target dir is missing or empty.
116	    """
117	    src = cfg.target.bf16_model_dir
118	    if not src or not Path(src).is_dir():
119	        return 0
120	    import json
121	    index_path = Path(src) / "model.safetensors.index.json"
122	    if not index_path.exists():
123	        return 0
124	    index = json.loads(index_path.read_text())
125	    wm = index["weight_map"]
126	    pfx = f"model.layers.{cfg.target.init_layer}"
127	    raw = model.module if hasattr(model, "module") else model
128	    layer_map = {
129	        f"{pfx}.mlp.gate_proj.weight": raw.midlayer.mlp.gate_proj.weight,
130	        f"{pfx}.mlp.up_proj.weight": raw.midlayer.mlp.up_proj.weight,
131	        f"{pfx}.mlp.down_proj.weight": raw.midlayer.mlp.down_proj.weight,
132	        f"{pfx}.self_attn.o_proj.weight": raw.midlayer.self_attn.o_proj.weight,
133	    }
134	    loaded = 0
135	    for key, param in layer_map.items():
136	        if key not in wm:
137	            continue
138	        with safe_open(Path(src) / wm[key], framework="pt", device="cpu") as f:
139	            w = f.get_tensor(key).to(torch.bfloat16)
140	        if w.shape == param.shape:
141	            with torch.no_grad():
142	                param.copy_(w.to(param.device))
143	            loaded += 1
144	    return loaded
145	
146	
147	def _build_model(cfg: Config, device: torch.device) -> tuple[nn.Module, nn.Module]:
148	    """Build the wrapped (DDP) and unwrapped model.
149	
150	    Imports eagle.* lazily, after inject_model_env has run, so the trainer's
151	    module-level constants reflect the config.
152	    """
153	    # Imports here, not at file top, so config-driven env vars are honored.
154	    from eagle.training.sala_draft.train import Eagle3ModelV4  # noqa: E402
155	
156	    embed_w, lm_head_w = _load_target_artifacts(cfg)
157	
158	    raw = Eagle3ModelV4(embed_w, lm_head_w).to(device).to(torch.bfloat16)
159	    if cfg.data.vocab_cache:
160	        raw.load_v4_vocab_cache(Path(cfg.data.vocab_cache))
161	    n_warm = _init_mlp_warm_start(raw, cfg)
162	    if dist_mod.is_rank0():
163	        print(f"[scale] MLP warm-start tensors loaded: {n_warm}")
164	
165	    state = dist_mod.state()
166	    if state["world_size"] > 1:
167	        # static_graph=True requires the autograd graph to be identical across
168	        # iterations — which our TTT-fixed loop satisfies. gradient_as_bucket_view
169	        # cuts a copy on each allreduce. find_unused_parameters=False is the
170	        # default but we make it explicit for clarity.
171	        model = DDP(
172	            raw,
173	            device_ids=[state["local_rank"]],
174	            output_device=state["local_rank"],
175	            gradient_as_bucket_view=True,
176	            static_graph=True,
177	            find_unused_parameters=False,
178	        )
179	    else:
180	        model = raw
181	
182	    return model, raw
183	
184	
185	def _build_optimizer(model: nn.Module, cfg: Config) -> torch.optim.Optimizer:
186	    """AdamW with weight-decay split. Same convention as legacy trainer."""
187	    decay, no_decay = [], []
188	    for name, p in model.named_parameters():
189	        if not p.requires_grad:
190	            continue
191	        if name.endswith(".bias") or name.endswith("norm.weight"):
192	            no_decay.append(p)
193	        else:
194	            decay.append(p)
195	    return torch.optim.AdamW(
196	        [
197	            {"params": decay, "weight_decay": cfg.optim.weight_decay},
198	            {"params": no_decay, "weight_decay": 0.0},
199	        ],
200	        lr=cfg.optim.lr,
201	        betas=(cfg.optim.beta1, cfg.optim.beta2),
202	        fused=True,
203	    )
204	
205	
206	def _resolve_files(cfg: Config) -> tuple[list[Path], list[Path], list[Path]]:
207	    train_files = enumerate_files(cfg.data.train_dir, max_files=cfg.data.train_max_files)
208	    val_ind = []
209	    val_ood = []
210	    if cfg.data.val_ind_dir and Path(cfg.data.val_ind_dir).exists():
211	        val_ind = sorted(Path(cfg.data.val_ind_dir).glob("*.pt"))[: cfg.data.eval_max_ind]
212	    if cfg.data.val_ood_dir and Path(cfg.data.val_ood_dir).exists():
213	        val_ood = sorted(Path(cfg.data.val_ood_dir).glob("*.pt"))[: cfg.data.eval_max_ood]
214	    return train_files, val_ind, val_ood
215	
216	
217	def build_runtime(cfg: Config, run_dir: Path, fixed_support: bool = False) -> Runtime:
218	    """Wire everything together. Must be called after dist_mod.init()."""
219	    # Inject eagle env vars BEFORE any eagle.* import in dataset/trainer.
220	    inject_model_env(cfg)
221	
222	    state_info = dist_mod.state()
223	    device = torch.device(f"cuda:{state_info['local_rank']}")
224	    torch.manual_seed(cfg.train.seed + state_info["rank"])
225	
226	    train_files, val_ind, val_ood = _resolve_files(cfg)
227	    if dist_mod.is_rank0():
228	        banner(
229	            f"scale {cfg.runtime.run_name or 'run'} "
230	            f"world={state_info['world_size']} rank={state_info['rank']} "
231	            f"train={len(train_files)} val_ind={len(val_ind)} val_ood={len(val_ood)}"
232	        )
233	
234	    model, raw_model = _build_model(cfg, device)
235	    optimizer = _build_optimizer(model, cfg)
236	
237	    train_loader, train_sampler = build_train_loader(
238	        NVFP4Dataset(train_files),
239	        micro_batch=cfg.optim.micro_batch,
240	        seq_len_max=cfg.model.seq_len_max,
241	        world_size=state_info["world_size"],
242	        rank=state_info["rank"],
243	        num_workers=cfg.runtime.dataloader_workers,
244	        seed=cfg.train.seed,
245	        fixed_support=fixed_support,
246	        pin_memory=True,
247	    )
248	
249	    logger = JsonlLogger(run_dir / "train.jsonl", also_stdout=True)
250	
251	    state = ckpt_mod.ResumeState()
252	    if cfg.train.resume_from:
253	        resume_path = Path(cfg.train.resume_from)
254	        if resume_path.is_dir():
255	            resume_path = resume_path / "best.pt"
256	        state = ckpt_mod.load(resume_path, model, optimizer)
257	        if dist_mod.is_rank0():
258	            logger.log("resume", path=str(resume_path), **state.__dict__)
259	
260	    return Runtime(
261	        cfg=cfg,
262	        run_dir=run_dir,
263	        device=device,
264	        model=model,
265	        raw_model=raw_model,
266	        optimizer=optimizer,
267	        train_loader=train_loader,
268	        train_sampler=train_sampler,
269	        val_ind_files=val_ind,
270	        val_ood_files=val_ood,
271	        state=state,
272	        logger=logger,
273	    )
274	
275	
276	# ── LR schedule ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
277	
278	
279	def make_lr_fn(cfg: Config):
280	    """Linear warmup → cosine decay, matches the legacy trainer."""
281	    total = cfg.train.steps
282	    warmup = max(cfg.optim.warmup_min_steps, int(round(total * cfg.optim.warmup_ratio)))
283	    floor_factor = cfg.optim.lr_min_factor
284	    lr = cfg.optim.lr
285	
286	    def fn(step: int) -> float:
287	        if step < warmup:
288	            return lr * step / max(1, warmup)
289	        progress = (step - warmup) / max(1, total - warmup)
290	        progress = min(1.0, max(0.0, progress))
291	        cos = 0.5 * (1.0 + math.cos(math.pi * progress))
292	        return lr * (floor_factor + (1.0 - floor_factor) * cos)
293	
294	    return fn
295	
296	
297	def _set_lr(opt: torch.optim.Optimizer, lr: float) -> None:
298	    for g in opt.param_groups:
299	        g["lr"] = lr
300	
301	
302	# ── Evaluation ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
303	
304	
305	def evaluate(
306	    runtime: Runtime,
307	    files: list[Path],
308	    label: str,
309	    tail: bool,
310	) -> list[float] | None:
311	    """Rank0-only forward over the eval files. Returns per-TTT-step acc."""
312	    if not files:
313	        return None
314	    if not dist_mod.is_rank0():
315	        return None
316	
317	    cfg = runtime.cfg
318	    raw = runtime.raw_model
319	    raw.eval()
320	    ttt = cfg.model.ttt_steps
321	
322	    loader = build_eval_loader(
323	        files,
324	        seq_len_max=cfg.model.seq_len_max,
325	        num_workers=cfg.runtime.dataloader_workers,
326	        tail=tail,
327	    )
328	
329	    correct = [0.0] * ttt
330	    valid = [0.0] * ttt
331	    n_batches = 0
332	    with torch.no_grad():
333	        for batch in loader:
334	            if batch is None:
335	                continue
336	            batch = batch_to_cuda(batch, runtime.device)
337	            _, _, _, stats = raw(**batch, return_stats=True)
338	            for i in range(ttt):
339	                correct[i] += stats["correct"][i]
340	                valid[i] += stats["valid"][i]
341	            n_batches += 1
342	    raw.train()
343	    if n_batches == 0:
344	        return None
345	    return [correct[i] / max(valid[i], 1.0) for i in range(ttt)]
346	
347	
348	# ── Main loop ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
349	
350	
351	def train_loop(
352	    runtime: Runtime,
353	    *,
354	    max_steps: int | None = None,
355	    save_best: bool = True,
356	    profile_hook=None,
357	) -> dict[str, Any]:
358	    """Run the optimizer loop and return a summary dict.
359	
360	    ``max_steps`` overrides cfg.train.steps when set (used by smoke / probe).
361	    ``profile_hook(step)`` runs after each optimizer step and can call out to
362	    a torch.profiler.profile() context the caller manages.
363	    """
364	    cfg = runtime.cfg
365	    model = runtime.model
366	    opt = runtime.optimizer
367	    device = runtime.device
368	    logger = runtime.logger
369	    state = runtime.state
370	
371	    target_steps = max_steps if max_steps is not None else cfg.train.steps
372	    lr_fn = make_lr_fn(cfg)
373	
374	    # Step-0 eval (skip on resume — already evaluated before the prior crash).
375	    if state.global_step == 0 and save_best:
376	        best = _eval_and_maybe_save(runtime, state, save_best=save_best)
377	        state = best
378	
379	    model.train()
380	    data_iter = _epoch_iterator(runtime)
381	    step_t0 = time.monotonic()
382	    tokens_seen = 0
383	
384	    while state.global_step < target_steps:
385	        state.global_step += 1
386	        opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
387	        lr_now = lr_fn(state.global_step)
388	        _set_lr(opt, lr_now)
389	
390	        accum_loss = 0.0
391	        accum_acc = [0.0] * cfg.model.ttt_steps
392	        accum_n = 0
393	
394	        for micro_step in range(cfg.optim.grad_accum):
395	            batch = next(data_iter, None)
396	            if batch is None:
397	                # Epoch boundary; re-seed sampler and continue.
398	                state.epoch += 1
399	                state.sample_index = 0
400	                data_iter = _epoch_iterator(runtime)
401	                batch = next(data_iter)
402	            if batch is None:
403	                raise RuntimeError("data loader produced no usable batches")
404	            tokens_seen += int(batch["token_ids"].numel())
405	            batch = batch_to_cuda(batch, device)
406	
407	            is_last_ga = micro_step == cfg.optim.grad_accum - 1
408	            sync_ctx = nullcontext() if is_last_ga else dist_mod.no_sync(model)
409	            with sync_ctx:
410	                total_loss, _step_losses, step_accs = model(**batch)
411	                (total_loss / cfg.optim.grad_accum).backward()
412	
413	            accum_loss += float(total_loss.detach().item())
414	            for i, a in enumerate(step_accs):
415	                accum_acc[i] += float(a)
416	            accum_n += 1
417	            state.sample_index += cfg.optim.micro_batch
418	
419	        grad_norm = torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), cfg.optim.max_grad_norm)
420	        opt.step()
421	
422	        avg_loss = accum_loss / max(accum_n, 1)
423	        avg_acc = [a / max(accum_n, 1) for a in accum_acc]
424	        # Reduce across ranks for honest aggregated logging.
425	        avg_loss = dist_mod.reduce_mean(avg_loss, device)
426	        avg_acc = [dist_mod.reduce_mean(a, device) for a in avg_acc]
427	
428	        if math.isnan(avg_loss) or math.isinf(avg_loss):
429	            raise RuntimeError(f"loss NaN/Inf at step {state.global_step}")
430	
431	        if state.global_step % cfg.train.log_every == 0:
432	            elapsed = time.monotonic() - step_t0
433	            step_t0 = time.monotonic()
434	            logger.log(
435	                "train",
436	                step=state.global_step,
437	                epoch=state.epoch,
438	                loss=avg_loss,
439	                acc=avg_acc,
440	                grad_norm=float(grad_norm.detach().item()),
441	                lr=lr_now,
442	                step_s=elapsed,
443	                tok_per_s=tokens_seen / max(elapsed, 1e-6),
444	            )
445	            tokens_seen = 0
446	
447	        if profile_hook is not None:
448	            profile_hook(state.global_step)
449	
450	        if cfg.train.eval_every > 0 and state.global_step % cfg.train.eval_every == 0:
451	            state = _eval_and_maybe_save(runtime, state, save_best=save_best)
452	            model.train()
453	
454	        if cfg.train.ckpt_every > 0 and state.global_step % cfg.train.ckpt_every == 0:
455	            ckpt_mod.save(runtime.run_dir, "latest.pt", model, opt, state)
456	
457	        if cfg.train.empty_cache_every > 0 and state.global_step % cfg.train.empty_cache_every == 0:
458	            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
459	
460	    # Final eval + save
461	    if save_best:
462	        state = _eval_and_maybe_save(runtime, state, save_best=save_best, final=True)
463	    ckpt_mod.save(runtime.run_dir, "final.pt", model, opt, state)
464	    runtime.state = state
465	    return {"state": state, "run_dir": str(runtime.run_dir)}
466	
467	
468	def _epoch_iterator(runtime: Runtime):
469	    """Wrap the dataloader so the sampler's set_state is honored on resume."""
470	    sampler = runtime.train_sampler
471	    state = runtime.state
472	    if hasattr(sampler, "set_state"):
473	        sampler.set_state(state.epoch, state.sample_index)
474	    elif hasattr(sampler, "set_epoch"):
475	        sampler.set_epoch(state.epoch)
476	    for batch in runtime.train_loader:
477	        if batch is None:
478	            continue
479	        yield batch
480	
481	
482	def _eval_and_maybe_save(
483	    runtime: Runtime,
484	    state: ckpt_mod.ResumeState,
485	    save_best: bool,
486	    final: bool = False,
487	) -> ckpt_mod.ResumeState:
488	    cfg = runtime.cfg
489	    ind = evaluate(runtime, runtime.val_ind_files, "ind", tail=False)
490	    ood = evaluate(runtime, runtime.val_ood_files, "ood", tail=True)
491	    select = (ood[0] if ood else (ind[0] if ind else -1.0))
492	    improved = select > state.best_metric
493	    if dist_mod.is_rank0():
494	        runtime.logger.log(
495	            "eval",
496	            step=state.global_step,
497	            ind=ind,
498	            ood=ood,
499	            best_metric=state.best_metric,
500	            select_metric=select,
501	            improved=improved,
502	            final=final,
503	        )
504	    if save_best and improved:
505	        state.best_metric = select
506	        state.best_step = state.global_step
507	        ckpt_mod.save(
508	            runtime.run_dir,
509	            "best.pt",
510	            runtime.model,
511	            runtime.optimizer,
512	            state,
513	            extra={"ind": ind, "ood": ood},
514	        )
515	    dist_mod.barrier()
516	    return state
517
```

1	"""Smoke entry: short e2e run that exercises every path.
2	
3	Verifies in ~50 step that dist init / dataloader / forward / backward /
4	allreduce / eval / ckpt save+load / jsonl logging all work end to end. Loss
5	is not expected to converge — the only fail mode is a crash or NaN.
6	
7	Run:
8	    torchrun --nproc_per_node=1 -m scale.cli.smoke --config configs/local/smoke.yaml
9	    torchrun --nproc_per_node=8 -m scale.cli.smoke --config configs/h100/smoke.yaml
10	"""
11	from __future__ import annotations
12	
13	from scale.cli._common import lifecycle
14	from scale.core.trainer import build_runtime, train_loop
15	
16	
17	def main() -> None:
18	    with lifecycle("scale.cli.smoke") as (cfg, run_dir):
19	        runtime = build_runtime(cfg, run_dir, fixed_support=False)
20	        # smoke always honors cfg.train.steps (yaml sets it small, e.g. 50).
21	        train_loop(runtime, save_best=True)
22	
23	
24	if __name__ == "__main__":
25	    main()
26
```

1	"""Shared cli plumbing — argparse, dist init, run dir, teardown."""
2	from __future__ import annotations
3	
4	import argparse
5	from contextlib import contextmanager
6	from pathlib import Path
7	
8	from scale.core import dist
9	from scale.core.config import Config, dump_effective, load_config, resolve_run_dir
10	from scale.core.logging import banner
11	
12	
13	def parse_args(prog: str) -> argparse.Namespace:
14	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=prog)
15	    ap.add_argument("--config", required=True, type=Path)
16	    return ap.parse_args()
17	
18	
19	def setup(prog: str) -> tuple[Config, Path]:
20	    args = parse_args(prog)
21	    cfg = load_config(args.config)
22	    dist.init()
23	    run_dir = resolve_run_dir(cfg, args.config)
24	    run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
25	    if dist.is_rank0():
26	        dump_effective(cfg, run_dir)
27	        banner(f"{prog} → {run_dir}")
28	    dist.barrier()
29	    return cfg, run_dir
30	
31	
32	@contextmanager
33	def lifecycle(prog: str):
34	    """Setup, yield (cfg, run_dir), and tear down."""
35	    cfg, run_dir = setup(prog)
36	    try:
37	        yield cfg, run_dir
38	    finally:
39	        dist.shutdown()
40

> TOOL

tool_result Read
```
1	"""Checkpoint save (rank0) + load with broadcast to all ranks.
2	
3	The on-disk format mirrors the legacy ``best.pt`` so the converter in
4	``eagle/training/sala_draft/convert_to_sglang.py`` keeps working.
5	
6	Resume contract:
7	    * model weights (excluding the frozen embed_tokens — saves ~600 MB)
8	    * optimizer state (m/v for true AdamW resume)
9	    * sampler state (epoch + sample index, so we don't re-train recent data)
10	    * RNG state (python + torch + cuda)
11	    * global_step + best_metric
12	"""
13	from __future__ import annotations
14	
15	import dataclasses
16	import random
17	import time
18	from dataclasses import dataclass
19	from pathlib import Path
20	from typing import Any
21	
22	import numpy as np
23	import torch
24	import torch.nn as nn
25	
26	from scale.core import dist
27	
28	
29	@dataclass
30	class ResumeState:
31	    """Everything needed to pick up training mid-stream."""
32	    global_step: int = 0
33	    epoch: int = 0
34	    sample_index: int = 0  # next sample index within current epoch (per-rank)
35	    best_metric: float = -1.0
36	    best_step: int = -1
37	
38	
39	def _unwrap(model: nn.Module) -> nn.Module:
40	    return model.module if hasattr(model, "module") else model
41	
42	
43	def save(
44	    dest_dir: Path,
45	    name: str,
46	    model: nn.Module,
47	    optimizer: torch.optim.Optimizer,
48	    state: ResumeState,
49	    extra: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
50	) -> Path | None:
51	    """Atomic rank0 save. Returns the written path (None on non-rank0)."""
52	    if not dist.is_rank0():
53	        return None
54	
55	    dest_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
56	    out_path = dest_dir / name
57	    tmp_path = dest_dir / (name + ".tmp")
58	
59	    raw = _unwrap(model)
60	    # Drop frozen embed_tokens like the legacy trainer.
61	    model_state = {
62	        k: v.detach().cpu()
63	        for k, v in raw.named_parameters()
64	        if not k.startswith("embed_tokens")
65	    }
66	    # Buffers (t2d / d2t / draft_idx_map / rope_cos / rope_sin) need to be
67	    # saved too so reload picks up the vocab cache without re-running it.
68	    for k, v in raw.named_buffers():
69	        if k.startswith("embed_tokens"):
70	            continue
71	        model_state[k] = v.detach().cpu()
72	
73	    ckpt = {
74	        "model_state_dict": model_state,
75	        "optimizer_state_dict": optimizer.state_dict(),
76	        "resume_state": dataclasses.asdict(state),
77	        "rng": _capture_rng(),
78	        "timestamp": time.time(),
79	    }
80	    if extra:
81	        ckpt["extra"] = extra
82	
83	    torch.save(ckpt, tmp_path)
84	    tmp_path.replace(out_path)
85	    return out_path
86	
87	
88	def load(
89	    src_path: Path,
90	    model: nn.Module,
91	    optimizer: torch.optim.Optimizer | None,
92	) -> ResumeState:
93	    """Load on rank0, broadcast model/optim/state to other ranks.
94	
95	    Strategy: rank0 reads from disk, then DDP's standard parameter broadcast
96	    (triggered by the next forward) handles weight sync. Optimizer state and
97	    resume_state are broadcast explicitly via broadcast_object_list because
98	    they are not DDP-managed.
99	    """
100	    src_path = Path(src_path)
101	    if dist.is_rank0():
102	        if not src_path.exists():
103	            raise FileNotFoundError(f"resume ckpt not found: {src_path}")
104	        ckpt = torch.load(src_path, weights_only=False, map_location="cpu")
105	    else:
106	        ckpt = None
107	
108	    raw = _unwrap(model)
109	    # Model weights: rank0 loads, others zero-init then re-broadcast on first
110	    # forward (DDP does this automatically on construct). We still ensure
111	    # rank0 loaded successfully before all ranks proceed.
112	    if dist.is_rank0():
113	        missing, unexpected = raw.load_state_dict(ckpt["model_state_dict"], strict=False)
114	        if missing:
115	            print(f"[ckpt] missing keys (will use init): {missing[:3]}{'...' if len(missing) > 3 else ''}")
116	        if unexpected:
117	            print(f"[ckpt] unexpected keys: {unexpected[:3]}{'...' if len(unexpected) > 3 else ''}")
118	
119	    # Broadcast model parameters from rank0 to all ranks (avoids needing
120	    # every rank to read the same file).
121	    state_info = dist.state()
122	    if state_info["world_size"] > 1:
123	        import torch.distributed as tdist
124	        for p in raw.parameters():
125	            tdist.broadcast(p.data, src=0)
126	        for b in raw.buffers():
127	            if b.dtype.is_floating_point or b.dtype == torch.bool or b.dtype in (
128	                torch.int8, torch.int16, torch.int32, torch.int64,
129	            ):
130	                tdist.broadcast(b.data, src=0)
131	
132	    # Optimizer state: broadcast as a python object.
133	    if optimizer is not None:
134	        opt_state = ckpt["optimizer_state_dict"] if dist.is_rank0() else None
135	        opt_state = dist.broadcast_object(opt_state, src=0)
136	        if opt_state is not None:
137	            try:
138	                optimizer.load_state_dict(opt_state)
139	            except Exception as e:
140	                print(f"[ckpt] optimizer state incompatible, fresh: {e}")
141	
142	    resume_dict = ckpt["resume_state"] if dist.is_rank0() else None
143	    resume_dict = dist.broadcast_object(resume_dict, src=0)
144	    state = ResumeState(**resume_dict) if resume_dict else ResumeState()
145	
146	    rng_state = ckpt["rng"] if dist.is_rank0() else None
147	    rng_state = dist.broadcast_object(rng_state, src=0)
148	    if rng_state:
149	        _restore_rng(rng_state)
150	
151	    dist.barrier()
152	    return state
153	
154	
155	def _capture_rng() -> dict[str, Any]:
156	    """Snapshot python/numpy/torch/cuda RNG state for exact resume."""
157	    state = {
158	        "python": random.getstate(),
159	        "numpy": np.random.get_state(),
160	        "torch": torch.get_rng_state().tolist(),
161	    }
162	    if torch.cuda.is_available():
163	        state["cuda"] = [t.tolist() for t in torch.cuda.get_rng_state_all()]
164	    return state
165	
166	
167	def _restore_rng(state: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
168	    random.setstate(state["python"])
169	    np.random.set_state(state["numpy"])
170	    torch.set_rng_state(torch.tensor(state["torch"], dtype=torch.uint8))
171	    if torch.cuda.is_available() and "cuda" in state:
172	        cuda_states = [torch.tensor(t, dtype=torch.uint8) for t in state["cuda"]]
173	        torch.cuda.set_rng_state_all(cuda_states)
174
```

```
1	"""Batch-size probe: find per-rank micro_batch ceiling for the configured shape.
2	
3	Two modes:
4	  * explicit sweep — cfg.probe.sweep_micro_batches is non-empty; try each in
5	    order, record (micro_batch, peak_mem_gb, step_s, ok) to probe.jsonl
6	  * auto binary search — sweep_micro_batches=[]; binary-search [1, max_try]
7	    for the largest micro_batch that survives 10 optimizer steps without OOM
8	
9	Always logs to ``<run_dir>/probe.jsonl``. Never writes a best.pt or final.pt.
10	
11	Run:
12	    torchrun --nproc_per_node=8 -m scale.cli.batch_probe --config configs/h100/batch_probe.yaml
13	"""
14	from __future__ import annotations
15	
16	import dataclasses
17	import gc
18	import json
19	import time
20	from pathlib import Path
21	
22	import torch
23	
24	from scale.cli._common import lifecycle
25	from scale.core import dist
26	from scale.core.config import Config
27	from scale.core.trainer import build_runtime, train_loop
28	
29	
30	_MAX_AUTO = 32  # auto search upper bound
31	
32	
33	def _try_one(cfg: Config, run_dir: Path, micro_batch: int) -> dict:
34	    """Attempt 10 steps at the given micro_batch. Return result dict."""
35	    # Patch the dataclass field for this attempt.
36	    patched = dataclasses.replace(
37	        cfg,
38	        optim=dataclasses.replace(cfg.optim, micro_batch=micro_batch),
39	        train=dataclasses.replace(cfg.train, eval_every=0, ckpt_every=0, log_every=1),
40	    )
41	    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
42	    torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats()
43	    t0 = time.monotonic()
44	    try:
45	        runtime = build_runtime(patched, run_dir, fixed_support=False)
46	        train_loop(runtime, max_steps=10, save_best=False)
47	        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
48	        peak = torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() / 1024**3
49	        return {
50	            "micro_batch": micro_batch,
51	            "ok": True,
52	            "peak_mem_gb": peak,
53	            "wall_s": elapsed,
54	            "step_s_est": elapsed / 10,
55	        }
56	    except torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError as e:
57	        return {"micro_batch": micro_batch, "ok": False, "reason": "oom", "error": str(e)[:200]}
58	    except Exception as e:
59	        return {"micro_batch": micro_batch, "ok": False, "reason": "error", "error": str(e)[:200]}
60	    finally:
61	        gc.collect()
62	        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
63	
64	
65	def _save(run_dir: Path, results: list[dict]) -> None:
66	    if not dist.is_rank0():
67	        return
68	    out = run_dir / "probe.jsonl"
69	    with out.open("w") as f:
70	        for r in results:
71	            f.write(json.dumps(r) + "\n")
72	
73	
74	def main() -> None:
75	    with lifecycle("scale.cli.batch_probe") as (cfg, run_dir):
76	        sweep = list(cfg.probe.sweep_micro_batches)
77	        results: list[dict] = []
78	        if sweep:
79	            for mb in sweep:
80	                r = _try_one(cfg, run_dir, mb)
81	                if dist.is_rank0():
82	                    print(f"[probe] mb={mb}: {r}")
83	                results.append(r)
84	        else:
85	            # Binary search: keep doubling until OOM, then binary refine.
86	            lo, hi = 1, 1
87	            while hi <= _MAX_AUTO:
88	                r = _try_one(cfg, run_dir, hi)
89	                results.append(r)
90	                if dist.is_rank0():
91	                    print(f"[probe] auto mb={hi}: {r}")
92	                if not r["ok"]:
93	                    break
94	                lo = hi
95	                hi *= 2
96	            # Binary search between last OK (lo) and first OOM (hi).
97	            while hi - lo > 1:
98	                mid = (lo + hi) // 2
99	                r = _try_one(cfg, run_dir, mid)
100	                results.append(r)
101	                if dist.is_rank0():
102	                    print(f"[probe] auto mb={mid}: {r}")
103	                if r["ok"]:
104	                    lo = mid
105	                else:
106	                    hi = mid
107	            if dist.is_rank0():
108	                print(f"[probe] auto search done; max safe micro_batch = {lo}")
109	
110	        _save(run_dir, results)
111	
112	
113	if __name__ == "__main__":
114	    main()
115
```

1	"""Profile entry: torch.profiler over N warm + M active steps, dump chrome trace.
2	
3	Output:
4	  * ``<run_dir>/profile/trace.json`` — Chrome trace, open in chrome://tracing
5	    or perfetto.dev for kernel-level analysis
6	  * ``<run_dir>/profile/summary.txt`` — top-20 ops by self CUDA time
7	
8	Both small enough to download from a submission environment by hand.
9	
10	Run:
11	    torchrun --nproc_per_node=8 -m scale.cli.profile --config configs/h100/profile.yaml
12	"""
13	from __future__ import annotations
14	
15	from pathlib import Path
16	
17	import torch
18	
19	from scale.cli._common import lifecycle
20	from scale.core import dist
21	from scale.core.trainer import build_runtime, train_loop
22	
23	
24	def main() -> None:
25	    with lifecycle("scale.cli.profile") as (cfg, run_dir):
26	        runtime = build_runtime(cfg, run_dir, fixed_support=False)
27	
28	        warmup = cfg.probe.profile_warmup
29	        active = cfg.probe.profile_active
30	        total_steps = warmup + active
31	
32	        prof_dir = run_dir / "profile"
33	        prof_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
34	
35	        activities = [torch.profiler.ProfilerActivity.CPU, torch.profiler.ProfilerActivity.CUDA]
36	        # Only rank0 writes the chrome trace; other ranks profile but discard.
37	        on_trace_ready = (
38	            torch.profiler.tensorboard_trace_handler(str(prof_dir))
39	            if dist.is_rank0()
40	            else (lambda p: None)
41	        )
42	
43	        with torch.profiler.profile(
44	            activities=activities,
45	            schedule=torch.profiler.schedule(
46	                wait=0, warmup=warmup, active=active, repeat=1,
47	            ),
48	            on_trace_ready=on_trace_ready,
49	            record_shapes=False,
50	            profile_memory=False,
51	            with_stack=False,
52	        ) as prof:
53	            def hook(step: int) -> None:
54	                prof.step()
55	
56	            train_loop(runtime, max_steps=total_steps, save_best=False, profile_hook=hook)
57	
58	        if dist.is_rank0():
59	            summary = prof.key_averages().table(
60	                sort_by="self_cuda_time_total", row_limit=20,
61	            )
62	            (prof_dir / "summary.txt").write_text(summary)
63	            print(summary)
64	            print(f"[profile] chrome trace written under {prof_dir}/")
65	
66	
67	if __name__ == "__main__":
68	    main()
69
```

1	# Multi-stage build for the scale training image.
2	#
3	# Build context MUST be the repo user_4813494d (/user_4813494d/openbmb), not scale/, so we can
4	# COPY eagle/ alongside scale/. See docker/build_and_push.sh.
5	#
6	# Base image expectations:
7	#   - PyTorch >= 2.3 with CUDA 12 (H100 sm_90 friendly)
8	#   - safetensors, numpy, pyyaml, tqdm CAN be missing — we install them below
9	#
10	# Override BASE_IMAGE at build time if your platform mandates a different one,
11	# e.g. --build-arg BASE_IMAGE=nvcr.io/nvidia/pytorch:24.10-py3
12	
13	ARG BASE_IMAGE=pytorch/pytorch:2.4.0-cuda12.4-cudnn9-runtime
14	
15	# ── Stage 1: install Python deps from offline wheels ─────────────────────
16	FROM ${BASE_IMAGE} AS deps
17	
18	WORKDIR /tmp/install
19	
20	# Copy wheels first so this layer cache survives code edits.
21	COPY scale/docker/wheels/ /tmp/install/wheels/
22	COPY scale/requirements.txt /tmp/install/requirements.txt
23	
24	# Offline install: --no-index + --find-links guarantees zero external network.
25	# If wheels/ is empty (dev convenience) fall through to a normal install so
26	# the build still works on the dev box; production builds populate wheels/ via
27	# build_and_push.sh.
28	RUN if [ "$(ls -A /tmp/install/wheels 2>/dev/null)" ]; then \
29	        pip install --no-cache-dir --no-index --find-links /tmp/install/wheels \
30	            -r /tmp/install/requirements.txt ; \
31	    else \
32	        echo "WARN: wheels/ is empty, falling back to online install" >&2 && \
33	        pip install --no-cache-dir -r /tmp/install/requirements.txt ; \
34	    fi
35	
36	
37	# ── Stage 2: runtime image with code ─────────────────────────────────────
38	FROM deps AS runtime
39	
40	WORKDIR /workspace
41	
42	# eagle/ provides Eagle3ModelV4 + nvfp4_codec + arch + fp4_quant — required at
43	# import time by scale.core.trainer. Treated as read-only inside the container.
44	COPY eagle/ /workspace/eagle/
45	
46	# scale package + configs.
47	COPY scale/scale/ /workspace/scale/
48	COPY scale/configs/ /workspace/configs/
49	COPY scale/docker/entrypoint.sh /workspace/entrypoint.sh
50	RUN chmod +x /workspace/entrypoint.sh
51	
52	# Both packages on path; importable as `import eagle` and `import scale`.
53	ENV PYTHONPATH=/workspace
54	ENV PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1
55	# Use expandable segments to avoid fragmentation on long training runs.
56	ENV PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True
57	
58	# Honor SCALE_ENTRY / SCALE_CONFIG / SCALE_NPROC at start time; see entrypoint.sh.
59	ENTRYPOINT ["/workspace/entrypoint.sh"]
60
```

1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	# Single image, many entries. Platform sets env vars; we dispatch torchrun.
3	#
4	# Required env:
5	#   SCALE_ENTRY    one of: train | smoke | overfit | batch_probe | profile
6	#   SCALE_CONFIG   path under /workspace/configs/ (e.g. h100/train.yaml) OR
7	#                  an absolute path inside the container
8	#   SCALE_NPROC    GPUs per node, typically 8 on H100
9	#
10	# Optional env (defaults shown):
11	#   SCALE_OUTPUT_user_4813494d=/output   (overrides cfg.runtime.output_user_4813494d only if a
12	#                                separate launch wraps SCALE_CFG_OVERRIDES, which
13	#                                this entrypoint does not — set output_user_4813494d in
14	#                                the yaml instead)
15	#   SCALE_EXTRA_ARGS=""         extra torchrun flags
16	#
17	# torchrun init is rendezvous-less (--standalone) since we run single-node 8-GPU.
18	# Multi-node would need RDZV_BACKEND / RDZV_ENDPOINT — out of scope here.
19	
20	set -euo pipefail
21	
22	ENTRY="${SCALE_ENTRY:-train}"
23	CONFIG="${SCALE_CONFIG:-h100/train.yaml}"
24	NPROC="${SCALE_NPROC:-8}"
25	EXTRA="${SCALE_EXTRA_ARGS:-}"
26	
27	# Resolve CONFIG: if it isn't absolute, look under /workspace/configs/.
28	case "${CONFIG}" in
29	    /*) CFG_PATH="${CONFIG}" ;;
30	    *)  CFG_PATH="/workspace/configs/${CONFIG}" ;;
31	esac
32	
33	if [[ ! -f "${CFG_PATH}" ]]; then
34	    echo "entrypoint: config not found: ${CFG_PATH}" >&2
35	    exit 2
36	fi
37	
38	# Validate ENTRY is a real module before launching (cheap sanity vs torchrun's
39	# late failure).
40	case "${ENTRY}" in
41	    train|smoke|overfit|batch_probe|profile) ;;
42	    *) echo "entrypoint: unknown SCALE_ENTRY=${ENTRY}" >&2; exit 2 ;;
43	esac
44	
45	cd /workspace
46	
47	echo "============================================================"
48	echo "  scale entrypoint"
49	echo "  entry  : ${ENTRY}"
50	echo "  config : ${CFG_PATH}"
51	echo "  nproc  : ${NPROC}"
52	echo "  extra  : ${EXTRA}"
53	echo "============================================================"
54	
55	# shellcheck disable=SC2086
56	exec torchrun \
57	    --nproc_per_node="${NPROC}" \
58	    --standalone \
59	    -m "scale.cli.${ENTRY}" \
60	    --config "${CFG_PATH}" \
61	    ${EXTRA}
62
```
