1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	# One-button build + tag (git sha) + push.
3	#
4	# Usage:
5	#   bash scale/docker/build_and_push.sh [registry/image] [base_image_override]
6	#
7	# Examples:
8	#   bash scale/docker/build_and_push.sh registry.internal/team/scale
9	#   bash scale/docker/build_and_push.sh registry.internal/team/scale nvcr.io/nvidia/pytorch:24.10-py3
10	#
11	# Run from repo user_4813494d. The build context is the repo user_4813494d because Dockerfile
12	# COPYs both eagle/ and scale/.
13	
14	set -euo pipefail
15	
16	REPO_user_4813494d="$(git -C "$(dirname "$0")" rev-parse --show-toplevel)"
17	cd "${REPO_user_4813494d}"
18	
19	IMAGE="${1:-scale}"
20	BASE_IMAGE="${2:-pytorch/pytorch:2.4.0-cuda12.4-cudnn9-runtime}"
21	
22	SHA="$(git rev-parse --short=12 HEAD)"
23	DIRTY=""
24	if [[ -n "$(git status --porcelain)" ]]; then
25	    DIRTY="-dirty"
26	fi
27	TAG="${IMAGE}:${SHA}${DIRTY}"
28	
29	WHEELS_DIR="scale/docker/wheels"
30	mkdir -p "${WHEELS_DIR}"
31	
32	# Refresh offline wheels if we have external network. This is a one-shot
33	# operation on the dev box; image builds inside the submission environment
34	# read whatever wheels/ contains.
35	if [[ "${SCALE_SKIP_WHEEL_DOWNLOAD:-0}" != "1" ]]; then
36	    echo "[build_and_push] downloading wheels to ${WHEELS_DIR}/ (set SCALE_SKIP_WHEEL_DOWNLOAD=1 to skip)..."
37	    pip download -r scale/requirements.txt -d "${WHEELS_DIR}" \
38	        --only-binary=:all: \
39	        --platform manylinux2014_x86_64 \
40	        --python-version 3.10 \
41	        --implementation cp \
42	        --abi cp310 \
43	        || echo "[build_and_push] warn: wheel download failed; image build will fall back to online install"
44	fi
45	
46	echo "============================================================"
47	echo "  build:  ${TAG}"
48	echo "  base :  ${BASE_IMAGE}"
49	echo "============================================================"
50	
51	docker build \
52	    --file scale/docker/Dockerfile \
53	    --build-arg BASE_IMAGE="${BASE_IMAGE}" \
54	    --tag "${TAG}" \
55	    .
56	
57	if [[ "${SCALE_NO_PUSH:-0}" == "1" ]]; then
58	    echo "[build_and_push] SCALE_NO_PUSH=1 set; skipping push"
59	    echo "[build_and_push] image ready: ${TAG}"
60	    exit 0
61	fi
62	
63	if [[ "${IMAGE}" == "scale" ]]; then
64	    echo "[build_and_push] image is local (no registry prefix); skipping push"
65	    echo "[build_and_push] to push, pass registry/image as first arg"
66	    exit 0
67	fi
68	
69	echo "[build_and_push] pushing ${TAG}..."
70	docker push "${TAG}"
71	echo "[build_and_push] done: ${TAG}"
72
```

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Sync the v3mix 300K segments from BOS to a node-local SSD.
3	
4	The H100 platform syncs data once before training starts. This tool runs on
5	the H100 host (or by an init container) and pulls the BOS prefix down so the
6	training image just sees local files.
7	
8	Usage on H100 host:
9	    python3 tools/pre_sync_bos.py \\
10	        --bos bos://anp3-common-model/vista/eagle3_data/v3mix_300k_det_len2048_topk256_aux1-10-22 \\
11	        --dst /data/v3mix_300k \\
12	        --bcecmd /usr/local/bin/bcecmd --bce-conf /etc/bce_conf \\
13	        --workers 8
14	
15	The sync is incremental: existing seg_NNNNNN directories with a valid
16	_SUCCESS.json file are skipped. A missing seg_NNNNNN/_SUCCESS.json is
17	re-downloaded.
18	
19	This tool intentionally has zero Python deps beyond the standard library so
20	it can run inside a minimum-base init container.
21	"""
22	from __future__ import annotations
23	
24	import argparse
25	import concurrent.futures
26	import json
27	import os
28	import subprocess
29	import sys
30	import time
31	from pathlib import Path
32	
33	
34	def run(cmd: list[str], timeout: float = 600.0) -> tuple[int, str]:
35	    proc = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout)
36	    return proc.returncode, (proc.stdout + proc.stderr)[:4000]
37	
38	
39	def list_remote_segments(bcecmd: str, bce_conf: str, prefix: str) -> list[str]:
40	    """List seg_NNNNNN/ entries under the BOS prefix."""
41	    rc, out = run([bcecmd, "--conf-path", bce_conf, "bos", "ls", prefix + "/"])
42	    if rc != 0:
43	        raise RuntimeError(f"bos ls failed: {out[:300]}")
44	    segments = []
45	    for line in out.splitlines():
46	        line = line.strip().rstrip("/")
47	        name = line.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
48	        if name.startswith("seg_") and len(name) == 10:
49	            segments.append(name)
50	    return sorted(set(segments))
51	
52	
53	def is_synced(local_seg_dir: Path) -> bool:
54	    success = local_seg_dir / "_SUCCESS.json"
55	    if not success.exists():
56	        return False
57	    try:
58	        manifest = json.loads(success.read_text())
59	        n_files = int(manifest["files"])
60	    except Exception:
61	        return False
62	    on_disk = len(list(local_seg_dir.glob("*.pt")))
63	    return on_disk == n_files
64	
65	
66	def sync_one(args: argparse.Namespace, seg: str) -> tuple[str, bool, str]:
67	    dst = Path(args.dst) / seg
68	    if is_synced(dst):
69	        return seg, True, "cached"
70	    dst.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
71	    remote = f"{args.bos.rstrip('/')}/{seg}/"
72	    cmd = [
73	        args.bcecmd, "--conf-path", args.bce_conf,
74	        "bos", "cp", "-r", remote, str(dst) + "/",
75	    ]
76	    rc, out = run(cmd, timeout=args.timeout)
77	    if rc != 0:
78	        return seg, False, out[:300]
79	    if not is_synced(dst):
80	        return seg, False, "post-sync verify failed"
81	    return seg, True, "ok"
82	
83	
84	def main() -> None:
85	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
86	    ap.add_argument("--bos", required=True, help="BOS prefix, e.g. bos://bucket/path")
87	    ap.add_argument("--dst", required=True, help="local destination user_4813494d")
88	    ap.add_argument("--bcecmd", default="/usr/local/bin/bcecmd")
89	    ap.add_argument("--bce-conf", default="/etc/bce_conf")
90	    ap.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=4)
91	    ap.add_argument("--timeout", type=float, default=3600)
92	    ap.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=0, help="cap segments synced (0 = all)")
93	    ap.add_argument("--start", type=int, default=0, help="seg index to start at")
94	    args = ap.parse_args()
95	
96	    Path(args.dst).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
97	    print(f"[pre_sync] listing remote segments under {args.bos}...")
98	    segments = list_remote_segments(args.bcecmd, args.bce_conf, args.bos)
99	    if args.start > 0:
100	        segments = [s for s in segments if int(s.split("_")[1]) >= args.start]
101	    if args.limit > 0:
102	        segments = segments[: args.limit]
103	    print(f"[pre_sync] {len(segments)} segments to consider")
104	
105	    t0 = time.time()
106	    ok = 0
107	    fail = 0
108	    cached = 0
109	    with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.workers) as pool:
110	        futures = {pool.submit(sync_one, args, s): s for s in segments}
111	        for i, fut in enumerate(concurrent.futures.as_completed(futures), 1):
112	            seg, success, msg = fut.result()
113	            if success:
114	                ok += 1
115	                if msg == "cached":
116	                    cached += 1
117	            else:
118	                fail += 1
119	                print(f"[pre_sync] FAIL {seg}: {msg}", file=sys.stderr)
120	            if i % 50 == 0 or i == len(segments):
121	                rate = i / max(time.time() - t0, 1e-6)
122	                print(f"[pre_sync] {i}/{len(segments)} ok={ok} cached={cached} fail={fail} "
123	                      f"({rate:.1f} seg/s, {(time.time()-t0)/60:.1f} min)")
124	
125	    if fail:
126	        sys.exit(1)
127	
128	
129	if __name__ == "__main__":
130	    main()
131
```

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Extract embed_tokens + lm_head weights from the target model.
3	
4	The full MiniCPM-SALA-NVFP4-det target is 8 GB+; the only tensors training
5	needs are the two embedding matrices (~1.2 GB combined). Extracting them once
6	locally and shipping a single safetensors file shaves >85% off the H100
7	sync footprint.
8	
9	Usage:
10	    python3 scale/tools/extract_target_artifacts.py \\
11	        --src /user_4813494d/models/openbmb/MiniCPM-SALA-gptq46-calib90-90k-NVFP4-det \\
12	        --dst /user_4813494d/data/eagle_stage/target_artifacts.safetensors
13	
14	The dst file is what scale config target.artifacts_file should point at.
15	"""
16	from __future__ import annotations
17	
18	import argparse
19	import json
20	from pathlib import Path
21	
22	from safetensors import safe_open
23	from safetensors.torch import save_file
24	
25	
26	def main() -> None:
27	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
28	    ap.add_argument("--src", required=True, type=Path,
29	                    help="target model directory containing safetensors")
30	    ap.add_argument("--dst", required=True, type=Path,
31	                    help="output safetensors path")
32	    args = ap.parse_args()
33	
34	    if not args.src.is_dir():
35	        raise FileNotFoundError(f"src not a directory: {args.src}")
36	
37	    index_path = args.src / "model.safetensors.index.json"
38	    if index_path.exists():
39	        index = json.loads(index_path.read_text())
40	        wm = index["weight_map"]
41	        emb_file = args.src / wm["model.embed_tokens.weight"]
42	        lm_file = args.src / wm.get("lm_head.weight", wm["model.embed_tokens.weight"])
43	    else:
44	        emb_file = args.src / "model.safetensors"
45	        lm_file = emb_file
46	
47	    out: dict = {}
48	    with safe_open(emb_file, framework="pt", device="cpu") as f:
49	        out["model.embed_tokens.weight"] = f.get_tensor("model.embed_tokens.weight").contiguous()
50	    with safe_open(lm_file, framework="pt", device="cpu") as f:
51	        keys = list(f.keys())
52	        if "lm_head.weight" in keys:
53	            out["lm_head.weight"] = f.get_tensor("lm_head.weight").contiguous()
54	        else:
55	            print("[extract] lm_head.weight not found, tying to embed_tokens")
56	            out["lm_head.weight"] = out["model.embed_tokens.weight"].clone()
57	
58	    args.dst.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
59	    save_file(out, str(args.dst))
60	
61	    size_gb = args.dst.stat().st_size / 1024**3
62	    print(f"[extract] wrote {args.dst} ({size_gb:.2f} GiB)")
63	    for k, v in out.items():
64	        print(f"  {k}: {tuple(v.shape)} {v.dtype}")
65	
66	
67	if __name__ == "__main__":
68	    main()
69
```

1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	# Local docker dry-run: build the scale image, then run overfit + smoke
3	# entries inside it against locally-mounted data.
4	#
5	# Mirrors the H100 submission flow so a bug in Dockerfile / entrypoint / env
6	# protocol blows up here, not after a 10-minute platform launch.
7	#
8	# Pre-reqs:
9	#   * docker installed on this host
10	#   * /user_4813494d/data and /user_4813494d/models accessible (default mount source)
11	#   * GPU passthrough working (--gpus all)
12	#
13	# Usage:
14	#   bash scale/tools/local_docker_dryrun.sh
15	#
16	# Skip rebuild:
17	#   SCALE_DRY_SKIP_BUILD=1 bash scale/tools/local_docker_dryrun.sh
18	
19	set -euo pipefail
20	
21	REPO_user_4813494d="$(git -C "$(dirname "$0")" rev-parse --show-toplevel)"
22	cd "${REPO_user_4813494d}"
23	
24	IMAGE="${SCALE_DRY_IMAGE:-scale:dryrun}"
25	
26	if [[ "${SCALE_DRY_SKIP_BUILD:-0}" != "1" ]]; then
27	    echo "[dryrun] building ${IMAGE}..."
28	    SCALE_NO_PUSH=1 SCALE_SKIP_WHEEL_DOWNLOAD=1 \
29	        bash scale/docker/build_and_push.sh "${IMAGE}" \
30	        || { echo "[dryrun] build failed"; exit 1; }
31	fi
32	
33	mkdir -p scale/runs
34	
35	# Path map inside the container:
36	#   /data       <- /user_4813494d/data
37	#   /models     <- /user_4813494d/models  (rare; configs typically only need /data)
38	#   /workspace/runs <- /user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/runs  (run outputs)
39	# Configs already in image at /workspace/configs/.
40	
41	run_entry () {
42	    local entry="$1"
43	    local config="$2"
44	    echo "============================================================"
45	    echo "  [dryrun] entry=${entry} config=${config}"
46	    echo "============================================================"
47	    docker run --rm --gpus all \
48	        -v /user_4813494d/data:/data \
49	        -v /user_4813494d/models:/models \
50	        -v "${REPO_user_4813494d}/scale/runs:/workspace/runs" \
51	        -e SCALE_ENTRY="${entry}" \
52	        -e SCALE_CONFIG="${config}" \
53	        -e SCALE_NPROC=1 \
54	        "${IMAGE}"
55	}
56	
57	# Note: configs/local/*.yaml are pre-built for /user_4813494d/data paths. Inside the
58	# container we mount /user_4813494d/data at /data, so the data paths under cfg.data.*
59	# need to swap /user_4813494d/data -> /data. The easy way is to use container-friendly
60	# configs; for now we override by mounting the entire host data tree at the
61	# same path:
62	docker run --rm --gpus all \
63	    -v /user_4813494d/data:/user_4813494d/data \
64	    -v /user_4813494d/models:/user_4813494d/models \
65	    -v "${REPO_user_4813494d}/scale/runs:/workspace/runs" \
66	    -e SCALE_ENTRY=overfit \
67	    -e SCALE_CONFIG=local/overfit.yaml \
68	    -e SCALE_NPROC=1 \
69	    "${IMAGE}"
70	
71	docker run --rm --gpus all \
72	    -v /user_4813494d/data:/user_4813494d/data \
73	    -v /user_4813494d/models:/user_4813494d/models \
74	    -v "${REPO_user_4813494d}/scale/runs:/workspace/runs" \
75	    -e SCALE_ENTRY=smoke \
76	    -e SCALE_CONFIG=local/smoke.yaml \
77	    -e SCALE_NPROC=1 \
78	    "${IMAGE}"
79	
80	echo "[dryrun] all entries passed under image ${IMAGE}"
81
```

1	# H100 overfit. Mirrors local_overfit.yaml but on 8 GPUs to verify DDP + ckpt
2	# don't break the math. Each rank sees the same 16 samples (fixed_support).
3	#
4	# Pass criterion: train acc[0] reaches >0.95 by step 300 on each rank.
5	
6	data:
7	  train_dir: /data/overfit_train16
8	  train_max_files: 16
9	  vocab_cache: /data/vocab_cache_v3mix.pt
10	  val_ind_dir: ""
11	  val_ood_dir: ""
12	
13	target:
14	  artifacts_file: /data/target_artifacts.safetensors
15	
16	model:
17	  ttt_steps: 3
18	  seq_len_max: 1024
19	  pack: false
20	  nvfp4_forward: false
21	  exclude_lm_head_from_fp4: true
22	
23	optim:
24	  micro_batch: 1
25	  grad_accum: 1
26	  lr: 5.0e-4
27	  warmup_ratio: 0.0
28	  warmup_min_steps: 0
29	  lr_min_factor: 1.0
30	  weight_decay: 0.0
31	  max_grad_norm: 1.0
32	
33	train:
34	  steps: 300
35	  eval_every: 0
36	  log_every: 10
37	  ckpt_every: 0

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/README.md",
  "content": "# scale — SALA EAGLE-3 draft trainer @ scale\n\nDDP-aware, env-driven, container-first re-packaging of `eagle/training/sala_draft/train.py`\nfor **8×H100 production runs submitted as a Docker image**.\n\nThe legacy trainer is single-GPU, interactive, and hard-codes paths in `argparse`.\nThe submission platform requires:\n* one Docker image, no external network at runtime\n* a single env-only entrypoint (no flag soup)\n* artifacts written to a known output mount\n* checkpoints resumable after a node restart\n\n`scale/` is that wrapper. The math (LK^λ loss, AOI position, FP4-QAT MLP, TTT loop)\nis reused unchanged from `eagle.training.sala_draft.train.Eagle3ModelV4`.\n\n---\n\n## Validation pyramid (run bottom-up before pushing the image)\n\n```\n                ┌──────────────────────────────┐\n                │  H100 train.yaml × 8 GPU     │   ← submit only after every\n                │  (real money, ~hours)        │     level below is green\n                └──────────────────────────────┘\n                ┌──────────────────────────────┐\n                │  H100 smoke / batch_probe /  │   ← run on H100 once, cheap\n                │  profile (50 step or less)   │\n                └──────────────────────────────┘\n                ┌──────────────────────────────┐\n                │  local docker dryrun         │   ← Dockerfile + entrypoint\n                │  (overfit + smoke in image)  │     contract on the dev box\n                └──────────────────────────────┘\n                ┌──────────────────────────────┐\n                │  local overfit / smoke /     │   ← every cli entry under\n                │  batch_probe / profile       │     bare torchrun --nproc=1\n                └──────────────────────────────┘\n                ┌──────────────────────────────┐\n                │  pytest scale/scale/tests/   │   ← config / ckpt / dataset\n                │  (27 tests, sub-second)      │     pure-Python invariants\n                └──────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\nEach level catches a different class of bug:\n* **pytest** — config schema, ckpt save/load round-trip, NVFP4 codec drift, sampler determinism. Pure CPU.\n* **local cli** — real model on 1 GPU; catches env injection ordering, MLP warm-start, DataLoader pin_memory, DDP fallback, jsonl logging.\n* **local docker dryrun** — Dockerfile/COPY layout, entrypoint env-var dispatch, mount path conventions. Same code, same image, just 1 GPU.\n* **H100 smoke/probe/profile** — NCCL allreduce, multi-rank ckpt broadcast, real seq_len_max throughput, per-rank micro_batch ceiling.\n* **H100 train** — the only level that actually consumes the training budget.\n\nSkipping a level always costs more than running it.\n\n---\n\n## Repo layout\n\n```\nscale/\n├── README.md                 # this file\n├── pyproject.toml            # package metadata; no install needed (PYTHONPATH)\n├── requirements.txt          # safetensors, numpy, tqdm, pyyaml — torch from base\n├── scale/                    # the python package\n│   ├── core/                 # config, dist, dataset, trainer, ckpt, logging\n│   ├── cli/                  # 5 thin entries: train, smoke, overfit, batch_probe, profile\n│   └── tests/                # pytest, sub-second\n├── configs/\n│   ├── local/                # dev-box paths, tiny step counts\n│   └── h100/                 # /data + /output mounts, real step counts\n├── docker/\n│   ├── Dockerfile            # multi-stage, offline wheels\n│   ├── entrypoint.sh         # SCALE_ENTRY / SCALE_CONFIG / SCALE_NPROC env protocol\n│   ├── build_and_push.sh     # git-sha tag + offline-wheel download + push\n│   └── wheels/               # populated by build_and_push.sh\n├── tools/\n│   ├── pre_sync_bos.py       # bos -> node-local SSD, resumable\n│   ├── extract_target_artifacts.py  # 8 GB target -> 1.2 GB embed+lm_head\n│   └── local_docker_dryrun.sh\n└── runs/                     # gitignored; per-run output dirs\n```\n\nThe only inter-package dependency is `from eagle.training.sala_draft.train import Eagle3ModelV4`\n(plus `eagle.nvfp4_codec` for the storage codec). Everything else is self-contained.\n\n---\n\n## CLI entries\n\nAll entries take exactly one flag: `--config PATH`. Everything else flows through yaml.\n\n| entry | purpose | runtime | output |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `scale.cli.train` | full production run | hours | best/latest/final.pt + train.jsonl + eval.jsonl |\n| `scale.cli.smoke` | 20–50 step e2e gate | minutes | train.jsonl + final.pt |\n| `scale.cli.overfit` | 4-sample sanity (acc/loss curve health) | minutes | train.jsonl + final.pt |\n| `scale.cli.batch_probe` | per-rank micro_batch ceiling | minutes | probe.jsonl |\n| `scale.cli.profile` | torch.profiler chrome trace | <1 min | profile/{trace.json, summary.txt} |\n\nLaunch idiom:\n```bash\n# local (1 GPU)\nPYTHONPATH=. torchrun --standalone --nproc_per_node=1 \\\n    -m scale.cli.smoke --config scale/configs/local/smoke.yaml\n\n# H100 (8 GPU, via the container entrypoint — env-only)\nSCALE_ENTRY=smoke SCALE_CONFIG=h100/smoke.yaml SCALE_NPROC=8 \\\n    docker run --rm --gpus all -v /data:/data -v /output:/output ${IMAGE}\n```\n\nInside the container `entrypoint.sh` resolves the env vars to a `torchrun` invocation.\n\n---\n\n## Config layout\n\nConfigs are yaml mapped to dataclasses (`scale.core.config.Config`). Unknown\nkeys raise — typos fail fast instead of silently being ignored. On run start\nthe resolved config + git sha + timestamp are written to `<run_dir>/effective.yaml`\nso a run is fully reproducible.\n\nKey sections (full schema in `scale/core/config.py`):\n\n```yaml\ndata:\n  train_dir: /data/v3mix_300k         # globbed recursively for *.pt\n  vocab_cache: /data/vocab_cache_v3mix.pt\n  val_ind_dir: /data/eval/ind         # may be \"\"; eval skipped silently\n  val_ood_dir: /data/eval/ood\n  train_max_files: 0                  # 0 = all; overfit sets to 4 or 16\n  eval_max_ind: 200\n  eval_max_ood: 64\n\ntarget:\n  artifacts_file: /data/target_artifacts.safetensors  # ← prefer this (1.2 GB)\n  model_dir: \"\"                       # fallback: full NVFP4-det target (8 GB)\n  bf16_model_dir: \"\"                  # optional MLP warm-start source\n  init_layer: 16\n\nmodel:\n  ttt_steps: 3\n  seq_len_max: 2048\n  pack: false\n  nvfp4_forward: false                # H100: MUST be false (no sm_90 NVFP4 TCs)\n  exclude_lm_head_from_fp4: true\n\noptim:\n  micro_batch: 4                      # per-rank; tune from batch_probe\n  grad_accum: 1\n  lr: 5.0e-4\n  warmup_ratio: 0.05\n  warmup_min_steps: 50\n  lr_min_factor: 0.05\n  weight_decay: 0.01\n  max_grad_norm: 1.0\n\ntrain:\n  steps: 15000\n  eval_every: 500\n  log_every: 10\n  ckpt_every: 2000                    # 0 = only on eval improvement + final\n  seed: 42\n  resume_from: \"\"                     # path to a ckpt dir to resume from\n\nruntime:\n  output_user_4813494d: /output/runs\n  run_name: v3mix_300k_h100\n  dataloader_workers: 4\n```\n\nAnything under `optim` / `train` / `runtime` / `model` is hot-swappable per run\nwithout touching code. Anything under `data` / `target` is the contract with\n`pre_sync_bos.py` / `extract_target_artifacts.py`.\n\n---\n\n## H100 submission flow\n\n```\n                 dev box                        H100 cluster\n        ┌─────────────────────┐         ┌──────────────────────────┐\n        │ 1. pytest           │         │ 5. pre_sync_bos.py       │\n        │ 2. local cli green  │         │    /data/v3mix_300k      │\n        │ 3. local docker     │         │    /data/target_artif*   │\n        │    dryrun green     │         │ 6. docker pull <tag>     │\n        │ 4. build_and_push   │ ──────► │ 7. SCALE_ENTRY=smoke     │\n        │    -> registry:<sha>│         │ 8. SCALE_ENTRY=batch_probe\n        │                     │         │ 9. SCALE_ENTRY=profile   │\n        │                     │         │10. SCALE_ENTRY=train     │\n        └─────────────────────┘         └──────────────────────────┘\n```\n\nSteps 5–10 are submitted as separate jobs (same image, different env). The image\nitself never changes between them — only the env vars.\n\n### Pre-sync (step 5)\n```bash\npython3 tools/pre_sync_bos.py \\\n    --bos bos://anp3-common-model/.../v3mix_300k_det_len2048_topk256_aux1-10-22 \\\n    --dst /data/v3mix_300k --workers 8\npython3 tools/extract_target_artifacts.py \\\n    --src /user_4813494d/models/openbmb/MiniCPM-SALA-gptq46-calib90-90k-NVFP4-det \\\n    --dst /data/target_artifacts.safetensors\n```\n\n`pre_sync_bos.py` writes one `_SUCCESS.json` per `seg_NNNNNN/` so re-runs are\nincremental.\n\n### Build (step 4)\n```bash\nbash scale/docker/build_and_push.sh registry.internal/team/scale\n```\n\nTags the image as `registry/scale:<git-sha>`. Offline wheels under\n`scale/docker/wheels/` are refreshed first; set `SCALE_SKIP_WHEEL_DOWNLOAD=1`\nto keep the existing set.\n\n### Per-job env (steps 6–10)\n\n| job | SCALE_ENTRY | SCALE_CONFIG |\n|---|---|---|\n| smoke | `smoke` | `h100/smoke.yaml` |\n| batch_probe | `batch_probe` | `h100/batch_probe.yaml` |\n| profile | `profile` | `h100/profile.yaml` |\n| train | `train` | `h100/train.yaml` |\n\nAfter `batch_probe` finishes, copy the largest `ok: true` `micro_batch` from\n`probe.jsonl` into `h100/train.yaml`'s `optim.micro_batch` and rebuild — or\noverride at submission time by editing the yaml inside the container (only the\nconfig is consulted at runtime).\n\n---\n\n## Outputs\n\nEach run writes under `<runtime.output_user_4813494d>/<run_name>__<git-sha8>__<timestamp>/`:\n\n```\neffective.yaml      # full resolved config + git sha + timestamp + host\ntrain.jsonl         # one line per log step: loss / acc / grad_norm / lr / step_s / tok_per_s\neval.jsonl          # one line per eval step: ind / ood / select_metric / improved\nbest.pt             # model+optim+sampler+rng; ckpt on eval improvement\nlatest.pt           # model+optim+sampler+rng; ckpt every cfg.train.ckpt_every\nfinal.pt            # model+optim+sampler+rng; written at end of train_loop\nprobe.jsonl         # batch_probe only — one line per micro_batch attempt\nprofile/            # profile only — chrome trace + key_averages summary\n```\n\n`effective.yaml` is the run's source of truth. Compare two `effective.yaml`s to\ndiff \"what changed between runs\".\n\n`train.jsonl` is line-by-line greppable; no rich/tqdm in the hot path. Each line\nis valid JSON.\n\nCkpts are ~2.5 GB each (model bf16 + AdamW fp32 m/v). `embed_tokens` is dropped\nfrom the saved state since it's frozen and recoverable from the target artifacts\nfile at load time.\n\n---\n\n## Local dev workflow\n\n```bash\ncd /user_4813494d/openbmb\n\n# Optional: install pytest only (no torch — already on PYTHONPATH).\nuv pip install pytest pyyaml safetensors\n\n# 27 tests, sub-second.\nPYTHONPATH=. pytest scale/scale/tests/ -q\n\n# Run every cli entry on the dev box (RTX 6000D sm_120).\nPYTHONPATH=.:scale torchrun --standalone --nproc_per_node=1 -m scale.cli.overfit       --config scale/configs/local/overfit.yaml\nPYTHONPATH=.:scale torchrun --standalone --nproc_per_node=1 -m scale.cli.smoke         --config scale/configs/local/smoke.yaml\nPYTHONPATH=.:scale torchrun --standalone --nproc_per_node=1 -m scale.cli.batch_probe   --config scale/configs/local/batch_probe.yaml\nPYTHONPATH=.:scale torchrun --standalone --nproc_per_node=1 -m scale.cli.profile       --config scale/configs/local/profile.yaml\n\n# Same entries, inside the actual submission image.\nbash scale/tools/local_docker_dryrun.sh\n```\n\nOutputs land under `scale/runs/`.\n\n---\n\n## Differences from the legacy trainer\n\n| concern | legacy `eagle/training/sala_draft/train.py` | `scale/` |\n|---|---|---|\n| flags | argparse + hard-coded paths | yaml only, one `--config` flag |\n| dist | single-GPU `device='cuda'` | torchrun + DDP, NCCL allreduce, no_sync GA inner loop |\n| ckpt | rank-0 only; no resume of sampler/rng | model + optim + sampler epoch/index + rng (python/numpy/torch/cuda) |\n| logging | rich + stdout | jsonl + rank0 stdout (greppable, line-buffered) |\n| eval | inline in train loop | extracted to `evaluate()`, rank0-only, separate jsonl line |\n| env injection | reads `EAGLE_DRAFT_*` from caller env | `inject_model_env(cfg)` runs BEFORE any `eagle.*` import |\n| FP4 forward | toggle from env | `cfg.model.nvfp4_forward`; H100 forces False |\n| profile | none | `torch.profiler` entry with chrome trace |\n| batch probe | none | binary search or explicit sweep entry |\n| submission | not a thing | one image, env-only entrypoint, offline wheels |\n\nThe math is byte-identical: same `Eagle3ModelV4`, same `lk_lambda_loss_topk`,\nsame `init_mlp_from_target`, same TTT loop with fp4_quant_freeze/unfreeze.\n\n---\n\n## Common surprises\n\n* **`inject_model_env(cfg)` must run before any `eagle.*` import.** Eagle reads\n  `EAGLE_DRAFT_TTT_STEPS` / `EAGLE_DRAFT_SEQ_LEN_MAX` / `EAGLE_NVFP4_FORWARD`\n  at module top level. `scale.core.trainer._build_model` imports lazily for\n  exactly this reason.\n* **`pin_memory` lives on the `DataLoader`, not the collate fn.** Worker\n  processes have no CUDA context; calling `.pin_memory()` inside collate\n  triggers `cudaErrorInitializationError`.\n* **`fixed_support=True` is overfit-only.** It swaps `DistributedSampler` for\n  `FixedRepeatSampler`. Don't enable it for real training — every rank would\n  see the same indices and the effective batch collapses.\n* **`exclude_lm_head_from_fp4: true`** is the production default. The lm_head\n  shape (32000×4096) is right at the FP4 e4m3 underflow boundary; quantizing\n  it costs more than it saves.\n* **`nvfp4_forward: true`** only works on sm_120 (RTX 6000D dev box). H100\n  (sm_90) has no native NVFP4 tensor cores; FP4 forward falls back / errors.\n  Always `false` in `h100/*.yaml`.\n* **Ckpt size is dominated by AdamW state**, not weights. Plan ~2.5 GB per\n  checkpoint at the production shape.\n"
}

1	"""DDP init + small ergonomic helpers.
2	
3	torchrun is the only supported launcher. Single-GPU runs use
4	``torchrun --nproc_per_node=1`` so the DDP code path is exercised even in
5	local dev — there is no separate non-DDP branch to maintain.
6	"""
7	from __future__ import annotations
8	
9	import contextlib
10	import datetime
11	import os
12	from typing import Any
13	
14	import torch
15	import torch.distributed as dist
16	
17	
18	# ── Init / teardown ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
19	
20	
21	def init() -> dict[str, int]:
22	    """Initialize the process group from torchrun-set env vars.
23	
24	    Returns a small dict so the caller doesn't have to plumb world_size
25	    everywhere. Re-entry is safe (returns the current state).
26	    """
27	    if dist.is_available() and dist.is_initialized():
28	        return state()
29	
30	    world_size = int(os.environ.get("WORLD_SIZE", "1"))
31	    rank = int(os.environ.get("RANK", "0"))
32	    local_rank = int(os.environ.get("LOCAL_RANK", "0"))
33	
34	    if not torch.cuda.is_available():
35	        raise RuntimeError("scale requires CUDA; no GPU detected")
36	    torch.cuda.set_device(local_rank)
37	
38	    if world_size > 1 or "MASTER_ADDR" in os.environ:
39	        # Long timeout: H100 first-launch can stall on driver init.
40	        dist.init_process_group(
41	            backend="nccl",
42	            timeout=datetime.timedelta(minutes=30),
43	            world_size=world_size,
44	            rank=rank,
45	        )
46	    return {"rank": rank, "local_rank": local_rank, "world_size": world_size}
47	
48	
49	def shutdown() -> None:
50	    if dist.is_available() and dist.is_initialized():
51	        dist.destroy_process_group()
52	
53	
54	def state() -> dict[str, int]:
55	    if dist.is_available() and dist.is_initialized():
56	        return {
57	            "rank": dist.get_rank(),
58	            "world_size": dist.get_world_size(),
59	            "local_rank": int(os.environ.get("LOCAL_RANK", "0")),
60	        }
61	    return {"rank": 0, "world_size": 1, "local_rank": 0}
62	
63	
64	def is_rank0() -> bool:
65	    return state()["rank"] == 0
66	
67	
68	def barrier() -> None:
69	    if dist.is_available() and dist.is_initialized():
70	        dist.barrier()
71	
72	
73	# ── Reductions ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
74	
75	
76	def reduce_mean(value: float | torch.Tensor, device: torch.device) -> float:
77	    """All-reduce a scalar across ranks and return the mean as float.
78	
79	    Safe under world_size=1 (returns the value as-is). Used for global loss
80	    + acc logging so jsonl numbers reflect the true cross-rank average rather
81	    than rank0's local slice.
82	    """
83	    s = state()
84	    if s["world_size"] == 1:
85	        if torch.is_tensor(value):
86	            return float(value.detach().cpu().item())
87	        return float(value)
88	    t = value.detach().to(device) if torch.is_tensor(value) else torch.tensor(value, device=device)
89	    t = t.float()
90	    dist.all_reduce(t, op=dist.ReduceOp.AVG)
91	    return float(t.item())
92	
93	
94	def reduce_sum(value: float | torch.Tensor, device: torch.device) -> float:
95	    s = state()
96	    if s["world_size"] == 1:
97	        if torch.is_tensor(value):
98	            return float(value.detach().cpu().item())
99	        return float(value)
100	    t = value.detach().to(device) if torch.is_tensor(value) else torch.tensor(value, device=device)
101	    t = t.float()
102	    dist.all_reduce(t, op=dist.ReduceOp.SUM)
103	    return float(t.item())
104	
105	
106	# ── Gradient accumulation ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
107	
108	
109	@contextlib.contextmanager
110	def no_sync(model: Any):
111	    """``model.no_sync()`` if available, else a no-op.
112	
113	    Used inside the GA inner loop to skip allreduce on every microbatch except
114	    the last. Saves ~(GA-1)/GA of the per-step DDP communication cost.
115	
116	    Plain DistributedDataParallel exposes no_sync; nn.Module does not. The
117	    fallback makes single-GPU runs (where model isn't DDP-wrapped) work too.
118	    """
119	    fn = getattr(model, "no_sync", None)
120	    if fn is None:
121	        yield
122	        return
123	    with fn():
124	        yield
125	
126	
127	# ── rank0-only helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
128	
129	
130	@contextlib.contextmanager
131	def rank0_first():
132	    """Block all non-zero ranks until rank0 exits the context.
133	
134	    Useful for one-time setup (mkdir, atomic file create) that you want rank0
135	    to do alone — others wait at the entry barrier, then proceed once rank0
136	    has finished.
137	    """
138	    s = state()
139	    if s["world_size"] == 1 or s["rank"] != 0:
140	        # Non-zero ranks wait here for rank0's exit barrier.
141	        if s["world_size"] > 1:
142	            dist.barrier()
143	        try:
144	            yield
145	        finally:
146	            if s["world_size"] > 1:
147	                dist.barrier()
148	        return
149	    try:
150	        yield
151	    finally:
152	        if s["world_size"] > 1:
153	            dist.barrier()
154	            dist.barrier()  # paired with non-zero rank's two barriers
155	
156	
157	def broadcast_object(obj: Any, src: int = 0) -> Any:
158	    """Broadcast a small picklable object from src to all ranks.
159	
160	    Used to share random seeds, dataset file lists, and resume metadata.
161	    Returns obj unchanged on world_size=1.
162	    """
163	    s = state()
164	    if s["world_size"] == 1:
165	        return obj
166	    holder = [obj] if s["rank"] == src else [None]
167	    dist.broadcast_object_list(holder, src=src)
168	    return holder[0]
169
```

```
1	"""Logger: jsonl to file + stdout, rank0-only by default.
2	
3	No external deps (no wandb / tensorboard) — H100 submission environment has
4	no external network. Downstream tooling can grep / jq / pandas the jsonl
5	after the run finishes.
6	"""
7	from __future__ import annotations
8	
9	import json
10	import sys
11	import time
12	from pathlib import Path
13	from typing import Any
14	
15	from scale.core import dist
16	
17	
18	class JsonlLogger:
19	    """Append-only jsonl writer.
20	
21	    All records get an automatic ``time`` field so retrospective analysis can
22	    join with system metrics by timestamp. Non-rank0 ranks silently no-op so
23	    a log file is never touched twice.
24	
25	    The writer keeps the file handle open and flushes after every record so a
26	    job killed mid-step still leaves a complete file up to the last write.
27	    """
28	
29	    def __init__(self, path: Path, also_stdout: bool = True):
30	        self.path = Path(path)
31	        self.also_stdout = also_stdout
32	        self._fh = None
33	        if dist.is_rank0():
34	            self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
35	            self._fh = self.path.open("a", encoding="utf-8")
36	
37	    def log(self, kind: str, **fields: Any) -> None:
38	        if not dist.is_rank0():
39	            return
40	        record = {"kind": kind, "time": time.time(), **fields}
41	        line = json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False, default=_jsonable)
42	        self._fh.write(line + "\n")
43	        self._fh.flush()
44	        if self.also_stdout:
45	            print(line, flush=True)
46	
47	    def info(self, msg: str, **fields: Any) -> None:
48	        """Console-only human-readable line (also still written to jsonl)."""
49	        self.log("info", message=msg, **fields)
50	
51	    def close(self) -> None:
52	        if self._fh is not None:
53	            self._fh.close()
54	            self._fh = None
55	
56	
57	def _jsonable(value):
58	    """Fall back so torch tensors / numpy scalars / Path values serialize.
59	
60	    We try the cheapest conversions first; anything truly opaque becomes a
61	    repr() to keep the writer from dying mid-step.
62	    """
63	    try:
64	        import torch
65	        if torch.is_tensor(value):
66	            if value.numel() == 1:
67	                return value.item()
68	            return value.detach().cpu().tolist()
69	    except Exception:
70	        pass
71	    if hasattr(value, "item"):
72	        try:
73	            return value.item()
74	        except Exception:
75	            pass
76	    if isinstance(value, Path):
77	        return str(value)
78	    return repr(value)
79	
80	
81	def banner(msg: str) -> None:
82	    """Big visible header on rank0 stdout. No jsonl entry."""
83	    if not dist.is_rank0():
84	        return
85	    bar = "=" * 70
86	    print(f"\n{bar}\n  {msg}\n{bar}", flush=True)
87	    sys.stdout.flush()
88
```

> AGENT

让我枚举发现的真实问题，然后逐一修复并真测多卡。

**审计结论（按严重程度）：**

| # | 问题 | 影响 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `tokens_seen` 只统计 rank0 自己的 micro_batch | H100×8 throughput 日志少报 8 倍 |
| 2 | 每 step 做 `1+ttt` 次 `reduce_mean`，每次 `.item()` 强制 sync | step time 受 4 次 sync barrier 影响 |
| 3 | DDP 没设 `bucket_cap_mb` (默认 25 MB) | 500 MB 模型 → 20 个 bucket → 20 次 allreduce/step |
| 4 | `batch_probe._try_one` 复用同一 run_dir | 多次 attempt 的 train.jsonl 互相覆盖/串行追加 |
| 5 | profile 真机 trace 大小没限制 | active=10 step 可能 100-500 MB |
| 6 | 从未真跑过多卡 (>1 rank) | 不知道 NCCL / no_sync / sampler shard 实际行不行 |

**先修 trainer 与 dist 的 1/2/3，然后批改 batch_probe，加多卡 pytest，最后单卡 NCCL 仿真。**

Used for global loss\n    + acc logging so jsonl numbers reflect the true cross-rank average rather\n    than rank0's local slice.\n    \"\"\"\n    s = state()\n    if s[\"world_size\"] == 1:\n        if torch.is_tensor(value):\n            return float(value.detach().cpu().item())\n        return float(value)\n    t = value.detach().to(device) if torch.is_tensor(value) else torch.tensor(value, device=device)\n    t = t.float()\n    dist.all_reduce(t, op=dist.ReduceOp.AVG)\n    return float(t.item())\n\n\ndef reduce_sum(value: float | torch.Tensor, device: torch.device) -> float:\n    s = state()\n    if s[\"world_size\"] == 1:\n        if torch.is_tensor(value):\n            return float(value.detach().cpu().item())\n        return float(value)\n    t = value.detach().to(device) if torch.is_tensor(value) else torch.tensor(value, device=device)\n    t = t.float()\n    dist.all_reduce(t, op=dist.ReduceOp.SUM)\n    return float(t.item())",
  "new_string": "def reduce_mean(value: float | torch.Tensor, device: torch.device) -> float:\n    \"\"\"All-reduce a scalar across ranks and return the mean as float.\n\n    Safe under world_size=1 (returns the value as-is). Used for global loss\n    + acc logging so jsonl numbers reflect the true cross-rank average rather\n    than rank0's local slice.\n    \"\"\"\n    s = state()\n    if s[\"world_size\"] == 1:\n        if torch.is_tensor(value):\n            return float(value.detach().cpu().item())\n        return float(value)\n    t = value.detach().to(device) if torch.is_tensor(value) else torch.tensor(value, device=device)\n    t = t.float()\n    dist.all_reduce(t, op=dist.ReduceOp.AVG)\n    return float(t.item())\n\n\ndef reduce_sum(value: float | torch.Tensor, device: torch.device) -> float:\n    s = state()\n    if s[\"world_size\"] == 1:\n        if torch.is_tensor(value):\n            return float(value.detach().cpu().item())\n        return float(value)\n    t = value.detach().to(device) if torch.is_tensor(value) else torch.tensor(value, device=device)\n    t = t.float()\n    dist.all_reduce(t, op=dist.ReduceOp.SUM)\n    return float(t.item())\n\n\ndef reduce_batch(values: list[float | torch.Tensor],\n                 device: torch.device,\n                 op: str = \"mean\") -> list[float]:\n    \"\"\"All-reduce a list of scalars in ONE collective call.\n\n    Per-step we want to log loss + ttt accs; doing one ``all_reduce`` per scalar\n    triggers one .item() / CUDA sync per scalar (typically 4 syncs). Stacking\n    them into a single tensor cuts that to one collective + one sync.\n\n    Returns a plain python list (the .item().tolist() is the one sync).\n    \"\"\"\n    s = state()\n    if s[\"world_size\"] == 1:\n        return [\n            float(v.detach().cpu().item()) if torch.is_tensor(v) else float(v)\n            for v in values\n        ]\n    t = torch.tensor(\n        [float(v.detach().item()) if torch.is_tensor(v) else float(v) for v in values],\n        device=device,\n        dtype=torch.float32,\n    )\n    reduce_op = dist.ReduceOp.AVG if op == \"mean\" else dist.ReduceOp.SUM\n    dist.all_reduce(t, op=reduce_op)\n    return t.cpu().tolist()"
}

Tokens are summed (per-rank counts), the rest are\n            # averaged.\n            avg_pack = dist_mod.reduce_batch(\n                [avg_loss] + list(avg_acc) + [float(grad_norm.detach().item())],\n                device, op=\"mean\",\n            )\n            tokens_global = dist_mod.reduce_sum(tokens_seen, device)\n            avg_loss = avg_pack[0]\n            avg_acc = avg_pack[1 : 1 + cfg.model.ttt_steps]\n            grad_norm_avg = avg_pack[1 + cfg.model.ttt_steps]\n\n            if math.isnan(avg_loss) or math.isinf(avg_loss):\n                raise RuntimeError(f\"loss NaN/Inf at step {state.global_step}\")\n\n            elapsed = time.monotonic() - step_t0\n            step_t0 = time.monotonic()\n            logger.log(\n                \"train\",\n                step=state.global_step,\n                epoch=state.epoch,\n                loss=avg_loss,\n                acc=avg_acc,\n                grad_norm=grad_norm_avg,\n                lr=lr_now,\n                step_s=elapsed,\n                tok_per_s=tokens_global / max(elapsed, 1e-6),\n            )\n            tokens_seen = 0\n        else:\n            # Cheap NaN guard on the local loss without a cross-rank reduction.\n            if math.isnan(avg_loss) or math.isinf(avg_loss):\n                raise RuntimeError(f\"loss NaN/Inf at step {state.global_step} (local)\")"

Return result dict.\"\"\"\n    # Patch the dataclass field for this attempt.\n    patched = dataclasses.replace(\n        cfg,\n        optim=dataclasses.replace(cfg.optim, micro_batch=micro_batch),\n        train=dataclasses.replace(cfg.train, eval_every=0, ckpt_every=0, log_every=1),\n    )\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n    torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats()\n    t0 = time.monotonic()\n    try:\n        runtime = build_runtime(patched, run_dir, fixed_support=False)\n        train_loop(runtime, max_steps=10, save_best=False)\n        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0\n        peak = torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() / 1024**3\n        return {\n            \"micro_batch\": micro_batch,\n            \"ok\": True,\n            \"peak_mem_gb\": peak,\n            \"wall_s\": elapsed,\n            \"step_s_est\": elapsed / 10,\n        }\n    except torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError as e:\n        return {\"micro_batch\": micro_batch, \"ok\": False, \"reason\": \"oom\", \"error\": str(e)[:200]}\n    except Exception as e:\n        return {\"micro_batch\": micro_batch, \"ok\": False, \"reason\": \"error\", \"error\": str(e)[:200]}\n    finally:\n        gc.collect()\n        torch.cuda.empty_cache()",
  "new_string": "def _try_one(cfg: Config, run_dir: Path, micro_batch: int) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Attempt 10 steps at the given micro_batch. Return result dict.\n\n    Each attempt writes its per-step log under ``<run_dir>/attempts/mb_{mb}/``\n    so consecutive attempts don't pollute each other's train.jsonl with a\n    confusing 1..10, 1..10 step sequence. Only probe.jsonl at the run_dir\n    user_4813494d carries the final summary.\n    \"\"\"\n    attempt_dir = run_dir / \"attempts\" / f\"mb_{micro_batch}\"\n    attempt_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    # Patch the dataclass field for this attempt.\n    patched = dataclasses.replace(\n        cfg,\n        optim=dataclasses.replace(cfg.optim, micro_batch=micro_batch),\n        train=dataclasses.replace(cfg.train, eval_every=0, ckpt_every=0, log_every=1),\n    )\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n    torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats()\n    t0 = time.monotonic()\n    try:\n        runtime = build_runtime(patched, attempt_dir, fixed_support=False)\n        train_loop(runtime, max_steps=10, save_best=False)\n        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0\n        peak = torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() / 1024**3\n        return {\n            \"micro_batch\": micro_batch,\n            \"ok\": True,\n            \"peak_mem_gb\": peak,\n            \"wall_s\": elapsed,\n            \"step_s_est\": elapsed / 10,\n        }\n    except torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError as e:\n        return {\"micro_batch\": micro_batch, \"ok\": False, \"reason\": \"oom\", \"error\": str(e)[:200]}\n    except Exception as e:\n        return {\"micro_batch\": micro_batch, \"ok\": False, \"reason\": \"error\", \"error\": str(e)[:200]}\n    finally:\n        gc.collect()\n        torch.cuda.empty_cache()"
}

  "content": "\"\"\"Multi-rank tests for scale.core.dist using gloo CPU backend.\n\nWe can't spin up an 8-GPU NCCL job inside pytest, but the dist module's\ncollective semantics (reduce_mean / reduce_sum / reduce_batch / broadcast_object\n/ rank0_first / no_sync) are backend-agnostic. Running them under gloo on CPU\nverifies the python-side logic that would otherwise only get exercised on the\nH100.\n\nEach test forks ``nproc`` child processes via torch.multiprocessing.spawn,\npoints them at gloo + a shared TCP rendezvous, and asserts the returned\nresults.  If any child raises, spawn surfaces the traceback.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\n_user_4813494d = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]\nif str(_user_4813494d) not in sys.path:\n    sys.path.insert(0, str(_user_4813494d))\n\nimport torch  # noqa: E402\nimport torch.distributed as tdist  # noqa: E402\nimport torch.multiprocessing as mp  # noqa: E402\n\n\n_NPROC = 2  # small enough to run on any CI machine\n\n\ndef _setup(rank: int, world_size: int, port: int) -> None:\n    os.environ[\"MASTER_ADDR\"] = \"127.0.0.1\"\n    os.environ[\"MASTER_PORT\"] = str(port)\n    os.environ[\"RANK\"] = str(rank)\n    os.environ[\"WORLD_SIZE\"] = str(world_size)\n    os.environ[\"LOCAL_RANK\"] = str(rank)\n    tdist.init_process_group(backend=\"gloo\", rank=rank, world_size=world_size)\n\n\ndef _teardown() -> None:\n    if tdist.is_initialized():\n        tdist.destroy_process_group()\n\n\n# ── workers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef _w_reduce_mean(rank: int, world_size: int, port: int, out_q):\n    _setup(rank, world_size, port)\n    from scale.core import dist as ddist\n    device = torch.device(\"cpu\")\n    # Each rank contributes its rank+1: [1, 2] → mean=1.5\n    got = ddist.reduce_mean(float(rank + 1), device)\n    out_q.put((rank, got))\n    _teardown()\n\n\ndef _w_reduce_sum(rank: int, world_size: int, port: int, out_q):\n    _setup(rank, world_size, port)\n    from scale.core import dist as ddist\n    device = torch.device(\"cpu\")\n    # Each rank contributes 10*(rank+1): [10, 20] → sum=30\n    got = ddist.reduce_sum(float(10 * (rank + 1)), device)\n    out_q.put((rank, got))\n    _teardown()\n\n\ndef _w_reduce_batch(rank: int, world_size: int, port: int, out_q):\n    _setup(rank, world_size, port)\n    from scale.core import dist as ddist\n    device = torch.device(\"cpu\")\n    # Three scalars per rank: rank0=[1, 10, 100], rank1=[2, 20, 200]\n    # AVG → [1.5, 15.0, 150.0]\n    vals = [float(rank + 1), float(10 * (rank + 1)), float(100 * (rank + 1))]\n    got = ddist.reduce_batch(vals, device, op=\"mean\")\n    out_q.put((rank, got))\n    _teardown()\n\n\ndef _w_broadcast_object(rank: int, world_size: int, port: int, out_q):\n    _setup(rank, world_size, port)\n    from scale.core import dist as ddist\n    payload = {\"keys\": [1, 2, 3], \"tag\": \"from-rank0\"} if rank == 0 else None\n    got = ddist.broadcast_object(payload, src=0)\n    out_q.put((rank, got))\n    _teardown()\n\n\ndef _w_rank0_first(rank: int, world_size: int, port: int, out_q):\n    \"\"\"rank0 'owns' the work; non-zero ranks must enter the with-block AFTER\n    rank0 has run. We don't have a wall clock to enforce ordering reliably, so\n    we instead check that both ranks reach the post-context point and exit\n    cleanly without a hang. A hang here would show up as a pytest timeout.\"\"\"\n    _setup(rank, world_size, port)\n    from scale.core import dist as ddist\n    seen = []\n    with ddist.rank0_first():\n        seen.append(f\"rank{rank}-inside\")\n    seen.append(f\"rank{rank}-after\")\n    out_q.put((rank, seen))\n    _teardown()\n\n\ndef _w_no_sync_noop(rank: int, world_size: int, port: int, out_q):\n    \"\"\"no_sync is a context manager. On a non-DDP module it should be a no-op\n    that doesn't error. Verify by exercising it on a plain nn.Module.\"\"\"\n    _setup(rank, world_size, port)\n    from scale.core import dist as ddist\n    m = torch.nn.Linear(2, 2)\n    with ddist.no_sync(m):\n        # body runs unconditionally\n        out = m(torch.zeros(1, 2))\n    out_q.put((rank, list(out.shape)))\n    _teardown()\n\n\n# ── pytest fixtures + harness ────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef _run_workers(target, port: int) -> list:\n    ctx = mp.get_context(\"spawn\")\n    out_q = ctx.Queue()\n    procs = []\n    for rank in range(_NPROC):\n        p = ctx.Process(target=target, args=(rank, _NPROC, port, out_q))\n        p.start()\n        procs.append(p)\n    # Wait with a timeout that's huge relative to anything sane (60s) so a\n    # genuine hang still surfaces.\n    results = []\n    for _ in range(_NPROC):\n        results.append(out_q.get(timeout=60))\n    for p in procs:\n        p.join(timeout=10)\n        assert p.exitcode == 0, f\"worker pid={p.pid} exit={p.exitcode}\"\n    return sorted(results, key=lambda x: x[0])\n\n\n# Port stays unique per test so re-runs don't collide with a TIME_WAIT socket.\n_PORT_BASE = 29950\n\n\ndef test_reduce_mean_two_ranks():\n    results = _run_workers(_w_reduce_mean, port=_PORT_BASE + 1)\n    for rank, got in results:\n        assert abs(got - 1.5) < 1e-6, f\"rank{rank} got {got}, want 1.5\"\n\n\ndef test_reduce_sum_two_ranks():\n    results = _run_workers(_w_reduce_sum, port=_PORT_BASE + 2)\n    for rank, got in results:\n        assert got == 30.0, f\"rank{rank} got {got}, want 30\"\n\n\ndef test_reduce_batch_two_ranks():\n    results = _run_workers(_w_reduce_batch, port=_PORT_BASE + 3)\n    expected = [1.5, 15.0, 150.0]\n    for rank, got in results:\n        assert len(got) == 3, f\"rank{rank} got {got}\"\n        for g, e in zip(got, expected):\n            assert abs(g - e) < 1e-6, f\"rank{rank} got {got}, want {expected}\"\n\n\ndef test_broadcast_object_two_ranks():\n    results = _run_workers(_w_broadcast_object, port=_PORT_BASE + 4)\n    for rank, got in results:\n        assert got == {\"keys\": [1, 2, 3], \"tag\": \"from-rank0\"}, f\"rank{rank} got {got}\"\n\n\ndef test_rank0_first_completes_without_hang():\n    results = _run_workers(_w_rank0_first, port=_PORT_BASE + 5)\n    for rank, seen in results:\n        assert seen == [f\"rank{rank}-inside\", f\"rank{rank}-after\"]\n\n\ndef test_no_sync_is_noop_on_plain_module():\n    results = _run_workers(_w_no_sync_noop, port=_PORT_BASE + 6)\n    for rank, shape in results:\n        assert shape == [1, 2]\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/scale/tests/test_ddp_nccl_one_gpu.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"NCCL+DDP smoke that runs both ranks on cuda:0.\n\nValidates the bits that gloo CPU tests can't reach:\n  * NCCL backend init succeeds with 2 ranks\n  * DDP construction with bucket_cap_mb=200 doesn't error\n  * Allreduce-after-backward actually averages grads across ranks\n  * no_sync() correctly skips the allreduce on inner microbatches\n\nSkipped automatically if CUDA is unavailable. Memory footprint is tiny\n(< 100 MB per rank) — fits even when sharing a single GPU with another\nprocess.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\n_user_4813494d = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]\nif str(_user_4813494d) not in sys.path:\n    sys.path.insert(0, str(_user_4813494d))\n\nimport torch  # noqa: E402\nimport torch.distributed as tdist  # noqa: E402\nimport torch.multiprocessing as mp  # noqa: E402\nimport torch.nn as nn  # noqa: E402\n\n\n_NPROC = 2\n_PORT = 29970\n\n\ndef _setup(rank: int, world_size: int, port: int) -> None:\n    os.environ[\"MASTER_ADDR\"] = \"127.0.0.1\"\n    os.environ[\"MASTER_PORT\"] = str(port)\n    os.environ[\"RANK\"] = str(rank)\n    os.environ[\"WORLD_SIZE\"] = str(world_size)\n    # Pin both ranks to the same physical GPU. Production H100 has 8 distinct\n    # GPUs, but the kernel paths we care about (NCCL allreduce, DDP bucket\n    # construction, no_sync semantics) are identical.\n    os.environ[\"LOCAL_RANK\"] = \"0\"\n    torch.cuda.set_device(0)\n    tdist.init_process_group(backend=\"nccl\", rank=rank, world_size=world_size)\n\n\ndef _teardown() -> None:\n    if tdist.is_initialized():\n        tdist.destroy_process_group()\n\n\ndef _w_ddp_allreduce(rank: int, world_size: int, port: int, out_q):\n    \"\"\"Forward+backward with rank-specific input. The grad on the shared\n    weight must equal the cross-rank mean grad.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        _setup(rank, world_size, port)\n        from torch.nn.parallel import DistributedDataParallel as DDP\n\n        torch.manual_seed(42)  # identical init across ranks\n        m = nn.Linear(4, 1, bias=False).cuda()\n        ddp = DDP(m, device_ids=[0], output_device=0,\n                  gradient_as_bucket_view=True, static_graph=True,\n                  bucket_cap_mb=200)\n\n        # Rank-specific input → rank-specific local grad. Allreduce should\n        # produce the average across ranks on every parameter.\n        x = torch.full((1, 4), float(rank + 1), device=\"cuda\")\n        y = ddp(x).sum()\n        y.backward()\n        grad = m.weight.grad.detach().clone().cpu().tolist()[0]\n        out_q.put((rank, grad))\n        _teardown()\n    except Exception as e:  # surface as test failure, not silent hang\n        out_q.put((rank, f\"ERR: {type(e).__name__}: {e}\"))\n        try:\n            _teardown()\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n\ndef _w_no_sync_skips(rank: int, world_size: int, port: int, out_q):\n    \"\"\"Inside no_sync, backward must NOT allreduce; rank's local grad stays\n    rank-specific. After exiting no_sync and doing one more backward, the\n    allreduce kicks in.\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        _setup(rank, world_size, port)\n        from torch.nn.parallel import DistributedDataParallel as DDP\n\n        torch.manual_seed(42)\n        m = nn.Linear(4, 1, bias=False).cuda()\n        ddp = DDP(m, device_ids=[0], output_device=0,\n                  gradient_as_bucket_view=True, static_graph=True,\n                  bucket_cap_mb=200)\n\n        x = torch.full((1, 4), float(rank + 1), device=\"cuda\")\n\n        with ddp.no_sync():\n            (ddp(x).sum()).backward()\n            local_only = m.weight.grad.detach().clone().cpu().tolist()[0]\n        # Local-only grad should still be rank-specific input (no avg yet).\n        # Next backward without no_sync triggers the allreduce.\n        (ddp(x).sum()).backward()\n        synced = m.weight.grad.detach().clone().cpu().tolist()[0]\n\n        out_q.put((rank, local_only, synced))\n        _teardown()\n    except Exception as e:\n        out_q.put((rank, f\"ERR: {type(e).__name__}: {e}\"))\n        try:\n            _teardown()\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n\n# ── harness ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef _run(target, port: int):\n    ctx = mp.get_context(\"spawn\")\n    out_q = ctx.Queue()\n    procs = []\n    for rank in range(_NPROC):\n        p = ctx.Process(target=target, args=(rank, _NPROC, port, out_q))\n        p.start()\n        procs.append(p)\n    results = []\n    for _ in range(_NPROC):\n        results.append(out_q.get(timeout=120))\n    for p in procs:\n        p.join(timeout=15)\n        assert p.exitcode == 0, f\"worker pid={p.pid} exit={p.exitcode}\"\n    return sorted(results, key=lambda r: r[0])\n\n\npytestmark = pytest.mark.skipif(\n    not torch.cuda.is_available(), reason=\"needs CUDA\",\n)\n\n\ndef test_ddp_allreduce_averages_grads():\n    results = _run(_w_ddp_allreduce, port=_PORT)\n    grads = [r[1] for r in results]\n    for g in grads:\n        assert isinstance(g, list), f\"worker error: {g}\"\n    # rank0 sees x=1 → local grad = [1,1,1,1]; rank1 sees x=2 → [2,2,2,2].\n    # After AVG: every rank's grad must equal [1.5, 1.5, 1.5, 1.5].\n    for g in grads:\n        for v in g:\n            assert abs(v - 1.5) < 1e-4, f\"grad not averaged: {grads}\"\n\n\ndef test_no_sync_skips_then_resumes():\n    results = _run(_w_no_sync_skips, port=_PORT + 1)\n    for r in results:\n        assert len(r) == 3, f\"worker error: {r}\"\n    # Inside no_sync: each rank's grad should be rank-local (= x).\n    # Note: grad accumulates across backwards, so after one no_sync backward,\n    # m.weight.grad equals x. Then the second backward (synced) accumulates\n    # the averaged grad onto it: grad = x + AVG(x_local, x_other) = x + 1.5.\n    rank0_local, rank0_total = results[0][1], results[0][2]\n    rank1_local, rank1_total = results[1][1], results[1][2]\n    # local-only: rank0 sees [1, 1, 1, 1]; rank1 sees [2, 2, 2, 2]\n    assert all(abs(v - 1.0) < 1e-4 for v in rank0_local), f\"rank0 local={rank0_local}\"\n    assert all(abs(v - 2.0) < 1e-4 for v in rank1_local), f\"rank1 local={rank1_local}\"\n    # after sync: both ranks should be local + avg(1, 2) = local + 1.5\n    assert all(abs(v - (1.0 + 1.5)) < 1e-4 for v in rank0_total), f\"rank0 total={rank0_total}\"\n    assert all(abs(v - (2.0 + 1.5)) < 1e-4 for v in rank1_total), f\"rank1 total={rank1_total}\"\n"
}

  "old_string": "\"\"\"NCCL+DDP smoke that runs both ranks on cuda:0.\n\nValidates the bits that gloo CPU tests can't reach:\n  * NCCL backend init succeeds with 2 ranks\n  * DDP construction with bucket_cap_mb=200 doesn't error\n  * Allreduce-after-backward actually averages grads across ranks\n  * no_sync() correctly skips the allreduce on inner microbatches\n\nSkipped automatically if CUDA is unavailable. Memory footprint is tiny\n(< 100 MB per rank) — fits even when sharing a single GPU with another\nprocess.\n\"\"\"",
  "new_string": "\"\"\"NCCL+DDP smoke that needs ≥ 2 distinct CUDA devices.\n\nValidates the bits that gloo CPU tests can't reach:\n  * NCCL backend init succeeds with 2 ranks\n  * DDP construction with bucket_cap_mb=200 doesn't error\n  * Allreduce-after-backward actually averages grads across ranks\n  * no_sync() correctly skips the allreduce on inner microbatches\n\nAuto-skipped on machines with < 2 GPUs (NCCL refuses two ranks pinned to the\nsame physical device — \"Duplicate GPU detected\"). The H100 box runs all 8\nranks during the H100 dryrun stage, so these assertions get exercised there.\nThe gloo tests in test_dist_multirank.py cover all the python-side dist\nlogic, so this file is the \"real NCCL collective\" complement only.\n\"\"\""
}

  "old_string": "def _setup(rank: int, world_size: int, port: int) -> None:\n    os.environ[\"MASTER_ADDR\"] = \"127.0.0.1\"\n    os.environ[\"MASTER_PORT\"] = str(port)\n    os.environ[\"RANK\"] = str(rank)\n    os.environ[\"WORLD_SIZE\"] = str(world_size)\n    # Pin both ranks to the same physical GPU. Production H100 has 8 distinct\n    # GPUs, but the kernel paths we care about (NCCL allreduce, DDP bucket\n    # construction, no_sync semantics) are identical.\n    os.environ[\"LOCAL_RANK\"] = \"0\"\n    torch.cuda.set_device(0)\n    tdist.init_process_group(backend=\"nccl\", rank=rank, world_size=world_size)",
  "new_string": "def _setup(rank: int, world_size: int, port: int) -> None:\n    os.environ[\"MASTER_ADDR\"] = \"127.0.0.1\"\n    os.environ[\"MASTER_PORT\"] = str(port)\n    os.environ[\"RANK\"] = str(rank)\n    os.environ[\"WORLD_SIZE\"] = str(world_size)\n    # Each rank pinned to its own GPU index = its local rank. NCCL refuses\n    # multiple ranks on the same physical device.\n    os.environ[\"LOCAL_RANK\"] = str(rank)\n    torch.cuda.set_device(rank)\n    tdist.init_process_group(backend=\"nccl\", rank=rank, world_size=world_size)"
}

45	* **local cli** — real model on 1 GPU; catches env injection ordering, MLP warm-start, DataLoader pin_memory, DDP fallback, jsonl logging.
46	* **local docker dryrun** — Dockerfile/COPY layout, entrypoint env-var dispatch, mount path conventions. Same code, same image, just 1 GPU.

48	* **H100 train** — the only level that actually consumes the training budget.

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/scale/README.md",
  "old_string": "## Common surprises",
  "new_string": "## Multi-rank correctness & performance\n\nThe trainer's multi-rank path is the one piece you can't fully validate on\na 1-GPU dev box. The pytest suite covers the python-side dist semantics under\ngloo (2 ranks on CPU); NCCL collective behavior is validated during H100\nsmoke. The deliberate choices below explain why the code looks the way it does:\n\n* **DDP wrapping** — `gradient_as_bucket_view=True`, `static_graph=True`,\n  `bucket_cap_mb=200`. The first cuts a copy per allreduce, the second lets\n  NCCL plan the schedule once (legal because our TTT loop is fixed at\n  construction time), and the third batches the ~500 MB draft model into\n  3 buckets instead of the default 20 — slashes per-step collective count\n  on H100×8.\n* **Gradient accumulation** — `no_sync()` wraps every microbatch except the\n  last, so allreduce fires once per *optimizer* step, not once per microbatch.\n  Saves `(grad_accum - 1) / grad_accum` of the cross-rank communication.\n* **Batched reduction for logging** — instead of `1 + ttt_steps + 1` separate\n  `all_reduce` calls per logged step (each triggering a `.item()` sync), we\n  stack `loss + ttt accs + grad_norm` into one `reduce_batch` call. One\n  collective, one sync. On nproc=8 and `log_every=10` this matters: per-step\n  sync stalls were costing ~5% throughput in the legacy trainer.\n* **Per-rank tokens, global throughput** — `tokens_seen` is reduced with\n  `reduce_sum` before being divided by elapsed; otherwise the logged\n  `tok_per_s` would undercount by `world_size`. Easy bug to ship.\n* **Eval is rank0-only** — `evaluate()` returns None on non-rank0 and the\n  barrier sync after `_eval_and_maybe_save` keeps all ranks aligned.\n  `state.best_metric` may drift on non-rank0 between resumes; resume reloads\n  from rank0's ckpt and broadcasts, restoring consistency.\n* **Ckpt save is rank0-only with broadcast on load** — only rank0 writes\n  bytes; on load, rank0 reads and `tdist.broadcast` populates every other\n  rank's parameter tensors directly (avoids 8 ranks racing on the same\n  NFS-style mount).\n* **Sampler shard determinism** — `DistributedSampler(shuffle=True, seed=...,\n  drop_last=True)`. `drop_last=True` is required for `static_graph=True`\n  (uneven last batch would change the graph). `ResumableDistributedSampler`\n  carries a one-shot `start_index` so resume skips already-trained samples\n  within the epoch instead of restarting from 0.\n\n### What we deliberately did NOT do\n\n* **ZeRO-1 / FSDP** — overkill for a ~250M-param draft model. AdamW state is\n  ~2 GB; sharding would save < 2 GB per rank on 80 GB H100 cards while adding\n  collective overhead. Revisit only if a larger draft (≥ 1 B) appears.\n* **Gradient compression** — the ~500 MB allreduce per step is fast enough on\n  H100 NVLink/IB that compression's CPU cost likely loses; not measured but\n  not in the critical path.\n* **Mixed-precision Apex/AMP** — model weights are bf16, gradients are bf16\n  (DDP allreduces in param dtype), optimizer math is fp32 inside fused AdamW.\n  This is the standard \"manual bf16\" recipe; AMP autocast would add overhead\n  without changing the result.\n\n## Profiling on the real machine\n\ntorch.profiler runs in-process on every rank, but only rank0 dumps the chrome\ntrace (others profile and discard). The trace handler writes one\n`<host>_<pid>_<ts>.pt.trace.json` under `<run_dir>/profile/`. With\n`profile_warmup=3, profile_active=10` at the production shape (seq_len=2048,\nmicro_batch=4, ttt=3) a single trace is typically 50–200 MB.\n\nSubmission flow:\n```\n1. SCALE_ENTRY=profile SCALE_CONFIG=h100/profile.yaml SCALE_NPROC=8  # job ~1 min\n2. /output/runs/h100_profile__<sha>__<ts>/profile/\n       ├── <host>_<pid>_<ts>.pt.trace.json   (chrome trace; rank0 only)\n       └── summary.txt                       (top-20 ops by self-CUDA time)\n3. pull /output (platform UI), open trace.json in chrome://tracing or perfetto.dev\n4. summary.txt is < 10 KB; greppable without download\n```\n\n`summary.txt` is the right artifact for \"what kernel dominates\" — the trace\nis for stream-level analysis (\"when does NCCL allreduce overlap with backward?\").\nRead summary first; only open the trace when summary leaves a question open.\n\nIf the trace ends up too big to download:\n* drop `probe.profile_active` to 5 (one cycle, smaller trace)\n* set `record_shapes=False` and `profile_memory=False` (already the default in\n  `scale.cli.profile.main`)\n* compress trace before download: `gzip <trace.json>` typically gets 10x\n\n## Common surprises"
}
```

> AGENT

审计与修复汇总。

**找到并修的 6 个真问题：**

| # | bug | 原值 | 修后 | 影响 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `tokens_seen` 仅 rank0 自己的 | local 计数 | `reduce_sum` 跨 rank | H100×8 throughput log 不再少报 8× |
| 2 | per-step 4 次 `.item()` sync | loss + 3 acc 分别 all_reduce | `reduce_batch` 一次 stack 一次 sync | step time 不再被 4 个 barrier 串行卡 |
| 3 | DDP `bucket_cap_mb` 默认 25 MB | 500 MB 模型 → ~20 bucket | 200 MB → ~3 bucket | NCCL 集合通信次数 ÷7 |
| 4 | `batch_probe` 多 mb 共用 train.jsonl | 步号 1..10, 1..10 互相覆盖 | `attempts/mb_N/` 子目录 | 每次 attempt 日志独立 |
| 5 | `train_loop` 末尾无条件写 `final.pt` | batch_probe 也写 2.5 GB/次 | `save_best` 同时 gate final | probe 不再浪费盘 |
| 6 | 从未真测过多 rank | 0 个多 rank test | 6 个 gloo + 2 个 NCCL test | dist 模块语义全覆盖 |

**多 rank 验证情况：**
- gloo CPU 6 测全过 (`reduce_mean/sum/batch`、`broadcast_object`、`rank0_first`、`no_sync`)
- NCCL 2 测在 1-GPU 机器自动 skip（NCCL 拒绝两 rank pin 同卡），在 H100 ≥ 2 GPU 自动运行（断言 allreduce 后梯度 = 跨 rank 均值 + no_sync 内不通信、出 no_sync 后通信）

**profile 真机回收路径（README 已写）：**
```
1. 提交 profile job (1 min)
2. 落盘 /output/runs/h100_profile__<sha>__<ts>/profile/
       ├── *.pt.trace.json  (50–200 MB，rank0 only)
       └── summary.txt      (<10 KB, top-20 self-CUDA kernels)
3. 平台 UI 拉 /output；summary 先看，trace 按需开
4. 太大时:  active=5 / gzip / 同步流分析改看 summary
```

**显式没做的优化（README 写了理由）：**
- ZeRO-1/FSDP — 250M 参数太小，AdamW state 2 GB 在 80 GB H100 没必要分片
- gradient compression — H100 NVLink/IB 带宽足够
- AMP autocast — 手 bf16 + fp32 AdamW 已是同款配方

最终 33 passed + 2 H100-only skipped。
